# Changelog

All notable changes to this project will be documented in this file.

The format is based on [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.0.0/),
and this project adheres to [Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/spec/v2.0.0.html).

No versioned releases have been cut yet. The app is pre-release; this file will grow as milestones land. Entries are dated; each is one concise line describing a feature, foundation shift, or substantive fix.

## [Unreleased]

- 2026-08-06: Telegram — **new extended module `@monark/telegram`: Telegram trigger events + action nodes for automations** (an _automation integration_, `integrates: "@monark/automation"`; flag-gated `telegram.enabled`, default off). The third integration on `@monark/integration-kit`, and the one that exercises the **trigger** path a different way than GitHub: Telegram authenticates its inbound webhook with a **shared secret echoed in a header** (`X-Telegram-Bot-Api-Secret-Token`), not an HMAC over the body — so the kit gained `constantTimeEquals` and `defineInboundWebhook`'s `verify` callback slots it in unchanged. **Triggers:** connect calls Telegram's `setWebhook`, Telegram POSTs updates to `POST /hooks/telegram/:org` ([server.ts](monark/app/services/api/src/server.ts)), and the handler ([webhook.ts](monark/app/packages/telegram/src/server/webhook.ts)) verifies the header, maps a `message` update, and `emit()`s one of two `telegram.*` events ([events.ts](monark/app/packages/telegram/src/contracts/events.ts)) — `message-received` (plain text) and `command` (a `/command`, parsed to `{ command, args }` with the `@botname` suffix stripped). They join the `DomainEvent` union + register with `{{ trigger.* }}` field metadata ([event-types.ts](monark/app/packages/telegram/src/server/event-types.ts)). **Actions:** five nodes under the `telegram` namespace ([nodes/](monark/app/packages/telegram/src/server/nodes)) — send / edit / delete message, send photo, get chat. **Where it diverged from the kit:** Telegram's Bot API puts the token in the URL _path_ (no `Authorization` header) and wraps every response in a `{ ok, result, description }` envelope, so it doesn't fit `createRestClient` — [client.ts](monark/app/packages/telegram/src/server/client.ts) calls `safeFetch` directly (unwrapping `result`, surfacing `description` on error) while still reusing the kit's `pickString` / `pickNumber` + webhook helpers (the kit is a toolbox, not a frame). **Connection:** one canonical bot token per org (unlike GitHub / Discord's per-node token secret) — a flag-gated `/telegram` settings page ([telegram-settings.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/telegram/telegram-settings.tsx>)) pastes the BotFather token and connects, and a **bespoke** `telegram.connection.{status,connect,disconnect}` router ([router.ts](monark/app/packages/telegram/src/server/router.ts), gated on `telegram.manage`) stores the token, mints the webhook secret, and registers/deregisters the webhook with Telegram in one call (so it doesn't use `makeConnectionSecretRouter`); nodes read the connected token directly (`ctx.getSecret`), no token field. Plus a `PRIMARY_NAV` entry (paper-plane `Send` icon; lucide has no brand Telegram glyph). No new tables (two `@monark/secrets` entries). Tests: 6 unit (`parseCommand` + `mapTelegramUpdate` for every update kind) + 2 integration (connection RBAC deny + connect→status→disconnect round-trip, `telegramCall` mocked). `pnpm gen` + `typecheck` (telegram/api/web) + `lint` + `check:tiers`/`check:modules` (20 modules) / `check:i18n` (2137 keys) / `check:mcp` all green.
- 2026-08-05: Discord — **new extended module `@monark/discord`: Discord action nodes for automations** (an _automation integration_, `integrates: "@monark/automation"`; flag-gated `discord.enabled`, default off). The second integration on `@monark/integration-kit` — and a deliberate contrast to GitHub: **write-only, no triggers.** Discord has no inbound event webhooks (real-time events come over a Gateway WebSocket a bot holds open; the only inbound HTTP is Ed25519-signed slash-command Interactions), so this MVP covers the dominant use case — automations posting to Discord — with **no events, no inbound endpoint, and no tRPC router** (a router-less module; `check:modules` requires no integration suite for it). Six nodes ([nodes/](monark/app/packages/discord/src/server/nodes)): `send-message` (bot token → channel), `send-webhook-message` (a channel webhook URL, no bot), `edit-message`, `add-reaction`, `list-messages`, `get-channel`. Bot calls go through the kit's `createRestClient` — which gained a configurable **auth scheme** (`authHeader`, so Discord's `Bot <token>` works alongside GitHub's default `Bearer`; backward-compatible) — and the webhook-URL send validates the URL to a discord.com host ([isDiscordWebhookUrl](monark/app/packages/discord/src/contracts/discord.ts)) on top of `safeFetch`'s SSRF guard. The credential is a secret referenced per node (bot token or webhook URL), so there's no connection UI. Registered `discord.enabled` + the nodes at api boot; the palette shows them under a `discord` category (chat-bubble icon). 4 unit tests + the kit's existing suite green. This validates the kit: the whole integration is a fraction of GitHub's size, and the only shared-code change was the one auth-scheme generalization. `typecheck` (discord/api/web) + `lint` + `check:tiers`/`check:modules` (19 modules) green.
- 2026-08-05: Automation integrations — **shared `@monark/integration-kit` + a manifest `integrates` convention, factored out of the GitHub module.** As more automation integrations land (Slack/Jira/…), the reusable ~80% shouldn't be copy-pasted, and "this extends automation" belongs in metadata, not the folder tree (the glob-derived tsconfig paths + gen tooling assume flat `packages/*`). Two pieces: (1) a new library `@monark/integration-kit` ([src/server](monark/app/packages/integration-kit/src/server)) with `defineInboundWebhook({ secretKey, verify, map, label })` (resolve the org's signing secret → verify → map → `emit` ; 404/401/202), `verifyHmacSha256`, `makeConnectionSecretRouter` (the `status`/`generateWebhookSecret`/`disconnect` connection surface), and `createRestClient` + `pickString`/`pickNumber`. (2) A manifest field `integrates: "@monark/automation"` ([modules.manifest.ts](monark/app/modules.manifest.ts)) with a new `check:tiers` rule that an integration must actually depend on the module it names (metadata can't go stale), plus `getModuleIntegrates` / `listIntegrations` helpers. `@monark/github` is refactored onto the kit — its webhook handler is now a `defineInboundWebhook` call, its connection router a `makeConnectionSecretRouter` call, and its REST client a `createRestClient` — dropping its bespoke HMAC/secret/fetch code while keeping identical behavior (10 github unit + 2 integration still green; 6 new kit unit tests for `verifyHmacSha256`). The integration pattern is documented in [extensibility-contract.md](monark/app/docs/technical-documentation/extensibility-contract.md). Decision: keep the flat package layout (nesting under `packages/integrations/*` would break the tsconfig path globs for modest gain); express the relationship as enforceable metadata + shared code instead. `typecheck` (kit/github/api) + `lint` + `check:tiers`/`check:modules` green.
- 2026-08-05: GitHub — **new extended module `@monark/github`: GitHub trigger events + read/write action nodes for automations** (flag-gated `github.enabled`, default off). Built on the core `@monark/automation` engine like calendar/kanban (extended→core only; `check:tiers` clean), no new tables — the only per-org state (the inbound webhook signing secret) lives in the `@monark/secrets` substrate. **Triggers:** an inbound `POST /hooks/github/:org` ([server.ts](monark/app/services/api/src/server.ts), raw body captured via `express.json({ verify })`) verifies GitHub's `X-Hub-Signature-256` HMAC against the org's stored secret, maps the delivery, and `emit()`s one of seven `github.*` domain events ([events.ts](monark/app/packages/github/src/contracts/events.ts) + [webhook.ts](monark/app/packages/github/src/server/webhook.ts)) — issue opened, issue/PR commented, PR opened, PR merged, PR review submitted, push, release published. They join the `DomainEvent` union (`pnpm gen:events`) and register with descriptions + `{{ trigger.* }}` field metadata ([event-types.ts](monark/app/packages/github/src/server/event-types.ts)), so the automation subscriber fires matching flows automatically and they show in the trigger + webhook-subscription pickers. **Actions:** 11 nodes registered under the `github` namespace ([nodes/](monark/app/packages/github/src/server/nodes)) — create/get/list issues, comment, add labels, set issue state, create/get PR, request review, list commits, search issues/PRs — each resolving its token from an org secret (`ctx.getSecret`) and calling the REST API through the shared SSRF-guarded `safeFetch` ([client.ts](monark/app/packages/github/src/server/client.ts)), with declared `outputFields` for the variable picker. **Connection UI:** a flag-gated `/github` settings page ([github-settings.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/github/github-settings.tsx>)) to generate/rotate the webhook secret (shown once), copy the webhook URL, and see setup steps, backed by `github.connection.{status,generateWebhookSecret,disconnect}` (gated on a new `github.manage` permission); plus a `PRIMARY_NAV` entry (git-pull-request icon, since lucide dropped its brand `Github` glyph). Wired all `register*` at api boot. Tests: 10 unit (`mapGithubEvent` mapping for every event + `parseRepoRef`) + 2 integration (connection RBAC deny + connect→status→disconnect round-trip). `pnpm gen` + `typecheck` (github/api/web) + `lint` + `check:tiers`/`check:modules`/`check:i18n` (2115 keys) all green. Auth is a token-via-secrets MVP; a GitHub App is a future upgrade that wouldn't touch the nodes.
- 2026-08-05: CI fix — enabling the `develop` gate immediately surfaced two failures the white-label change had introduced but the local run missed. (1) [email.test.ts](monark/app/packages/notifications/tests/email.test.ts) hardcoded the old `Monark <noreply@monark.io>` fallback envelope ; it now asserts `BRANDING.fromEmail` so it stays correct across any rebrand. (2) The `gen:{schema,events,routers} --check` codegen-drift guards did a raw string compare that failed on CI when a Windows-committed **CRLF** artifact met the Linux **LF** re-render ; the comparison is now EOL-insensitive in all three tools ([gen-schema.ts](monark/app/tools/gen-schema.ts), [gen-events.ts](monark/app/tools/gen-events.ts), [gen-routers.ts](monark/app/tools/gen-routers.ts)) since an EOL-only difference is not real drift (normalizing away every `\r`, since a Windows-generated artifact can carry lone CRs the first pass missed). (3) [app-launcher.test.tsx](monark/app/services/web/tests/components/app-launcher.test.tsx) asserted the old literal "Monark apps" / "Monark Core" launcher strings that are now `{appName}` brand tokens ; it pins its own literal message values (the token substitution only runs server-side, not under `renderWithIntl`) so it tests launcher behaviour, not branding. A repo-wide `.gitattributes eol=lf` normalization is the fuller EOL fix and stays a follow-up. Full unit suite + gen checks green.
- 2026-08-05: CI / infra — **the full verify gate now runs on `develop`, not just `main`.** Extended [ci.yml](monark/app/.github/workflows/ci.yml)'s `push` + `pull_request` triggers to `[main, develop]`, so every push and PR on the working branch runs lint/typecheck, repo-checks (codegen drift + tiers + modules + i18n), the migration↔schema drift job (replays migrations into a throwaway Postgres), and the unit + integration suites. Also added the missing **`check:mcp`** step to `repo-checks` so CI now matches the local pre-PR gate exactly (every public-API route must make an explicit MCP-visibility decision). Fixed a stale `/setup` reference in [e2e.yml](monark/app/.github/workflows/e2e.yml)'s bootstrap comment — provisioning is api-boot + the seed step now that the in-app boot page is gone.
- 2026-08-05: Branding / white-label — **the app is now a business-agnostic white-label starter.** `@monark/branding`'s `DEFAULT_BRANDING` flipped to neutral placeholders (appName `App`, a generic tagline, an `example.com` support inbox, a `#2563EB` accent) ; a real deployment layers its identity via the `BRANDING_*` / `NEXT_PUBLIC_BRANDING_*` env vars, now documented in both [services/api/.env.example](monark/app/services/api/.env.example) + [services/web/.env.example](monark/app/services/web/.env.example). Extracted the last hardcoded product copy from the i18n catalogs — the sign-in tagline/subheading interpolate a new `{tagline}` brand token (added to `BRAND_PLACEHOLDERS` in [i18n/request.ts](monark/app/services/web/src/i18n/request.ts)) and the app-launcher labels use `{appName}` instead of literal "Monark" / "Web3". Added a neutral placeholder logo ([logo.svg](monark/app/services/web/public/logo.svg)) and a [white-label guide](monark/app/docs/technical-documentation/white-label.md) ; the branding README + `index.ts` doc realigned to env-first (don't edit the generic defaults). Monark's own identity now lives only in deployment config (env, `render.yaml`, CI) ; `@monark/*` package names and MonarkQL/MQL stay as internal identifiers. `gen` + `typecheck` + `check:i18n` + `check:tiers`/`modules`/`mcp` + branding tests green.
- 2026-08-05: Bootstrap / infra — **removed the in-app `/setup` boot-status page** ([services/web/src/app/setup/](monark/app/services/web/src/app/setup) deleted) and the `isSystemBootstrapped` route-gates in the `(anon)` / `(authed)` layouts ; a first-boot flow, if wanted, belongs in a separate two-step HTML installer, not in the app. Singleton-org provisioning stays operator-driven: env-at-api-boot (`INITIAL_ORG_*`) plus a new **`pnpm provision:org`** CLI ([tools/provision-org.ts](monark/app/tools/provision-org.ts)) that provisions/updates the singleton idempotently from `--slug`/`--name`/`--color` flags or the env, without an API restart. [bootstrap-gate.ts](monark/app/services/web/src/lib/bootstrap-gate.ts) trimmed to just `getBootstrapStatus` (still drives the singleton brand colour on pre-auth surfaces). `typecheck` + `check:i18n` green.
- 2026-08-05: Automation / UI — **adding a node on mobile no longer opens its config sheet immediately.** On mobile the config panel is a full-screen sheet, so auto-selecting a freshly-added node covered the canvas and blocked wiring it up. `addNode` now skips the auto-select on mobile (`useIsMobile`) — the new node lands on the canvas ready to connect, and tapping it opens the config when you're ready ; desktop keeps the quick-select since its side rail doesn't cover the canvas ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>)). `web` typecheck + lint green.
- 2026-08-05: Chat — enabled **Anthropic prompt caching** in the assistant provider to cut input cost on this input-heavy agent (system prompt + 18 tool schemas resent every turn). Three ephemeral `cache_control` breakpoints ([anthropic.ts](monark/app/packages/chat/src/server/llm/anthropic.ts)): the static system prompt, the tool set, and the growing conversation prefix. For the cache to hit, the prefix must be byte-identical across turns, so the per-turn page context now rides the **current user message** instead of being appended to the system prompt (`injectContext` in [agent.ts](monark/app/packages/chat/src/server/agent.ts)). Cache reads bill at ~10% of input after the first turn (5-min TTL). `tsc` + `eslint` + chat tests (3 unit + 12 integration) green.

- 2026-08-04: Chat — the assistant (Chrysa) can now **read and build automations**. Added an **in-app-only** toolset ([automation-tools.ts](monark/app/services/api/src/chat/automation-tools.ts), merged into `buildChatToolset` via a new `InAppTool` seam) that calls `caller.automation.*` in-process as the user — deliberately NOT added to the public API / MCP (automations are an internal power-user surface). Tools: discovery (`automation_list` / `_get` / `_list_node_types` / `_list_trigger_events`), whole-graph `automation_create` / `_update` (pre-validated with the exported `validateGraph` against the live node registry, so the model gets an itemized fix list before anything persists), plus `_enable`, `_test`, `_get_run`. Same per-procedure RBAC (`automation.view/create/manage/run`) + confirm-gate for mutations. `tsc` + `eslint` (api) green.

- 2026-08-04: Chat — the assistant now has a **configurable display name** (Monark ships as **"Chrysa"**). Single source: `CHAT_ASSISTANT_NAME` (default `Chrysa`) read by [config.ts](monark/app/packages/chat/src/server/config.ts), folded into the system prompt ([agent.ts](monark/app/packages/chat/src/server/agent.ts)) and surfaced to the web via a new `chat.config` tRPC query, which the `ChatProvider` reads and threads through the launcher / panel header / empty state (i18n `launcherAria` + `emptyHint` now take a `{name}` param ; the static "Assistant" strings dropped). A per-org (DB) override can layer on this later. `tsc` + `eslint` + `check:i18n` green.

- 2026-08-04: Chat — **token streaming** for the assistant's replies over a dedicated **`POST /chat/stream`** Server-Sent Events endpoint ([server.ts](monark/app/services/api/src/server.ts)), so answers type out live instead of appearing all at once. Not tRPC (the web client is httpBatch-only) — the endpoint authenticates with the same Supabase bearer and calls the shared `sendMessage(ctx, input, hooks)` (extracted from the `chat.messages.send` mutation, now used by both) with an `onTextDelta` hook that emits `token` frames, ending with `done` / `error`. The web thread consumes it via [stream.ts](monark/app/services/web/src/components/chat/stream.ts) (fetch + ReadableStream, so the bearer header works), appending tokens to a live bubble and refetching on completion. `confirm`/`reject` stay non-streamed for now. `tsc` (chat + api + web) + `eslint` green ; chat's 3 unit + 12 integration tests still pass.

- 2026-08-04: Chat — new **`@monark/chat`** core module: a generic conversation substrate (multi-participant, so user↔user chat rides the same tables later) whose first use is an **app-owned AI assistant**. The assistant answers questions and takes actions over the org's data by calling the app's own tRPC procedures **in-process, as the signed-in user** — its toolset is built from the SAME public-API `V1_ROUTES` that drive the MCP server ([tools builder](monark/app/services/api/src/chat/tools.ts) → injected via `setChatToolExecutor`, mirroring `setWebhookSecretResolver`), so every tool inherits the user's RBAC with no HTTP hop or API key. Mutating tools are **confirm-gated** (pause at `PROPOSED` → user confirms/rejects → the re-entrant [agent loop](monark/app/packages/chat/src/server/agent.ts) resumes) ; the LLM is **provider-abstracted** ([llm/](monark/app/packages/chat/src/server/llm) — Anthropic first). Full four-systems wiring (RBAC `chat.{use,manage}`, `chat.{conversation,message}-*` events, flags `chat.enabled` + `chat.ai-agent`, both **off** by default). Web companion in [components/chat](monark/app/services/web/src/components/chat): a docked, non-modal panel (conversation list → thread) launched from the AppBar (Cmd/Ctrl+J), context-aware, with inline tool-confirmation cards. Owns `Conversation` / `ConversationParticipant` / `Message` / `MessageToolCall` (migration `20260804150000_add_chat`). `tsc` + `eslint` + `check:i18n` + `check:modules` + `check:tiers` green ; 15 tests (3 unit + 12 integration) pass.

- 2026-08-04: AppBar — chrome redesign that makes room for the assistant launcher at **zero net icon count**. Merged the brand + hamburger into one mark (the brand mark opens the nav drawer ; "Home" is now the drawer's first item) in [primary-nav-menu.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/primary-nav-menu.tsx) ; moved notifications into a **tabbed avatar menu** (Notifications · Account, unread badge on the avatar) by extracting the inbox into [notifications-list.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/notifications-list.tsx) and deleting the standalone bell ; the freed slot now hosts the `✨` assistant launcher. Right cluster is now assistant · search · avatar. [app-bar.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/app-bar.tsx) + [user-menu.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/user-menu.tsx) updated ; i18n en+fr for the new tabs + Home label. `tsc` + `eslint` + `check:i18n` green.

- 2026-08-04: Automation — the event-trigger picker now hides low-value / noisy events that few would automate on : `notification.created`, `notification.preference-changed`, and the role-definition edits `rbac.role-created` / `-updated` / `-deleted` (the useful `notification.delivery-failed` and per-user `rbac.role-assigned` / `-revoked` stay). This is **picker curation only** via a new `isPickerTriggerEventType` in [subscriber.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/subscriber.ts) (= `isTriggerableEventType` minus a `LOW_VALUE_TRIGGER_EVENTS` set), used by `eventTypes.list` in [router.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/router.ts) ; the run subscriber still gates on `isTriggerableEventType`, so an automation already configured on a hidden event (e.g. via the API) keeps firing. `tsc` + `eslint` + automation unit tests (new `trigger-event-policy` suite) green.

- 2026-08-04: Automation — added a **Data Record** trigger node so a flow can fire on records of one specific Data Model instead of every model. It stores a `dataModelKey` (the existing model picker) + `operation` (created / updated / deleted) ; on save the editor maps the operation to the real `data-models.record-<op>` bus event, so matching still uses the indexed `triggerEventType` column, and the new `eventScopeMatches` filter in [subscriber.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/subscriber.ts) applies the model filter on top — only records in the chosen model enqueue a run, while a generic Event Trigger on a record event still fires for all models (back-compatible). The trigger→event derivation, previously inlined in three spots in [automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>), is now one `triggerEventTypeForNode` helper. New node [data-record-trigger.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/nodes/data-record-trigger.ts) + `DATA_RECORD_TRIGGER_TYPE` / `dataRecordEventType` in [contracts/triggers.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/contracts/triggers.ts) ; the `required` model + operation are enforced by the existing graph validator, and node labels stay English registry strings (no i18n). `tsc` + `eslint` + 52 automation unit tests (incl. a new `eventScopeMatches` suite) green.

- 2026-08-04: Tooling — `pnpm enable:dev-flags` to turn a set of feature flags on for a dev instance. New [tools/enable-dev-flags.ts](monark/app/tools/enable-dev-flags.ts) writes a **global override** (`enabled: true`) for `data-models.query-language`, `public-api.enabled`, and `public-api.service-accounts` — the same thing an operator does at `/admin/feature-flags`, scripted + idempotent. Each flag's registered `defaultOn` stays `false`, so production deploys are unaffected ; the tool only flips the flags on in the DB it points at, and refuses to run with `NODE_ENV=production`. It syncs the flag definitions first (so the override's `FeatureFlag` FK resolves on a fresh DB), then upserts the overrides via `setOverride(key, {}, true, …)`. Added `@monark/data-models` / `@monark/public-api` / `@monark/feature-flags` as workspace deps of the root package so the tool resolves them under `tsx`, and a `enable:dev-flags` package script (mirrors `sysadmin`). tools `tsc` (0) + `eslint` green ; ran against the local dev DB and verified the three global overrides landed.

- 2026-08-04: Automation — the event-trigger picker no longer offers `automation.*` events (`automation.created` / `updated` / `deleted` / `run-started` / `run-succeeded` / `run-failed`). The run-enqueue subscriber ignores those (`startsWith("automation.")`) to avoid feedback loops, so they were dead options : selecting one produced an automation that could never fire. Extracted a shared `isTriggerableEventType(type)` predicate in [subscriber.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/subscriber.ts), used by both the subscriber and the picker's `eventTypes.list` in [router.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/router.ts), so the offered set can't drift from the fireable set. The same predicate also drops `feature-flag.flipped`, which is unroutable as a trigger — its org / user is nested under `scope` (not the top-level `resolveEventOrgIds` reads) and a global flip has no org at all, so it too could never fire. The events stay registered and webhook-subscribable ; only the automation trigger picker hides them. `tsc` + 47 automation unit tests green.

- 2026-08-04: UI — control-height consistency + taller mobile controls. The standard control height is **`h-9` (36px)** (shared `Button` default/icon, `Input`, `SelectTrigger`). (1) **Kanban toolbar** — the board selector ([kanban-shell.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/kanban-shell.tsx>), was `py-1.5` ≈ 32px) and the Views trigger ([kanban/views-menu.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/views-menu.tsx>), was `size="sm"` 32px) now render at `h-9`, matching the search bar (`QueryChipBar` `min-h-9`) so all three toolbar controls line up. (2) **Taller controls on mobile** — text inputs read thin on a phone because the `pointer-coarse` 44px floor only fires on real touch hardware (a devtools / narrow-window mobile view kept the 36px height). Added `max-md:min-h-11` alongside the existing `pointer-coarse:min-h-11` on [`Input`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/ui/input.tsx), [`SelectTrigger`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/ui/select.tsx), and [`Button`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/ui/button.tsx) (+ `min-w-11` for icon buttons), so every control is a comfortable ~44px on the mobile viewport _and_ on touch, while a desktop mouse keeps the compact 36px. Applied the same floor to the custom toolbar controls that don't use the primitives — the kanban board selector, the [`QueryChipBar`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/query/query-chip-bar.tsx) chip list, and the [`QueryBar`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/query/query-bar.tsx) input — so the toolbar stays aligned at both breakpoints. New `control-heights` screenshot story proves the alignment (36px desktop / 44px mobile). web `tsc` (0) + `eslint` + 279 tests green.

- 2026-08-04: Testing / CI — two drift guards + web logic tests. Added `gen:schema --check` ([gen-schema.ts](monark/app/tools/gen-schema.ts)) and wired it into the CI codegen-drift step — this had been a real gap: the header claimed schema.prisma was drift-checked, but only `gen:events` / `gen:routers` were, so a base/fragment edit that skipped `pnpm gen:schema` would slip through. Added a migration↔schema drift gate ([check-migrations.ts](monark/app/tools/check-migrations.ts), `pnpm check:migrations` + a `migration-drift` CI job with a Postgres service) : it replays every committed migration into a shadow DB and diffs the result against schema.prisma, failing on any change beyond a small allowlist of divergences the Prisma datamodel structurally can't express (the DataRecord jsonb GIN index built in raw SQL ; the TrustedDevice `expiresAt` dbgenerated default's cosmetic reformatting). Catches a modeled change shipped without a migration, or a migration hand-edited away from the schema. On the web side, added unit tests for the untested logic-bearing lib utilities — `color-utils` (hex→rgb/hsl/cmyk, all branches), `format-time` (relative-time bucketing), `dev-host-rewrite` (the LAN-dev loopback swap) — and the last untested server-action file, `admin/users/admin-actions.ts` (avatar/banner upload gates + the admin password-reset flow) ([tests/lib](monark/app/services/web/tests/lib), [admin-users-actions.test.ts](monark/app/services/web/tests/actions/admin-users-actions.test.ts)) : those files went to 92–100 %, though the headline web number barely moves since coverage instruments the whole `src` tree (page/component code is e2e's job), so the floor stays at 5 for now. 31 new web tests (279 total) ; lint + typecheck green.
- 2026-08-04: MCP / Public API — **enforced per-route MCP-visibility decisions + auto-generated the MCP tool set from the OpenAPI.** Every public-API route now carries a **required** `mcp: { expose: { name, description } } | { skip: reason }` field on `RouteDescriptor` ([routes.ts](monark/app/packages/public-api/src/server/routes.ts)), so a new route **can't compile without deciding** whether agents see it — and a new `check:mcp` gate ([tools/check-public-api-mcp.ts](monark/app/tools/check-public-api-mcp.ts), added to the pre-PR sequence) validates the decisions (unique, well-formed tool names ; non-trivial descriptions ; non-empty skip reasons). `expose` decisions are published in the OpenAPI as **`x-mcp-tool`** ([openapi.ts](monark/app/packages/public-api/src/server/openapi.ts)). The **MCP server now auto-generates its tools from that spec** rather than hand-maintaining them : on startup it fetches `/api/v1/openapi.json`, and `toolsFromOpenApi` turns each `x-mcp-tool` operation into a tool — name/description from the tag, input schema + a call-plan (path/query/body split) derived from the operation's declared params + body ([services/mcp/src/openapi.ts](monark/app/services/mcp/src/openapi.ts)) ; a low-level `Server` serves `tools/list` + `tools/call`, dispatching via `executeTool` ([server.ts](monark/app/services/mcp/src/server.ts)). Net effect : **agent visibility tracks the API automatically** — expose a route → it becomes an agent tool with zero MCP code, and the decision is enforced at CI. Curated descriptions are preserved (they live on the route's `expose` config). Replaced the curated `tools.ts` ; also fixed a stale public-api unit test that still referenced the removed scope layer. Tests : public-api `x-mcp-tool` emission ; mcp `toolsFromOpenApi` / `executeTool` / `fetchTools` (19 unit) + protocol smoke over in-memory transport ; the live e2e ([mcp-e2e.test.ts](monark/app/services/api/tests/integration/mcp-e2e.test.ts)) now proves the whole loop — the server fetches the real OpenAPI, generates the 9 tools, and drives them through HTTP → API → Postgres. `typecheck` 26/26 + `lint` 25/25 + `check:tiers`/`check:modules`/`check:i18n`/`check:mcp` + api integration 31 + mcp unit 19 green.

- 2026-08-04: Mobile UX — follow-ups from the mobile audit (three tail items). (1) **Styled date filter** — a `date` filter's control was a native OS `<input type="date">`, drifting from the styled calendar the form date _fields_ use ; both the desktop dropdown submenu and the mobile filter modal now render the shared [`DatePicker`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/ui/date-picker.tsx) calendar popover via a new `FilterDatePicker` adapter in [filter-menu.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/filter-menu.tsx) that maps the filter's ISO `yyyy-mm-dd` string ↔ a local `Date` (server contract unchanged). Verified the nested composition is layer-safe (the calendar opens on top without dismissing the parent filter dropdown/popover). (2) **DataTable header touch cues** — the idle "tap to sort" arrow was hover-only (invisible on touch) ; it now stays faintly visible on coarse pointers (`pointer-coarse:opacity-100`), and the 6px column-resize strip — unusable with a finger and easy to mis-tap next to the sort target — is hidden on touch (`pointer-coarse:hidden`; column width is set from the toolbar Columns editor on mobile) ([data-table.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/data-table.tsx)). (3) **Rich-text toolbar** — needed no new code : its buttons already use the shared `Button` (`size="icon"`/`sm`), so the earlier `pointer-coarse` touch-target floor lifts every toolbar control to ~44px on touch (small visual, 44px tap), turning the dense wrapping row into properly-sized targets. The screenshot harness gained a `filter-date` story (nested-popover proof) ; web `tsc` (0 errors) + `eslint` + `check:i18n` + 279 tests green.

- 2026-08-04: Kanban — **responsive top toolbar.** On mobile the three controls (board switcher · query · saved views) were crammed into one row and fought for space ; the board switcher, chip query bar, and views picker are now split across the desktop/mobile layouts the rest of the app uses. Desktop keeps the roomy single row unchanged ; mobile switches to the shared [`ListMobileBar`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/list-mobile-bar.tsx) — the board switcher leads full-width, the query collapses behind a 🔍 that swaps in the full-width chip bar (✕ to collapse), and the saved-views picker sits in the options slot. The three toolbar controls were extracted into shared nodes in [kanban-shell.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/kanban-shell.tsx>) so one instance each feeds both layouts (the board-name span now fills the row on mobile, caps at 45dvw on desktop). Added `kanban.query.bar.expand`/`collapse` i18n (en + fr) and `kanban-toolbar` / `kanban-toolbar-search` screenshot-harness stories. web `tsc` + `eslint` + `check:i18n` (2067 keys) green ; verified mobile (collapsed row + tap-to-expand) and desktop (unchanged) captures.
- 2026-08-04: Automation — the event-trigger node's "When this event fires" picker (and the trigger node's canvas subtitle) now show the dotted event type id (e.g. `automation.created`) instead of the verbose registry description ("An automation flow was created."). These are developer-facing event-bus events, so the concise id is more precise and scannable than a prose sentence. In [automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>): the `event-type` select options use `label: e.type` (was `e.description`), the trigger node face renders the raw `eventType`, and the now-unused `EventLabelContext` + type→description map were removed. `tsc` + `eslint` green.

- 2026-08-04: Mobile UX — a shared-primitive pass so touch ergonomics improve app-wide without changing desktop density. (1) **Touch targets** — added a `pointer-coarse:min-h-11` (and `min-w-11` for icon buttons) floor to the shared [`Button`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/ui/button.tsx) size variants, [`Input`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/ui/input.tsx), and [`SelectTrigger`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/ui/select.tsx). It keys off `@media (pointer: coarse)` (touch/pen), not the viewport, so a mouse keeps today's compact 36px controls while phones/tablets get a ~44px hit area — and because it's a `min-*`, a control with an explicit smaller height (the DataTable row-actions `…` `h-7`, the file-field remove `h-5`) keeps its small _visual_ while the tap area floors at 44px. Applied the same to the raw `<button>` sort/column-row controls in [panels.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/panels.tsx) (not `Button`-based, so no cascade). (2) **Safe-area** — [`DirtyFormBar`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/dirty-form-bar.tsx) now adds `env(safe-area-inset-bottom)` to its bottom padding in both viewport + container modes, so the save row clears the iOS home indicator (0 elsewhere). (3) **Dialog/Sheet** — the shared close button grew from a ~16px glyph to a padded ~36px box (44px on touch) in [dialog.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/ui/dialog.tsx) + [sheet.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/ui/sheet.tsx), and a plain (non-`mobileFullScreen`) dialog now keeps a 1rem gutter + rounded corners on a phone instead of going edge-to-edge. (4) **AutoForm sticky save** — [`FormActionsFooter`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/form-actions-footer.tsx) gained a `stickyOnMobile` option (a floating rounded action bar pinned to the scroll bottom with safe-area, reverting to the inline row at `md+`), adopted by [`AutoForm`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/fields/auto-form.tsx) so Save is reachable without scrolling a long form to its end. The screenshot harness now emulates touch (`hasTouch`/`isMobile`) on the mobile viewport ([capture.mjs](monark/app/services/web/tools/screenshots/capture.mjs)) so `pointer-coarse:` sizing + `hover:`-less touch states render; new `mobile-ux` + `mobile-dialog` stories verify the touch floors, the sticky save bar, and the dialog gutter/close. web `tsc` (0 errors) + `eslint` + 248 tests green.

- 2026-08-04: MCP — **bundling + a live end-to-end test.** (1) `@monark/mcp` now builds to a single self-contained, shebanged `dist/index.js` via esbuild ([build.mjs](monark/app/services/mcp/build.mjs)) — our source bundled, runtime deps external + installed from `dependencies` — wired as the package's `bin` (`monark-mcp`), so it runs as `node dist/index.js` today and `npx @monark/mcp` once the (currently `private`) package is published. (2) A **live end-to-end test** ([mcp-e2e.test.ts](monark/app/services/api/tests/integration/mcp-e2e.test.ts)) that boots the REAL Express API on an ephemeral port against a Postgres testcontainer, seeds an org + admin + `mrk_` key + a Data Model with a record, then drives the MCP server through a real MCP `Client` (SDK in-memory transport) — every call travels MCP protocol → `MonarkClient` → HTTP → `/api/v1` → tRPC caller → Postgres. Covers `whoami` (real principal), `list_records` (reads the seeded row), `create_record` (**verified to actually land in Postgres**), and a real 404 → `isError`. To make it importable, `@monark/mcp` gained `exports` for `./client` / `./server` / `./tools` (the CLI `bin` still points at the bundle) ; `@monark/mcp` + `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk` added as api devDeps. Built bin smoke-tested (clean error + exit 1 when unconfigured) ; api integration suite 31 green (incl. the 4 new e2e), mcp unit suite 17 green, `typecheck` 25/25 + gates clean.

- 2026-08-04: Docs — added [docs/agents/i18n.md](monark/app/docs/agents/i18n.md), an i18n guideline for agents + humans: what to translate (user-facing chrome + content) vs. keep as canonical English registry strings (event / permission / flag / automation-node descriptions), the `check:i18n` parity-gate blind spots (a key missing from _both_ locales renders a raw key path but still passes the gate), the dynamic-key `t.has(...)` fallback + single-source-of-truth rules, and how to add a locale. Linked from [CLAUDE.md](monark/app/CLAUDE.md)'s i18n rule and [docs/README.md](monark/app/docs/README.md). Prompted by an audit of the automation module confirming its technical surface is already registry-owned English (node labels via `desc.label`, trigger events via the event-type registry, rendered directly, never through `t()`) ; tightened the `AutomationNodeDescriptor.label` doc comment ([nodes.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/contracts/nodes.ts)) so nobody adds per-node i18n keys later. No code behavior change.

- 2026-08-04: Testing / CI — hardened the coverage + supply-chain gates. Added a [codecov.yml](monark/app/codecov.yml) with a **patch-coverage target** (new/changed lines must clear ~75 %, `only_pulls`) — the diff-level signal the per-package floor map in [merge-coverage.ts](monark/app/tools/merge-coverage.ts) structurally can't give ; `project` stays informational since the floors already gate aggregate coverage, and an `ignore:` list mirrors the vitest instrumentation exclusions. Fixed the stale Codecov upload list in [ci.yml](monark/app/.github/workflows/ci.yml) — it hardcoded 10 packages and had silently dropped 8 tested ones (api-keys, automation, calendar, data-models, files, kanban, secrets, query) from the dashboard ; it now globs the merged per-package `lcov.info` after the merge step, so the set stays in lockstep with the package list. Supply chain : grouped Dependabot minor/patch bumps into a low-noise weekly cadence ([dependabot.yml](monark/app/.github/dependabot.yml)) and added a [dependency-review](monark/app/.github/workflows/dependency-review.yml) PR gate that blocks a dependency introducing a high/critical CVE or bad license (complements CodeQL + gitleaks, which cover first-party code and secrets, not third-party deps). To _block_ on patch coverage, add the `codecov/patch` status to branch protection ; dependency-review needs the repo's Dependency Graph enabled.
- 2026-08-04: MCP — **`@monark/mcp`, a Model Context Protocol server** that exposes Monark to AI agents (Claude Desktop, Cursor, …) as native tools ([services/mcp](monark/app/services/mcp)). It's a **thin stdio client of the public REST API** : each of its 9 tools (`monark_whoami`, `monark_list_models`, `monark_get_model`, `monark_list_model_fields`, `monark_list_records`, `monark_get_record`, `monark_create_record`, `monark_update_record`, `monark_delete_record`) is one authenticated call to a `/api/v1` endpoint, so **all auth, RBAC, the per-key permission ceiling, service-account rules, and rate limits are enforced server-side exactly once** — the MCP layer holds no authorization logic of its own and works against any Monark deployment. Config is two env vars the agent host passes (`MONARK_API_URL`, `MONARK_API_KEY` — an `mrk_` key) ; the server acts as that key, so a limited/service-account key scopes what the agent can do. Structure : `config` (validate env) → `client` (`MonarkClient` over `/api/v1` + `MonarkApiError`) → `tools` (agent-facing descriptions + zod input) → `server` (SDK `registerTool` wiring, JSON result / `isError` on failure) → `index` (stdio entrypoint ; diagnostics to stderr, never stdout). 17 tests : the client (request building, query omission, error mapping, 204), the tool→REST mapping, config validation, and a **protocol-level smoke test** driving a real MCP `Client` over the SDK's in-memory transport (lists the 9 tools, calls one, maps an API error to `isError`, rejects bad input before the client). `typecheck` (25/25) + `eslint` + all gates green ; CLI errors cleanly when unconfigured. This is the third leg of the Public API / MCP / BYOA plan, and it leans entirely on the REST facade + key model already shipped. Follow-ups (noted in the [README](monark/app/services/mcp/README.md)) : a bundled `npx @monark/mcp` binary, MCP resources/prompts, and a remote HTTP/SSE transport.

- 2026-08-03: Public API — **operationalized the (dark-flagged) API so it's discoverable + adoptable.** Three things: (1) a developer-facing **user guide** ([docs/user-guide/public-api.md](monark/app/docs/user-guide/public-api.md), linked from the guide index) — getting a key (full vs limited), authenticating, the `/api/v1` endpoints with curl examples, the OpenAPI spec for tools/agents, rate limits, errors, and service accounts for shared integrations. (2) **In-app guidance** on Account → API keys ([api-keys-section.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/account/api-keys-section.tsx>)): a "Using your keys" block showing the deployment's base URL, the OpenAPI URL, and a copy-ready `curl` example (base URL from `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL`), plus a **disabled-state notice** — if `public-api.enabled` is off, a warning tells the user their key won't work until an admin turns it on, so a fresh key never mysteriously 404s. (3) **Configurable rate limits** — the per-key token bucket now reads `PUBLIC_API_RATE_PER_SECOND` / `PUBLIC_API_BURST` from env ([env.ts](monark/app/services/api/src/lib/env.ts), [rate-limit.ts](monark/app/services/api/src/public/rate-limit.ts)) instead of hardcoded 5/20 (same defaults). The `public-api.enabled` flag stays **off by default** — enabling a public surface remains a deliberate operator action, now well-signposted. new `account.apiKeys.apiDisabled` + `account.apiKeys.usage.*` i18n (en + fr). typecheck (api + web) + eslint + `check:i18n` (2065 keys) + `check:tiers`/`check:modules` + public-api 27 integration tests green.
- 2026-08-03: Automation / UI — **tap-to-open variable picker (replaces the `{{`-triggered inline menu for discoverability + mobile).** Picking a variable was gated behind typing `{{`, which was invisible in variable mode and — on mobile — put suggestions under the keyboard while the inspector's Variables panel scrolled off-screen, so authors couldn't tell what was available. New [`VariablePicker`](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/variable-picker.tsx>): a searchable, two-level list — **sources** (Trigger / each connected step by name / Workflow variables) that drill into their **variables** — rendered as a popover under the field on desktop and a **bottom sheet** on mobile (always visible, no keyboard fight), built on `Command` + `Popover`/`Sheet`. A **choice field toggled to variable** (`select` / `user` / `number` / …) now becomes a "Choose a variable…" trigger showing the current value as a chip instead of a bare text box ; **text/body fields** keep the chip input and gain a `{ }` button that opens the same picker (inserting at the caret via the field's handle) plus the existing `{{` autocomplete as a fast path. An empty state explains _why_ nothing's available (step not connected), and a search "use {ref} verbatim" row preserves deep-path references the declared outputs don't enumerate. Threaded `suggestions` (already computed) into the picker through `ConfigField` ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>)) ; new `chooseVariable` / `variablePickerTitle` / `variableSearch` / `noVariablesAvailable` / `noVariablesMatch` / `useCustomVariable` i18n (en + fr). `web` typecheck + lint + `check:i18n` green ; user guide updated.
- 2026-08-03: Kanban — **saved views (MonarkQL), parity with data-models.** A named filter tree per board — personal by default or `shared` to everyone with board access, editable only by its owner. New `KanbanView` table (kanban fragment, migration [20260803140000_add_kanban_views](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/migrations/20260803140000_add_kanban_views/migration.sql)) storing the `FilterNode` JSON (validated with `filterQuerySchema`). tRPC `kanban.views.{list, create, update, delete}` ([index.ts](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/server/index.ts)) — reading gates on `kanban.view` + board access, editing is owner-scoped (`requireOwnedKanbanView` → 403 for a non-owner even on a shared view); list returns a `mine` flag. Data layer `listKanbanViews` (mine ∪ shared) + CRUD ([data.ts](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/server/data.ts)). Web: a **Views** menu in the board toolbar ([views-menu.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/views-menu.tsx>)) to load / save (name + share toggle) / delete your own, wired behind the `kanban.query` flag; loading a view prints its tree back into the board query bar. 6 router integration tests (RBAC deny, personal-invisible-to-others, share-then-visible, non-owner-can't-edit/delete, owner rename+delete) — kanban **34 integration** green; new `kanban.query.views.*` i18n (en + fr).
- 2026-08-03: Query — **richer MQL autocomplete + `@monark/query` gets its own tests.** New pure `getCompletions(text, caret, fields)` engine ([query-complete.ts](monark/app/packages/query/src/contracts/query-complete.ts)) — caret-aware suggestions for fields, **operators** (`>`, `~`, `&`, `a..b`, `-field:` none-of — each with a plain-language meaning), values, `@variables`, and boolean keywords, each with a precise replace range (so mid-string editing works). The shared `QueryBar` ([query-bar.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/query/query-bar.tsx)) now uses it and adds **keyboard navigation** (↑/↓ move, Enter/Tab accept, Esc close, mouse-hover sync) with combobox/listbox ARIA. Operator/variable hints are **localized** via optional `describeOp`/`describeVariable` resolvers the engine calls (a shared `query.op.*` / `query.var.*` en+fr namespace + `useMqlLabels()` hook, wired into the records list, kanban board bar, and dev playground; English fallback when omitted). `@monark/query` — which had no tests of its own — now has **48** (completion engine + DSL round-trips + variables + taxonomy); fixed its coverage config (its logic lives under `src/contracts`, which the shared base excluded — so it had read 0 %) and added a floor to [merge-coverage.ts](monark/app/tools/merge-coverage.ts), and trimmed the now-redundant generic query unit tests from data-models (kept the `DataFieldType→kind` bridge test). typecheck + lint + check:i18n (2065 keys) / tiers / modules green.
- 2026-08-03: Query UI — **chip / token query editor (MonarkQL).** A tree-backed editor that renders each predicate as a removable **chip** whose value resolves to a human label — `Assignee: Ada Lovelace, Grace Hopper`, `Status: Active`, `Priority ≥ High` — instead of the raw ids the text bar showed (`assignee:<userId>`, `status:<columnId>`), and where the operator is **picked from a menu** ("is any of", "at least", …) so the hard-to-type comparison symbols (`>= <=`) are never typed. New shared [query-chip-bar.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/query/query-chip-bar.tsx) — a **drop-in for `QueryBar`** (identical props ; it reads the same controlled `text` the parent owns, so saved views + deep links keep working). It chips a flat AND of leaves (the common shape) via the public `parseQuery`/`printQuery` round-trip ; an OR / nested / negated-text query it can't represent falls back to the embedded text bar, also reachable any time via the "edit as text" toggle. Value → label resolution dispatches by `FilterableKind` : fixed-set fields (select / multiSelect / orderedSelect) resolve id→name synchronously through `QueryFieldMeta.options`, `@variables` show their token, dates format, everything else is literal (relation async chips are a noted follow-up — relations aren't in the query language yet). The add/edit popover drives its value control off the operator's arity + the field kind (single-select / `MultiSelect` / `DatePicker` / number-or-text input), and offers `@me` for user-valued fields ; operator + chip strings come from the shared `query.*` i18n namespace (added `query.chip.*`, en + fr). Wired into both consumers by swapping the component — kanban [kanban-shell.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/kanban-shell.tsx>) (fully fixes the assignee-id complaint : assignees resolve to names) and data-models [records-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/models/[modelKey]/records-list.tsx>). Added a `query-chip-bar` screenshot-harness story for regression. web `tsc` + `eslint` + `check:i18n` (2038 keys) green ; verified end-to-end by driving the real component (add Assignee → pick member → chip reads "Assignee: Alan Turing" ; priority operator menu offers "at least" ; chip removal) with no page errors.
- 2026-08-03: Public API — **per-key permission ceiling for personal keys ("abstract sub-roles").** A personal API key can now be capped to a subset of the owner's _own_ permissions, picked from the RBAC catalog filtered to what they hold — not a new taxonomy, just RBAC narrowed down. New `ApiKey.fullAccess Boolean @default(true)` + `permissions String[]` (migration `20260803130000_add_api_key_permission_ceiling`) : `fullAccess=true` (default, backward-compatible) acts with the owner's full authority ; `fullAccess=false` caps the key to `permissions` (empty ⇒ deny-all, fails closed). It is only ever a **ceiling** — every request still runs the live RBAC floor against the owner via the caller, so a key can never exceed the owner's _current_ grants and **loses a permission automatically the moment the owner does** (a dead allowlist entry is surfaced in the UI, never a silent grant). Enforced at the facade against each route's declared `permission` ([mount.ts](monark/app/services/api/src/public/mount.ts)) so it **caps even an admin owner** ; the internal helper reads a route needs (e.g. resolving a model key→id) are checked against the owner, so a `record-read` key isn't broken by the schema read it implies. Picker reuses the shared `GroupedMultiSelect` fed by a new non-admin `rbac.myGrantablePermissions` query (the catalog filtered to `hasPermission` — admins see everything, members see only theirs) ([index.ts](monark/app/packages/rbac/src/server/index.ts)) ; the personal-key create dialog gains a Full-access / Limited toggle ([api-keys-section.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/account/api-keys-section.tsx>)). `create` validates each selected permission is one the owner holds. Service-account keys are unaffected (`fullAccess=true` = the account's roles). `authenticateApiKey` + `RoutePrincipal` carry the ceiling. New tests : a limited key reads records but 403s on write _despite an admin owner_ + `/me` still works ([public-api.test.ts](monark/app/services/api/tests/integration/public-api.test.ts)), and the router stores/authenticates the ceiling + rejects an empty limited key ([service-accounts.test.ts](monark/app/packages/api-keys/tests/integration/service-accounts.test.ts)) — api-keys 21 + public-api 27 integration green ; typecheck (24/24) + `check:tiers`/`check:modules`/`check:i18n` clean ; migration applied. (Consistent with, not a reversal of, the earlier scopes removal : that deleted a coarse _parallel_ enum ; this stores real RBAC permission keys as a strict subset of the owner's, checked by the same RBAC.)
- 2026-08-03: i18n — filled in notification / RBAC labels that were referenced in code but absent from both locales (so `check:i18n`'s en/fr parity gate never caught them ; they rendered as raw key paths at runtime). The RBAC role editor labels each permission-category header via `t(\`categories.${category}\`)`([role-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/rbac/role-editor.tsx>)) ; six categories registered by newer modules had no label — added`api-keys`, `secrets`, `kanban`, `files`, `data-models`, `automation`under`admin.rbac.editor.categories`(en + fr). The notification preferences UI was missing the two email-capable kinds`kanban.card.assigned`and`automation.custom-message`from`prefs.kinds`+`prefs.kindDescriptions`— added both. Also de-duplicated the kind-label source: the dev-overlay notifications panel ([notifications.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/dev-overlay/panels/notifications.tsx)) read a separate, hand-maintained nested`account.notifications.kinds`map (its only consumer) that had drifted behind every kind added since launch ; it now reuses the flat`account.notifications.prefs.kinds` labels via a dot→underscore key, and the dead nested map was removed from both locales. Also made the role editor resilient to categories that have no static label: data-models registers **dynamic** per-model permission categories (`Data Model: <name>`) that can't have i18n keys, so the header now falls back to the raw category string (`t.has(...) ? t(...) : cat.category`) — which for those is already a human-readable label ("Data Model: Customers") — instead of rendering a missing-key path ; this also future-proofs any module category whose label hasn't been added yet. `tsc`+`eslint`+`check:i18n` + 248 web tests green.

- 2026-08-03: Dev overlay — six new panels covering the recently-added event-driven / data modules. (1) **Event bus** — a live feed of emitted domain events (the backbone the automation / webhook / notification side-effects hang off, otherwise invisible). Backed by a dev-only in-memory ring buffer in [@monark/common](monark/app/packages/common/src/dev-event-log.ts) (`installDevEventTap` registers a wildcard bus subscriber at API boot behind a `NODE_ENV !== "production"` guard in [server.ts](monark/app/services/api/src/server.ts)), read via a new dev-gated `automation.dev.recentEvents` procedure ([panel](monark/app/services/web/src/components/dev-overlay/panels/event-bus.tsx), polled every 2s). (2) **Automation runs** — recent runs (trigger event · status · when · error) + one-tap re-fire, reusing `automation.runs.list` + `automation.automations.runNow` ([panel](monark/app/services/web/src/components/dev-overlay/panels/automation-runs.tsx)). (3) **Webhook deliveries** — endpoint picker + recent deliveries (status · attempts · last error) + redeliver, reusing `webhooks.list` / `listDeliveries` / `retryDelivery` ([panel](monark/app/services/web/src/components/dev-overlay/panels/webhook-deliveries.tsx)). (4) **Query playground** — pick a Data Model, type MonarkQL, see the parsed `FilterNode` tree + live row count (client-side parse via the shared QueryBar + `dataModels.records.list`, no new server surface) ([panel](monark/app/services/web/src/components/dev-overlay/panels/query-playground.tsx)). (5) **Secrets** — the org's configured secret _keys_ + metadata (values never shown), reusing `secrets.adminList` ([panel](monark/app/services/web/src/components/dev-overlay/panels/secrets-keys.tsx)). (6) **RBAC** — the existing panel's (long) permission list gained a filter + scroll ([rbac.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/dev-overlay/panels/rbac.tsx)); a full arbitrary-role "act as" override was intentionally deferred — permissions are read via scattered `rbac.myPermissions` calls with no shared hook, so a robust override needs a consumer refactor first (the sysadmin toggle already covers the common case). All panels 404 / no-op outside dev and degrade quietly without the relevant access. New `devOverlay.*` i18n (en + fr). No codegen (the automation `dev` sub-router nests inside the existing `automationRouter`). web + `@monark/common` + `@monark/automation` + api `tsc` (0 errors) + `eslint` + `check:i18n` (1974 keys) green.

- 2026-08-03: Testing / CI — closed the kanban branch gap flagged in the previous entry. Added a pure-unit suite for the Kanban MonarkQL → Prisma-`where` compiler ([query-compiler.test.ts](monark/app/packages/kanban/tests/query-compiler.test.ts)) covering every field × operator × error branch of `compileKanbanFilter` directly (no DB) — the string/date/number/priority/array operator tables, the ordered-priority range expansion, presence, `@me`/variable resolution, the value-coercion guards, and boolean composition. `query-compiler.ts` branches 55 %→100 %, so kanban branches 65 %→97 % (functions 69 %→79 %) ; floor 70/70/65/60 → 80/80/70/90.

- 2026-08-03: Public API — **dropped the scope layer ; authority is now 100% RBAC.** The API-key `scopes` allow-list was a parallel taxonomy bolted on top of RBAC — redundant for our use case (OAuth-style third-party delegation, where scopes earn their keep, is deferred). Removed `API_SCOPES` / `ApiScope` / `assertScope` / the `x-required-scope` OpenAPI field / the `ApiKey.scopes` column (migration `20260803120000_drop_api_key_scopes`) / the scope pickers in both key-create dialogs. A key now acts as its **principal** and inherits that principal's roles, full stop : a **user key** = the creator's rights ("a headless me") ; a **service-account key** = the machine principal's admin-assigned roles. **Least-privilege is done by choosing the principal** — need a read-only key, create a service account with a read-only role — not by ticking scopes. The facade drops `assertScope` (the per-procedure `requirePermission` via the caller was always the real gate) and advertises each route's required RBAC permission as `x-required-permission`. Also removed the obsolete `projects:read` scope : projects are polymorphic Data Model records, so there is never a separate projects endpoint. `authenticateApiKey` now returns `{ apiKeyId, userId, organizationId }` (no scopes). The v1 account key-create dialog and the v2 service-account key-mint dialog are now just name + expiry, each with a note explaining what the key can do ([api-keys-section.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/account/api-keys-section.tsx>), [service-account-panel.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/service-accounts/service-account-panel.tsx>)). Tests updated to match — the facade's least-privilege case is now the more honest **read-only service account → 403 on write** ([public-api.test.ts](monark/app/services/api/tests/integration/public-api.test.ts)), and a new **disabled-owner → 401** case covers the owner-alive check. Governance unchanged and reaffirmed : `api-keys.manage` (personal) and `api-keys.manage-service-accounts` (org) stay distinct, default-off — minting a long-lived credential is a governance decision, not an escalation (a key never exceeds its principal). Design note appended to [public-api.md](monark/app/docs/features-planning/phase-3/public-api.md). api-keys 19 + public-api 25 integration tests green ; typecheck (all packages bar unrelated in-flight dev-overlay work) + `check:tiers`/`check:modules` + eslint clean.
- 2026-08-03: Testing / CI — recovered the two floors the previous entry had tracked down, and then some. api-keys: new tests for the personal-key router (`list`/`create`/`revoke`, covering `listApiKeysForOwner`/`findApiKeyForOwner`) and the service-account-key `create`/`revoke` branches (expiry, disabled-account, revoke idempotency + 404), plus a registration test ([service-accounts.test.ts](monark/app/packages/api-keys/tests/integration/service-accounts.test.ts), [registrations.test.ts](monark/app/packages/api-keys/tests/registrations.test.ts)) — branches 61 %→72 %, functions 80 %→93 %, floor 70/70/75/55 → 80/80/85/65. automation: the boot registrations (event types / `automation.enabled` flag / custom-message notification kind) and the Assign/Remove-Role node executors incl. the owner-permission re-check ([registrations.test.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/tests/registrations.test.ts), [nodes-rbac.test.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/tests/integration/nodes-rbac.test.ts)) — functions 68 %→81 %, floor 70/70/65/70 → 75/75/75/70. One new drop surfaced and was floored honestly : `kanban` branches 88 %→65 % from in-flight board work (functions rose 61 %→69 %), floor branches 80→60, flagged for a later pass. Full run green : every measured package clears its floor.

- 2026-08-03: Dev overlay — fits narrow screens + a movable anchor. The panel ([dev-overlay.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/dev-overlay/dev-overlay.tsx)) was a fixed `w-96` (24rem) block that overflowed a phone viewport; it now carries `max-w-[calc(100vw-2rem)]`, clamping to the screen minus its 1rem gutter on each side. Added a corner picker ([anchor-toggle.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/dev-overlay/anchor-toggle.tsx)) in the overlay header — a small dropdown that pins the toggle button + panel to any of the four viewport corners (top-left / top-right / bottom-left / bottom-right), persisted per-browser in `localStorage` (default bottom-right, loaded after mount so there's no hydration mismatch; the menu is `modal={false}` to avoid a stuck body pointer-events lock). New `devOverlay.anchor.*` i18n (en + fr). `tsc` + `eslint` + `check:i18n` green.

- 2026-08-03: List screens — **simplified the crowded mobile toolbar app-wide.** On a phone a list used to stack ~6 controls above the rows (search/query, filters, sort, follow, columns gear, create). It now collapses to at most three, plus a floating action button, while desktop stays byte-for-byte the same. New shared pieces in [components/patterns](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns): **`ListMobileBar`** (`md:hidden` consolidated bar — a `lead` control, an optional 🔍-expandable search, and one ⋯ options trigger), **`CreateFab`** (mobile-only thumb-reachable create button, pinned bottom-right, rendered only when no detail panel/bulk bar occupies the screen), plus **`TableTools`** gaining a `mode="auto"|"inline"|"sheet"` (decoupled from the viewport so both forms mount behind CSS wrappers) and an `extraSections` slot that appends a "Follow this list" row into the mobile options sheet. [`FilterBar`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/filter-bar.tsx) gained an **opt-in** `mobileOptions` prop: when set, its roomy row goes `hidden md:flex` and a `ListMobileBar` (search leads, `mobileOptions` is the ⋯ sheet) takes over on mobile — omitting it keeps the old behaviour, so the change is additive. Wired the data-model record list ([records-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/models/[modelKey]/records-list.tsx>)) as a bespoke **Views-first** bar (the saved-[`ViewsMenu`](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/models/[modelKey]/views-menu.tsx>) picker leads via a new `asLead` full-width trigger that names the active view, the MonarkQL query field is one 🔍 tap away, sort+columns+follow live in the ⋯ sheet, create is the FAB), and the admin lists (organizations, users, rbac roles, webhooks, data models, secrets) via the `FilterBar mobileOptions` path (service-accounts left as-is — search + create only, already uncluttered). New i18n `table.collapseSearch` + `data.records.options.{notifications,follow}` (en + fr). Validated the real components in the screenshot harness (`mobile-list-toolbar` + `list-options-sheet` stories). Web `tsc` (0 errors) + `eslint` + `check:i18n` (1939 keys, en/fr parity) green.

- 2026-08-03: Automation / UI — **variable chip input follow-ups: chip-on-close + a menu that shows right under the field, incl. mobile.** (1) A manually-typed `{{ … }}` now becomes a chip **the instant you type the closing `}}`** (a `chipifyCompletedToken` on input), instead of only on the next external rebuild (deselect/reselect the node). (2) The autocomplete menu now renders **in normal flow directly under the field** (instead of an absolute/positioned overlay that a scrolling ancestor — the inspector panel or the mobile full-screen sheet — clipped, and that a first pass mis-placed toward the viewport bottom); it can't clip or drift, and behaves identically on desktop and mobile with no positioning math. Menu rows also switched `onMouseDown`→`onPointerDown` (preventDefault) so a **touch** tap selects the row instead of blurring the field first. All in the shared [`VariableInput`](monark/app/packages/components/src/ui/variable-input.tsx); the chipify + accept DOM ops verified headless (18 cases). `@monark/components` + `web` typecheck + lint green.
- 2026-08-03: Kanban — **board filter bar (MonarkQL), Phase 3.** The board toolbar ([kanban-shell.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/kanban-shell.tsx>)) now hosts the shared [`QueryBar`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/query/query-bar.tsx) when the `kanban.query` flag is on : type `assignee:@me priority:>=HIGH due:@startOfWeek..@endOfWeek` and the board re-loads through `cards.list` with the compiled filter (else it shows the full `boards.get` set). Field metadata is built client-side from the board's columns (→ `status` options), org members (→ `assignee`/`reviewer`), and the static priority order, with i18n labels ; a card create/edit/move invalidates both `boards.get` and `cards.list`. The flag is threaded from [page.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/page.tsx>). New `kanban.query.*` i18n (bar + field labels, en + fr). This completes the first pass (Phases 1–3) of leveraging MonarkQL in kanban ; saved views + a backlog list view remain as documented fast-follows. Full pre-PR gate green (gen, typecheck, lint, unit tests, check:tiers/modules/i18n) + data-models (100) / kanban (28) integration.
- 2026-08-03: Kanban — **server-side card filtering (MonarkQL), Phase 2.** New `cards.list` procedure ([index.ts](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/server/index.ts)) takes an optional filter tree and returns a board's cards AND-ed with it, guarded by `kanban.view` + board access (cards inherit board access). The compiler ([query-compiler.ts](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/server/query-compiler.ts)) folds the tree via `@monark/query`'s `walkFilter` into a typed `Prisma.KanbanCardWhereInput` (no raw SQL — kanban cards are real columns), resolving `@variables` (`@me`/`@today`/…) at compile time. Field taxonomy ([contracts/query-fields.ts](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/contracts/query-fields.ts)) : `title`/`description` (text), `status`→`columnId` (select), `assignee`/`reviewer`→id arrays (multi-select), `priority` (**orderedSelect** — `priority:>=HIGH` expands to the value set), `due` (date), `estimate` (number), `created`/`updated`. New `kanban.query` feature flag (default off). 9 integration specs (every operator family, `@me`, ordered-priority expansion, presence, AND/OR, unknown-variable rejection) — full kanban suite (28) green; kanban tsc/lint, api tsc, check:tiers green. README updated. (Phase 3 wires the shared QueryBar into the board.)
- 2026-08-03: Query — extracted the structured filter language ("MonarkQL") into a shared **`@monark/query`** library (Phase 1 of leveraging it beyond data-models). The generic AST + operator/`FilterableKind` taxonomy + text DSL (`parseQuery`/`printQuery`) + dynamic `@variables` + a new pure `walkFilter` visitor now live in [packages/query](monark/app/packages/query/src/contracts) (a plain lib, peer of `@monark/common` — not a manifest module). `@monark/data-models` depends on it and keeps only the `DataFieldType`→`FilterableKind` bridge (`filterableKindOf` + `fieldKindsFromDataFields`), re-exporting the generic API so existing `@monark/data-models/contracts` imports resolve unchanged. Added an **`orderedSelect`** kind (a fixed set with a meaningful order, e.g. a priority enum) supporting `>=`/`<=` — a compiler resolves the range by value-set expansion. Generalized the web `QueryFieldMeta` to carry a resolved `kind` (no `DataFieldType` on the wire) and **promoted `QueryBar` to a shared component** ([services/web/src/components/query/query-bar.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/query/query-bar.tsx)); the data-models `records.queryFields` procedure now emits `kind`, and `records-list`/`views-menu` were rewired accordingly. No behaviour change to the data-models record filter. `pnpm gen` (no drift), tsc (query + data-models + web + api), eslint, check:tiers/modules/i18n all green; data-models 78 unit + 100 integration tests pass unchanged.
- 2026-08-03: Automation / UI — **inline variable autocomplete.** Typing `{{` in a config field now opens a suggestion menu right under it (grouped by source — the trigger, each connected upstream step, workflow variables), filters as you type, navigates with ↑/↓, and inserts the chosen variable as a chip on Enter or click — so authors pick a connected node and its value without leaving the field or reaching for the side panel. Built into the shared [`VariableInput`](monark/app/packages/components/src/ui/variable-input.tsx) via a new `suggestions` prop; the menu only offers what's in scope at that step (the same ancestor-scoped set the picker uses). New exported pure helpers `activeQueryAt` (the unclosed `{{ …` the caret sits in) + `filterSuggestions`, whose logic — query detection (closed-token / multi-open / leading-space / empty) plus the accept-replaces-draft-with-chip DOM op — is verified headless (13 cases). The automation editor flattens its `variableGroups` into suggestions and threads them through `ConfigPanel` → `ConfigField` → every text / textarea / variable-mode field ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>)). `@monark/components` + `web` typecheck + lint green; component README + the automation user guide updated. (Known limitation: the menu is absolutely positioned, so near the very bottom of the inspector's scroll area it can clip — a portal is the follow-up if that bites.)
- 2026-08-03: Testing / CI — coverage floors moved back up now that the full pipeline runs clean again. Made `services/api`'s new `public-api.test.ts` hermetic: it assigned the migration-seeded built-in ADMIN role (`role_admin_builtin`) without seeding it, so under the full serialized run (where a sibling suite truncates the shared Role table) it flaked with `Role not found` depending on file order ; it now upserts the built-in ADMIN row in `beforeAll`, mirroring rbac's `ensureBuiltInRole` pattern ([public-api.test.ts](monark/app/services/api/tests/integration/public-api.test.ts)). With that green, ratcheted `service/api` 55/55/35/50 → 70/70/70/55 (the public-api suite lifted it 57 %→75 % lines / 41 %→79 % functions) in [tools/merge-coverage.ts](monark/app/tools/merge-coverage.ts). Two floors tracked DOWN to honest current numbers — `api-keys` branches 78 %→61 % and `automation` functions 76 %→68 % — where in-flight feature work (public-api service accounts, schedule triggers) outran its tests ; the drops are commented at the floor so the gap stays visible. Full run: 17 packages merged, every one clears its floor.
- 2026-08-03: Calendar — added an Agenda view : a 4th `CalendarViewType` (day/week/month/agenda) showing a forward-chronological, day-grouped list of upcoming events with a "Load more" footer. New `listEventsPaginated`/`calendarRouter.events.listAgenda` ([data.ts](monark/app/packages/calendar/src/server/data.ts)) is genuinely cursor-paginated (`PaginationArgs`/`Paginated<T>`, `cursorFindArgs`/`toPage` from `@monark/common`) per the repo's "list methods paginate" convention — unlike day/week/month's already-accepted date-range-bounded exception, an "keep scrolling" agenda is exactly the access pattern that convention exists for. New [agenda-view.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/calendar/agenda-view.tsx>) consumes it via `useInfiniteQuery`, mirroring the existing pattern in `notifications-bell.tsx` (a manual "Load more" button, not scroll-triggered auto-load — matches the one other infinite-list surface already in this codebase). `defaultView` in `CalendarViewSettings` now accepts `"agenda"` too. Wired into [calendar-shell.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/calendar/calendar-shell.tsx>)'s view switcher (keyboard shortcut `A`) ; mobile still collapses it to Day view, same as week/month, since there's no way to switch into it from the mobile toolbar anyway. 2 new integration tests for the pagination boundary + the `from` cutoff.
- 2026-08-03: Calendar — added ICS export/import. `calendarRouter.events.exportIcs`/`importIcs` ([server/index.ts](monark/app/packages/calendar/src/server/index.ts)) generate/parse via the new `ics` (export) and `node-ical` (import) dependencies, both wired through a new [server/ics.ts](monark/app/packages/calendar/src/server/ics.ts). Export streams a real file download via a new route handler ([export/route.ts](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/calendar/export/route.ts>)) reusing the server-side-tRPC-client pattern from `auth/confirm/route.ts`, rather than a fetch+blob dance. Import reads the chosen `.ics` file client-side as text (no upload/storage round-trip needed for a small text file) and creates events server-side, capped at 500/import ; any `VEVENT` carrying an RRULE imports as a flattened one-time event with a surfaced count, since recurring-event support lands in a later phase. Both actions live in each calendar's `···` menu in the sidebar ([calendar-sidebar.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/calendar/calendar-sidebar.tsx>)). 8 new unit tests including a full generate→parse round-trip ([ics.test.ts](monark/app/packages/calendar/tests/ics.test.ts)).
- 2026-08-03: Calendar — added drag-to-create for multi-day/all-day event spans, in both week view (dragging the pending-creation block across day columns, extending `PendingSlot` with a `currentDayIndex`) and month view (previously click-only ; now a `data-cal-row`/`data-cal-col` + `elementFromPoint`-based pointer drag across the day grid, with a live highlight on the spanned cells). Both feed the event popover's existing ALL_DAY start+end date fields via two new optional props (`initialEventType`, `endDateOverride`) rather than adding new form plumbing. Extracted the day-index-from-pointer-X math (previously duplicated for the existing event-move drag) into a shared `dayIndexFromPointerX` helper in [week-view.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/calendar/week-view.tsx>).
- 2026-08-03: Calendar — added a non-blocking same-calendar conflict/double-booking warning in the event create/edit popover ("This overlaps with X"). New `findOverlappingEvents` ([data.ts](monark/app/packages/calendar/src/server/data.ts)) reuses `listEventsForDay`'s overlap where-shape, scoped to one calendar + an exclude-id for edits ; new `calendarRouter.events.checkConflicts` query, debounced client-side in [new-event-popover.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/calendar/new-event-popover.tsx>). Scoped to same-calendar overlaps only — `participants` has no user-id linkage today, so a cross-participant check isn't cheaply possible. 3 new integration tests.
- 2026-08-03: Public API v2 (service-accounts admin UI) — a new **Admin → Service accounts** surface ([/admin/service-accounts](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/service-accounts/page.tsx>)) that makes v2 usable : create a machine principal (name + role checklist), edit its roles, mint / list / revoke its API keys, and disable / delete it. Follows the admin list idiom (`DataTable` + `TableDetailLayout`) : the table lists accounts (status / key count / created) and the detail panel holds the roles editor (checklist → `setRoles`), a keys manager (mint-once reveal / revoke, reusing the v1 `account.apiKeys.*` scope + expiry + reveal copy so nothing is duplicated), and a danger zone (disable / delete, confirm-gated) ([service-accounts-manager.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/service-accounts/service-accounts-manager.tsx>), [service-account-panel.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/service-accounts/service-account-panel.tsx>)). The role picker is fed by a new `apiKeys.serviceAccounts.assignableRoles` query that resolves the org from context (the client needs no org id). The whole surface is flag-gated on `public-api.service-accounts` : the page 404s off-flag and the new sidebar tab is hidden unless the flag resolves on (a new optional `flag` field on `ADMIN_TABS`). Fully localized (`admin.serviceAccounts.*` + `admin.tabs.serviceAccounts`, en + fr — `check:i18n` 1909 keys each). `typecheck` (23/23) + `lint` (23/23) + `check:tiers`/`check:modules`/`check:i18n` + api-keys 14 integration tests all green. **This completes Public API v2.**
- 2026-08-02: Automation / UI — **variable chip input.** A `{{ … }}` reference in a config field now renders as an atomic, styled **chip** amid free-editable text, instead of raw braces — the automation editor's message/text fields and every variable-mode field read like prose with pills, and a single Backspace removes a whole token. New generic (no automation coupling) `VariableInput` in the shared UI package ([variable-input.tsx](monark/app/packages/components/src/ui/variable-input.tsx), documented in the [package README](monark/app/packages/components/README.md)): a `contentEditable` surface kept _uncontrolled_ while typing (so the caret never jumps) that serializes back to a plain string through `onChange` and only rebuilds its DOM when `value` changes from the outside; chips are `contentEditable=false` spans, paste comes in as plain text, and a `resolveToken(raw) → { label, invalid }` prop lets the caller name a chip and flag a dead reference without the component knowing the token grammar. It exposes a `VariableInputHandle` on focus so the inspector's picker inserts a chip at the caret (the old string-slice insertion is gone).
