The automation editor ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>)) swaps its text / textarea / variable-mode inputs for `VariableInput`, builds a `resolveToken` that maps `{{ steps.<slug>.… }}` to a friendly "step name.path" and marks a missing step invalid, and threads the focus handle through `ConfigPanel` → `ConfigField`. The pure `tokenizeValue` / `serializeEditor` helpers are exported and their round-trip verified headless (mixed tokens, newlines, trailing chips, `<br>`). `@monark/components` + `web` typecheck + lint + `check:i18n` green.
- 2026-08-02: Public API v2 (service-accounts backend) — org-owned **machine principals** whose keys act as the account, not a person ([public-api-service-accounts.md](monark/app/docs/features-planning/phase-3/public-api-service-accounts.md)). A service account is a **flagged `User` row** — new `User.kind` enum (`HUMAN` default | `SERVICE`) + `User.createdBy`, migration `20260802140000_add_user_kind` — so it flows through RBAC, memberships, `createdBy`, and events with zero polymorphic surgery ; the `ApiKey` model is unchanged (`ownerUserId` points at the machine row). New admin-gated `apiKeys.serviceAccounts` sub-router ([service-accounts.ts](monark/app/packages/api-keys/src/server/service-accounts.ts)) : `create` (mints a `svc_…`-id machine user + a membership + granted roles), `list`, `setRoles`, `disable`, `delete`, and nested `keys.{list,create,revoke}` — all behind a new `api-keys.manage-service-accounts` permission and the new `public-api.service-accounts` flag (off by default, independent of the v1 user-key surface so v2 ships dark). The **load-bearing safety work is the guards** that keep a machine row out of every human-only path : notification/email dispatch short-circuits on `kind = SERVICE` ([dispatch.ts](monark/app/packages/notifications/src/server/dispatch.ts)), and the admin Users list + every assignee/member picker (users, automation, kanban, calendar) filter `kind = HUMAN`. `authenticateApiKey` now also rejects a key whose owner is disabled/deleted (so disabling a service account instantly kills its keys — and this retroactively hardens v1 user keys too). New machine ids are minted with a `svc_` prefix (never a valid Supabase auth subject) and a reserved `@service.invalid` email. 6 new integration tests (create→machine-user shape, list, mint→**authenticate as the account**, disable→keys die, delete→cascade, flag gate) ; api-keys 14 integration + full unit suite + `typecheck` (23/23) + `check:tiers`/`check:modules`/`check:i18n` all green. The `/admin/service-accounts` UI is the remaining v2 increment.
- 2026-08-02: Public API (integration tests + error-mapping hardening) — an end-to-end HTTP suite for `/api/v1` that drives the **real Express app over supertest** against a Postgres testcontainer, with a real `mrk_` key authenticating a real org-ADMIN owner ([public-api.test.ts](monark/app/services/api/tests/integration/public-api.test.ts)). 13 cases cover the whole request path together : the unauthenticated-but-flag-gated `openapi.json`, auth (no key / bad key → 401, valid key → 200 + `X-RateLimit-*` headers), reads (`/models`, `/models/:key/records`, `/records/:id`, unknown id → 404), a write with a write-scoped key (200), scope enforcement (read-only key → 403), body validation (→ 400), revocation (revoked key → 401), and the `public-api.enabled` kill switch (flag off → 404 even with a valid key). Building the suite surfaced a real robustness gap : the facade's error→HTTP mapping ([mount.ts](monark/app/services/api/src/public/mount.ts)) classified errors with `instanceof`, which is unreliable when an error crosses package boundaries (a domain error thrown inside a tRPC procedure, re-wrapped by the caller) or when the module graph holds more than one copy of a class — so a `NotFoundError` re-wrapped by the caller fell through to a 500. Reworked `sendError` to classify by **shape** (`name` / `code` / `statusCode` / `issues`) instead of `instanceof`, preferring a wrapped domain error's own status/code/message ; every case now maps correctly regardless of which module instance minted the error. Full suite green (24 api integration tests incl. the prior 11 server tests) ; `typecheck` (all packages) + `check:tiers` / `check:modules` / `check:i18n`.

Excludes `event-type` (trigger-only, drives the event-fields UI) and `schedule` (structured builder). (2) `validateGraph` gained a `duplicate-slug` error so two nodes sharing a `{{ steps.<slug> }}` address are caught pre-run ([validate.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/contracts/validate.ts)) — the editor already prevents dupes, this covers graphs built via the public API; surfaced in the editor's preflight list and the server's `runNow` guard ([router.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/router.ts)). (3) First **user-facing** doc for the feature: [docs/user-guide/automation-variables.md](monark/app/docs/user-guide/automation-variables.md) (linked from the guide index) explains references, the picker, the value/variable toggle, reference names, and Set Variable — no code, for the person building automations. New i18n keys `useVariable` / `useFixedValue` / `variablePlaceholder` (ICU-escaped literal braces) / `preflight.duplicateSlug`, plus a reworded `variablesHint` (en + fr). New tests: 2 validate-unit (duplicate/unique slug) + 1 integration composing the whole redesign (a `steps.<slug>` output → Set Variable → `{{ vars }}` read downstream). **automation 43 unit + 29 integration green**; `web` typecheck + lint + `check:i18n` green.
- 2026-08-02: Testing / CI — **coverage-floor hardening**, moving the gate back up after the CI-fix that had lowered it. Root cause of the depressed floors was flaky _measurement_, not thin tests: the integration-coverage step ran testcontainers under turbo parallelism (container contention) and had a test-isolation race in `organizations/bootstrap`, so a package would intermittently collapse to unit-only. Fixed by serializing the integration tasks (`--concurrency=1` on `test:integration` / `test:integration:coverage` in [package.json](monark/app/package.json)) and resetting the org tables in `beforeEach`/`afterEach` ([bootstrap.test.ts](monark/app/packages/organizations/tests/integration/bootstrap.test.ts)). With a reliable merged run, re-baselined every floor in [tools/merge-coverage.ts](monark/app/tools/merge-coverage.ts) (~5pts below true), ratcheting up where new tests justify and correcting the handful the flaky baseline had over-set; the gate now also **fails on any covered-but-un-gated package** so a new module can't drift in un-floored (this caught `@monark/api-keys`, now floored). New tests for the lowest-coverage surfaces: rbac guard wrappers ([guards.test.ts](monark/app/packages/rbac/tests/integration/guards.test.ts)), common keyset pagination ([pagination.test.ts](monark/app/packages/common/tests/pagination.test.ts)), calendar data layer 18%→96% ([data.test.ts](monark/app/packages/calendar/tests/integration/data.test.ts)), organizations read / auto-membership / metadata 0%→100% each, secrets router authz ([secrets-router.test.ts](monark/app/packages/secrets/tests/integration/secrets-router.test.ts)), and auth's HMAC email-action tokens ([email-action-token.test.ts](monark/app/packages/auth/tests/email-action-token.test.ts)). A second pass lifted the two lowest stable modules further: rbac 57%→86% via the write.ts role-CRUD wrappers ([write-crud.test.ts](monark/app/packages/rbac/tests/integration/write-crud.test.ts)) and the boot-time permission / event-type registrations ([registrations.test.ts](monark/app/packages/rbac/tests/registrations.test.ts)), floor 50→80 ; webhooks 63%→92% via a full tRPC router suite ([router.test.ts](monark/app/packages/webhooks/tests/integration/router.test.ts)) driving every procedure's RBAC guard, the create→secret-once contract, update diff, rotation, delete idempotency, and delivery inspection, floor 60→85. Along the way fixed two latent missing workspace-dep declarations a clean isolated install had begun failing on (`@monark/calendar`→`@monark/users`, `@monark/data-models`→`@monark/feature-flags`) and updated the account sidebar test for the new **API keys** tab. All 15 integration-coverage tasks green; merge gate clears every floor.
- 2026-08-02: Data Models — **relation traversal** for MonarkQL : a dotted field filters through a relation, `assignee.title:alice` = "records whose related record matches". The AST needs no new shape — the leaf's `field` is `"assignee.title"` and the compiler splits on the dot ([query-compiler.ts](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/server/query-compiler.ts)), emitting an `EXISTS` over the target model's `DataRecord`s joined by the relation's stored id(s) — scalar (ONE : `t.id = r.data->>'rel'`) or array (MANY : `t.id IN (jsonb_array_elements_text(...))`). The JSONB column root is now parameterized (`r.data` outer, `t.data` in the subquery) so the outer relation value stays unambiguous under the alias shadow. **Safety** : `buildRelationTargets` ([router.ts](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/server/router.ts)) only resolves DATA_MODEL relations in the caller's org, requires the caller can **read** the target model (else 403), and threads the target's **row-level** role-access into the subquery (`recordRoleAccessSql` gained an `alias` param) — so traversal can't surface a related record the caller couldn't open directly. One level only (`a.b`, not `a.b.c`) ; SYSTEM_MODEL targets aren't traversable. New `dataModels.records.queryFields` procedure returns the model's fields plus **"virtual" dotted fields** for readable relations, so the query bar parses + autocompletes `assignee.title` like any field ([records-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/models/[modelKey]/records-list.tsx>) fetches it). Tests: `integration/traversal.test.ts` (ONE + MANY membership, composition, non-relation + two-level guards, and the router path that resolves targets) + 2 DSL (dotted parse + round-trip) — **data-models 78 unit + 100 integration green**. This completes the MonarkQL plan (core, DSL, query bar, variables, saved views, traversal) ; typecheck + lint + `check:i18n` + `check:tiers` + `check:modules` green.
- 2026-08-02: Data Models — **saved views** for MonarkQL : a named query tree per model, personal by default or `shared` to everyone with read access, editable only by its owner. New core table `DataRecordView` (base.prisma, migration [20260802140000_add_data_record_views](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/migrations/20260802140000_add_data_record_views/migration.sql)) storing the `FilterNode` JSON (validated with `filterQuerySchema` on write). tRPC `dataModels.views.{list, create, update, delete}` ([router.ts](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/server/router.ts)) — reading gates on `data-models.record-read`, editing is owner-scoped (`requireOwnedView` → 403 for a non-owner, even on a shared view); list returns a `mine` flag so the UI can gate rename/delete. Data layer `listDataRecordViews` (mine ∪ shared) + CRUD ([data.ts](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/server/data.ts)). Web: a **Views** menu ([views-menu.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/models/[modelKey]/views-menu.tsx>)) to load a saved view (prints its tree back into the now-controlled query bar), save the current query (name + share toggle), and delete your own (confirm) — wired into the record-list actions behind the flag; the query bar became controlled so a picked view can populate it. A stale view (a field it referenced was archived/retyped) simply errors at run time when the compiler rejects it — nothing to migrate. 6 new router integration tests (RBAC deny, personal-invisible-to-others, share-then-visible, non-owner-can't-edit/delete, owner rename+delete) — **data-models 94 integration green**; typecheck + lint + `check:i18n` (1854 keys) + `check:tiers` + `check:modules` green. Deferred now down to relation traversal.
- 2026-08-02: Data Models — **dynamic query variables** for MonarkQL (`@me`, `@today`, and relative date anchors `@now` / `@yesterday` / `@tomorrow` / `@startOfWeek` / `@endOfWeek` / `@startOfMonth` / `@endOfMonth` / `@startOfYear` / `@endOfYear`). A saved/typed query like `assignee:@me due:@startOfWeek..@endOfWeek` now means the right thing for whoever runs it, whenever. New [`contracts/query-variables.ts`](monark/app/packages/query/src/contracts/query-variables.ts) (pure/isomorphic): the variable catalog, `isQueryVariable`, `resolveQueryVariable(token, ctx)` (UTC date math, matching the formula engine), and `variablesForKind` for the autocomplete. Values carry the raw `@token` string — the parser/printer treat it like any value — and the SQL compiler swaps it for a concrete **bound** value at compile time against a `QueryContext { userId, now }` supplied by the router; an unknown or context-less variable is a 400 ([query-compiler.ts](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/server/query-compiler.ts), `listDataRecordsWithQuery` gains `queryContext`). The query bar autocompletes `@me` on relation fields and `@today…` on date fields ([query-bar.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/query/query-bar.tsx)). Tests: `query-variables.test.ts` (6 unit) + 2 integration (`@today` resolves end-to-end; unknown variable rejected) + 2 DSL round-trips — **data-models 76 unit + 88 integration green**; typecheck + lint clean.
- 2026-08-02: Data Models — **structured query language (MonarkQL) on the record list**, a GitHub / Notion / Linear-style filter system parallel to (and reusing) the classic filter menu, gated behind a new `data-models.query-language` flag (off by default). The canonical form is a **query tree** — predicate leaves + boolean groups — that both the menu and a new text query bar edit; the tree is the one thing the server runs. New [`contracts/query.ts`](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/contracts/query.ts): the `FilterNode` AST, the operator vocabulary, the field-type→operator taxonomy (`filterableKindOf` / `legalOps`, resolving FORMULA through its inferred result type), and a depth/count-bounded zod input schema. Deliberately **separate** from the FORMULA engine — a formula is a per-record compute (one scalar, in JS), a query is a set selector (a predicate over the table, compiled to SQL). New [`server/query-compiler.ts`](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/server/query-compiler.ts) compiles a tree to a boolean `Prisma.Sql` fragment, run by `listDataRecordsWithQuery` ([data.ts](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/server/data.ts)) — **raw SQL, not Prisma's structured JSON `where`**, for two reasons: text matching is case-insensitive (`ILIKE`), and comparisons hit the **expression indexes** `indexing.ts` builds (`(data->>'key')::numeric` / `::timestamptz`, GIN on arrays) that Prisma's `data:{path,gt}` form would miss. Field keys are interpolated as identifiers (safe: validated `^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*$` + immutable, the same guarantee the indexing DDL relies on); every value is a bound parameter. Keyset pagination (`updatedAt desc, id desc`, cursor resolved via subquery) and the record role-access predicate are preserved as SQL, so the compiled path is access-identical to the structured one. A text DSL ([`contracts/query-dsl.ts`](monark/app/packages/query/src/contracts/query-dsl.ts), `parseQuery` / `printQuery`) serializes the same tree — `status:open -assignee:present`, `priority:>3`, `tags:&red,blue`, `due:2026-01-01..2026-06-01`, implicit-AND / `OR` / parentheses — mirroring the formula engine's tokenizer → recursive-descent structure and round-tripping (parse∘print is identity on normalized trees). Web: a new **query bar** ([query-bar.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/query/query-bar.tsx)) with context-aware autocomplete (field names → value options from the field metadata), live parse + inline error, wired into the record list beside the sorting/columns tools behind the flag ([records-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/models/[modelKey]/records-list.tsx>), flag read in [page.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/models/[modelKey]/page.tsx>)); `records.list` gained an optional `filter` tree alongside the legacy `fieldFilters`. `@monark/db` now value-exports `Prisma` for its raw-SQL helpers. Tests: `query.test.ts` (taxonomy + schema) + `query-dsl.test.ts` (parser/printer/round-trip) unit + `integration/query-language.test.ts` (every operator family, groups, negation, keyset pagination against a real Postgres testcontainer) — **data-models 68 unit + 86 integration green**; web typecheck + lint + `check:i18n` (1837 keys) + `check:tiers` + `check:modules` all green. Dev doc: [data-model-queries.md](monark/app/docs/technical-documentation/data-model-queries.md). Deferred (explicit follow-ups): dynamic variables (`@me` / `today`), relation traversal, and saved views.

Minting opens a dialog for a name, a scope picker (checkboxes driven by `@monark/api-keys`'s `ALL_API_SCOPES` so it tracks the catalog), and an expiry preset (never / 30 / 90 / 365 days) ; on success the plaintext is shown **exactly once** in a copy-to-clipboard reveal dialog with a "you won't see this again" warning, matching the write-once contract ([api-keys-section.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/account/api-keys-section.tsx>)). The list shows each key's name, prefix, scopes, status (active / expired / revoked), and created / last-used / expiry, with a confirm-gated revoke. The whole surface is gated on the `api-keys.manage` permission (a member without it sees an explainer, and the `apiKeys.list` query stays disabled) ; it follows the account-section idiom (`PageSection` + `ConfirmDialog`) with a layout-accurate `loading.tsx` skeleton and a new sidebar entry. Fully localized (new `account.apiKeys.*` + `common.done`, en + fr — `check:i18n` 1832 keys each). Added files typecheck + lint clean ; `check:tiers` / `check:modules` / `check:i18n` green. (Note : the shared `web` typecheck is currently red on unrelated in-flight query-language work in `data/models/[modelKey]`, not from this change.)
- 2026-08-02: Automation — **variables now work on select / boolean / number config fields (value ↔ variable toggle).** Those controls (a dropdown, a switch, a numeric spinner) can't hold a `{{ }}` token, so there was no way to feed them a variable — the picker only inserts into a text box. Each such field ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>) `ConfigField`) now shows a small `{{ }}` toggle that swaps the native control for a plain text input accepting a reference (and wired to the click-to-insert picker like every other text field). It's forced on automatically whenever the stored value already is an expression, so a saved variable never silently disappears from a dropdown that has no matching option. Applies to `select` / `data-model` / `user` / `secret` / `boolean` / `number` ; `text` / `textarea` / `json` already took references, and `event-type` (trigger-only, drives the event-fields UI) / `schedule` (structured builder) are intentionally excluded. New i18n keys `useVariable` / `useFixedValue` / `variablePlaceholder` (en + fr). `web` typecheck + lint + `check:i18n` green.
- 2026-08-02: Automation — **stable-name addressing (phase 5 done, from [automation-data-flow.md](monark/app/docs/technical-documentation/automation-data-flow.md); completes the data-flow redesign).** A node's output is now addressed by a stable, human, editable **slug** — `{{ steps.find_record.id }}` instead of `{{ n-a1b2.id }}`. Every node carries a unique-in-graph slug ([slug.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/contracts/slug.ts): `slugifyLabel` + `uniqueNodeSlug`, `NodeInstance.slug` on the [graph contract](monark/app/packages/automation/src/contracts/graph.ts)), derived from its label on creation and de-duplicated. The engine mirrors each upstream output under `steps.<slug>` in the interpolation scope and teaches `dataDependencies` to resolve a `{{ steps.<slug> }}` reference back to its node for ordering ([engine.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/engine.ts)); the raw `<nodeId>` key stays in scope so graphs saved before slugs (and any un-migrated reference) still resolve. Editor ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>)): assigns slugs on load + on drop, enforces uniqueness, emits `{{ steps.<slug> }}` tokens from the picker, adds slug editing to the rename dialog (a "Reference name" field, validated + collision-checked, that rewrites `{{ steps.<oldslug> }}` references so they don't break), persists `slug` in the saved graph, and on load silently rewrites legacy `{{ <nodeId> }}` references to slug form (self-persists on the next save). New i18n keys `nodeDisplayName` / `nodeSlug` / `slugHint` / `slugInvalid` / `slugTaken` (en + fr). Also cleaned up two vestigial graph-contract fields (`inputs`, documented as dead post-phase-4). New tests: 6 slug-helper unit + 2 engine-unit (`steps.<slug>` ordering + interpolation) + 1 integration (a node referenced only by `{{ steps.<slug> }}` is pulled in and resolves) — **automation 41 unit + 28 integration green**; `web` typecheck + lint + `check:i18n` (1782 keys) green. With this the [data-flow redesign](monark/app/docs/technical-documentation/automation-data-flow.md) is fully shipped (phases 1–5).
- 2026-08-02: Public API (facade) — the callable **`/api/v1` REST surface** over Data Models + records, authenticated by `@monark/api-keys` and gated behind a new `public-api.enabled` flag (off by default). The facade is a thin adapter, not a re-implementation : each route runs the SAME tRPC procedures the web app uses through a server-side caller ([services/api/src/public/caller.ts](monark/app/services/api/src/public/caller.ts)) built from the API key's `{ userId, activeOrganizationId }`, so all RBAC, per-record access, event emission, and validation are reused verbatim. Endpoints (v1) : `GET /me`, `GET /models`, `GET /models/{key}`, `GET /models/{key}/fields`, `GET/POST /models/{key}/records`, `GET/PATCH/DELETE /records/{id}`, plus an unauthenticated (but flag-gated) `GET /openapi.json`. Each route declares a coarse **scope** (`assertScope`, layered on top of the procedure's fine-grained permission) and validates query/body with zod, mapping `ZodError` / `TRPCError` / `AppError` to REST error codes ([mount.ts](monark/app/services/api/src/public/mount.ts), [routes.ts](monark/app/services/api/src/public/routes.ts)). **`@monark/public-api`** is now a library core module (no tRPC router) exporting the route-descriptor type, `assertScope`, a curated hand-rolled zod→JSON-schema step, and an OpenAPI-3 builder that derives the spec from the same descriptors that serve traffic (so it can't drift) ([openapi.ts](monark/app/packages/public-api/src/server/openapi.ts)). New shared **rate-limit primitive** `@monark/common/rate-limit` : a storage-agnostic token bucket whose pure step function is unit-tested and whose default Postgres store (new core `RateLimitBucket` table, migration `20260802130000_add_rate_limit_bucket`) shares the limit across api replicas (no Redis in the stack) ; the `/api/v1` middleware applies a per-key 5 req/s + burst-20 limit with `X-RateLimit-*` / `Retry-After` headers ([rate-limit.ts](monark/app/packages/common/src/rate-limit.ts)). 10 new unit tests (token-bucket math + OpenAPI builder + `assertScope`). `pnpm gen` + `typecheck` + `eslint` + all four gates green ; the `RateLimitBucket` migration applies with `db:migrate:dev`. End-to-end HTTP integration tests for the mount + the v1 keys management UI are the next milestone.
- 2026-08-02: Automation — **workflow variables + a Set Variable node (phase 5 from [automation-data-flow.md](monark/app/docs/technical-documentation/automation-data-flow.md)).** Adds a run-global `{{ vars.<name> }}` scope distinct from per-step outputs — the literal "global variable" the original design gestured at (set a value on either arm of a branch and read it after they converge, or give a value a stable readable name). New **Set Variable** node ([set-variable.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/nodes/set-variable.ts)) writes `vars[name] = value` (value is a literal or a `{{ }}` reference, its resolved type preserved; compute with a Transform node first). The write is **durable across a suspend**: rather than couple the engine to a node type, the node registry gained a generic `collectVars(output) -> { name: value }` seam ([registry.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/registry.ts)) — the engine merges a node's `collectVars` result into `vars` after it runs, and on resume rebuilds `vars` by replaying each prior step's persisted output in `sequence` order ([engine.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/engine.ts)), so a variable set before a Delay is still in scope when the run resumes. The editor's variable picker gained a **Workflow variables** group listing the names any upstream Set Variable node defines, click-to-insertable like the rest ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>), new `varWorkflowVars` / `varWorkflowVarHint` keys en + fr). New tests: 1 engine-unit (`{{ vars.x }}` resolves from scope) + 2 integration (a Set Variable value reaches a downstream node; a variable survives a Delay suspend/resume) — **automation 33 unit + 27 integration green**. `web` typecheck + lint + `check:i18n` green. Also tidied a phase-4 leftover: the vestigial `linkable` config-field flag is documented as a no-op ([contracts/nodes.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/contracts/nodes.ts)).
- 2026-08-02: Public API (foundation) — **`@monark/api-keys`** core module, the credential layer the public REST API will authenticate with. Hashed, scoped, org-pinned API keys : `mint` returns a `mrk_<base64url(32)>` plaintext exactly once and stores only its SHA-256 hash (a `@unique` column), matching the invite / webhook / trusted-device pattern ([data.ts](monark/app/packages/api-keys/src/server/data.ts)). `authenticateApiKey(plaintext)` verifies a key and resolves it to `{ userId: ownerUserId, organizationId, scopes }` — rejecting unknown / revoked / expired / wrong-prefix keys and dropping unknown scopes — which the (next-milestone) public-API facade turns into the `{ userId, activeOrganizationId }` context the existing RBAC guards already consume, so **v1 keys act as their owning user and can never exceed that user's grants** (zero RBAC schema change). Keys are **org-pinned** and carry a coarse **scope** allow-list (`API_SCOPES`) that only ever narrows the owner's permissions. A management router (`apiKeys.{list,create,revoke}`, gated by a new `api-keys.manage` permission, never returns the hash) backs the forthcoming key-management UI ; emits `api-keys.key-created` / `-revoked` (webhook-subscribable, never carrying the secret). New `ApiKey` model ([prisma/api-keys.prisma](monark/app/packages/api-keys/prisma/api-keys.prisma), FK+cascade to `Organization` + `User`, migration `20260802120000_add_api_keys`). `register*` wired at api boot. Also scaffolded **`@monark/public-api`** (the REST + OpenAPI facade — next milestone ; its integration suite is an acknowledged gap for now). Design: [docs/features-planning/phase-3/public-api.md](monark/app/docs/features-planning/phase-3/public-api.md). 8 integration tests (mint/hash/authenticate/revoke/expiry/scope-filter). `pnpm gen` + `typecheck` (api-keys + public-api + api) + `eslint` + `check:tiers`/`check:modules`/`check:i18n` green ; migration to apply with `db:migrate:dev`.
- 2026-08-02: Calendar — added per-user view settings: hide/show weekends, first day of week (Sun/Mon), default view, 12h/24h time format, and a working-hours highlight, edited from a new "view options" popover in the calendar toolbar ([calendar-shell.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/calendar/calendar-shell.tsx>)). Stored via the users metadata sidecar as a single JSON blob (new `calendarRouter.settings.get/set/reset`, [server/index.ts](monark/app/packages/calendar/src/server/index.ts)), gated on `ctx.userId` only since it's self-owned data — the generic `trpc.users.metadata.*` procedures were not usable here since they `requirePermission` unconditionally even for a caller's own data. Consolidated three duplicated, hardcoded-Sunday-first `getWeekStart` copies (and two duplicated weekday-label arrays) into a new shared [date-utils.ts](monark/app/packages/calendar/src/client/date-utils.ts), also fixing a latent bug where month view's weekend tinting was column-index-based (silently wrong once week-start becomes configurable) rather than date-based. Hiding weekends does true column filtering (weekday columns expand to fill the width, not just CSS-hidden) across week and month view's multi-day/all-day event layout math. All defaults reproduce prior hardcoded behavior exactly, so this ships additive with no visible change until a user opens the popover. 17 new unit tests for the date-math edge cases ([date-utils.test.ts](monark/app/packages/calendar/tests/date-utils.test.ts)).
- 2026-08-02: Testing — raised two more coverage floors with real tests. **calendar 43% → 86%**: new [tests/integration/data.test.ts](monark/app/packages/calendar/tests/integration/data.test.ts) (13 specs) covering the previously-18%-covered `data.ts` — calendar CRUD, role-scoped `listAccessibleCalendars` (manager sees all / unrestricted-only / role-matched / archived), `ensurePersonalCalendar` (create + backfill + idempotent), event create/update/soft-delete with reminder scheduling, day-window + case-insensitive search, reminder delivery (`getPendingReminders` once-then-`markReminderNotified`, skips soft-deleted), and members resolution (all-members vs role-restricted) ; `data.ts` now 96%, floor 35→**75**. **organizations 56% → 67%**: `read.ts` 0→**100%** (`tests/integration/read.test.ts`, 10 specs — `getCurrentOrg` across every branch: no user, stale membership, multi-tenant, single-tenant sole-org fallback, soft-deleted-org count; `requireOrg` throw) and `auto-membership.ts` 0→**78%** (`tests/integration/auto-membership.test.ts`, 6 specs — singleton grant + `member-joined` emit, idempotent, respects operator removal via `leftAt`, multi-tenant / count≠1 skips, and the sign-up/sign-in bus subscribers), floor 50→**60** (functions 70→75). All 5 organizations integration files pass together (75 specs). Merge gate: every measured package clears its floor.
- 2026-08-02: Automation — **removed the data-wire authoring path (phase 4, option a from [automation-data-flow.md](monark/app/docs/technical-documentation/automation-data-flow.md)).** With references now usable everywhere (the hyphen-regex fix + the click-to-insert picker), per-field data wires were pure redundancy, so the editor now has exactly **one** data-authoring surface: `{{ }}` references via the picker. Dropped from [automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>): the `field:<key>` input ports on the node face, the per-field "expose as input" Cable toggle + `ResolvedValueField`, the data-edge styling / validation, and the now-vestigial `exposed` node state (and its `inputs` persistence in `toGraphPayload`). `isValidConnection` now rejects any `field:` target outright ; the canvas legend drops the "Variable" swatch (edges are only the run-order spine). **Backward-compat**: the engine still resolves legacy `field:` edges at run time (`unwrapLinkedValue` survives only on that path), and on load the editor rewrites each `field:` edge to an equivalent `{{ source }}` / `{{ source.value }}` reference in the target's config (unless it already holds a `{{`), so old automations run unchanged and self-migrate on the next save. Removed 6 now-dead i18n keys (`fieldWired`, `useAsInput`, `removeInput`, `resolvedFromRun`, `portDataHint`, `legendData` — en + fr, 1764 each). `web` typecheck + lint + `check:i18n` green ; automation 32 unit green (engine untouched this phase).

- 2026-08-02: Automation — **fixed `{{ }}` references silently not resolving for node outputs, and made a referenced value source run without a wire.** The editor mints hyphenated node ids (`genId("n")` → `n-a1b2`), but the engine's interpolation regex matched `[\w.]`, which excludes `-`, so a `{{ n-a1b2.field }}` token never matched and passed through as literal text — i.e. every node-to-node reference (only `{{ trigger.* }}` has no hyphen) was quietly broken. The regex now allows `-` ([engine.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/engine.ts) `WHOLE_TOKEN_RE` / `EMBEDDED_TOKEN_RE`). `executionOrder` also gained **reference-based dependencies**: a `{{ <node>… }}` token in a config now pulls that node into the run and orders it before its consumer (a `dataDependencies` scan folding in `{{ }}` refs + legacy `field:` edges), so a Constant / Transform referenced only via `{{ }}` — with no wire — still executes. Kahn's sort de-dupes `source->target` so a pair present as both a control edge and a reference can't inflate the indegree. Legacy `field:` data edges keep working (folded in as dependencies + the existing field-override at run time). 3 new engine-unit tests (hyphen reference resolves, `{{ }}`-only value-source pull-in + ordering, hyphenated dependency); **automation 32 unit + 25 integration green**. This makes the phase-2 picker genuinely functional — the tokens it inserts now resolve.

- 2026-08-02: Testing — **restored + raised the coverage floor after fixing why it dropped.** Root cause was a flaky _measurement_, not thin tests: the integration-coverage step (a) ran packages in parallel under `turbo` so many Postgres testcontainers contended, and (b) hit a **test-isolation race** in `organizations/bootstrap.test` (its "no orgs exist" case had no `beforeEach` DB reset, so a prior spec file's rows broke it). When a package flaked, its _merged_ number collapsed to unit-only (near-0 for data-layer code), forcing the floor down. **Fixes**: serialized the integration tasks (`test:integration{,:coverage}` → `turbo … --concurrency=1`, [package.json](monark/app/package.json)) so containers don't contend ; made `bootstrap.test` self-isolating (reset the org tables in `beforeEach`, not just `afterEach`). With that, a clean run is **17/17 unit + 14/14 integration** and the true numbers are back (automation **76%** not the flaky 34%, auth **57%** not 10%, data-models **71%** not 4%). **Floor moved back up + every module now gated** ([tools/merge-coverage.ts](monark/app/tools/merge-coverage.ts)): added floors for the six previously-un-gated modules (automation, data-models, kanban, calendar, secrets, files) and raised common (55→70 lines), notifications (60→65), webhooks branches (80→85), all set ~5 pts below the reliable baseline. **Structural fix**: a package that emits coverage but has no `THRESHOLDS` entry now **fails** the gate (was a silent `continue`) — so a new module can't ship un-gated again. **New tests for the highest-risk gaps**: `rbac/tests/integration/guards.test.ts` (9 specs — the `requirePermission`/`requireRoleKey` enforcement seam: Unauthorized/Forbidden throws, ADMIN short-circuit, `effectiveOrg` fallback + orgId override) and `@monark/common/tests/pagination.test.ts` (8 specs — `resolveLimit`/`cursorFindArgs`/`toPage`). Merge gate: _every measured package clears its floor_. Genuine remaining low spots (real, not artifacts): `calendar` 43%, `service/web` 6% (own track — server-action + component tests), `organizations`/`rbac`/`auth` ~57%.

- 2026-08-02: Automation — **variable picker is now click-to-insert** (phase 2 UX from [automation-data-flow.md](monark/app/docs/technical-documentation/automation-data-flow.md)). Focusing a text / textarea config field registers it as the insert target ; clicking a variable in the inspector's **Variables** panel drops its `{{ ... }}` token at the cursor (mid-text supported) instead of only copying it. Implemented in [automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>): `ConfigPanel` holds the active-input ref + an `insertVariable` handler (caret-preserving, `requestAnimationFrame` refocus), `ConfigField` registers text inputs `onFocus`, and the picker buttons `preventDefault` their mousedown so the field keeps focus ; clicking a variable with no field focused falls back to clipboard copy. New `automation.editor.insertVariable` key (en + fr, 1770 each). Together with the phase-1 output schemas, upstream node fields are both **visible and insertable without a test run** — the "globals can't be used in a regular input" gap is closed. `typecheck` + `lint` + `check:i18n` green.

- 2026-08-01: Automation — **node output schemas + a design for unifying variable transmission.** Wrote up [automation-data-flow.md](monark/app/docs/technical-documentation/automation-data-flow.md), which settles how a node's fields get their values (literals, `{{ }}` references, wired links), calls the wire/global-variable overlap what it is (two front-ends over one thing), and lays out a 5-phase path toward one reference model + a variable picker + declared node output schemas (with prior-art notes on n8n / Make / Unreal). **Phase 1 shipped:** each node descriptor now declares an `outputFields` schema (`{ key, type, description }`, mirroring the event-`fields` pattern) in [contracts/nodes.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/contracts/nodes.ts), and all 18 built-in action/value nodes ([server/nodes/](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/nodes)) declare theirs (triggers keep sourcing fields from the event scope). It flows to the editor through `nodeTypes.list` automatically. **Phase 2 (data side):** the inspector's variable picker now sources those declared schemas, so an upstream node's referenceable fields (e.g. Find Record's `id` / `title` / `data`) appear **without needing a test run first** ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>) `variableGroups`), merged with any extra keys a run actually produced. Pending: click-to-insert (currently click-to-copy), stable-name addressing, wires-as-reference-sugar, and workflow `vars`. `pnpm gen` + automation/web `typecheck` + `lint` + automation tests (29) green.

- 2026-07-29: Security — **audit follow-ups (batch 2): hardening the DoS / abuse surfaces.** **Formula engine** ([formula.ts](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/contracts/formula.ts)): a max input length (10k chars) + a recursion-depth cap (64) so a very long or deeply-nested expression can't burn CPU / overflow the parser stack, and `FUNCTIONS` lookups now use `Object.hasOwn` so inherited Object props (`constructor`, `hasOwnProperty`, …) parse as a clean "unknown function" instead of resolving to a prototype method; the automation `transform` node caps its expression at 2000 chars ([transform.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/nodes/transform.ts)). **Outbound HTTP** ([@monark/common/http](monark/app/packages/common/src/http.ts)): `safeFetch` gained an opt-in `maxBytes` response cap — the body is streamed inside the timeout window and the request throws if it exceeds the cap, so a hostile receiver can't force an unbounded read; the returned `Response` is reconstructed with the bounded body so `.ok`/`.text()` still work. **Files** ([files/server/index.ts](monark/app/packages/files/src/server/index.ts)): creating a **public** bucket now requires SYSADMIN (`hasSysadminAssignment`, newly exported from [@monark/rbac/server](monark/app/packages/rbac/src/server/index.ts)) — an org-tier `files.manage-buckets` holder can only create private buckets, since a public bucket serves stable unauthenticated URLs; and `finalize` now enforces the real object size against `bucket.fileSizeLimit ?? MAX_FILE_SIZE`, so a policy-less bucket is still bounded at the hard ceiling in app code (the `fileSizeLimit` column is INT4, so the ceiling stays a runtime comparison rather than a stored default). New tests: 3 formula cases (over-length, deep-nesting, inherited-prop rejection), 2 `safeFetch` maxBytes cases, and a public-bucket-denied router case. Deferred (still in [backlog](monark/app/docs/todo/backlog.md)): `FileBucket` org-scoping (needs a migration + a Supabase bucket-naming decision), HTTP-trigger replay/rate-limit (needs secret denormalization + a rate-limit primitive), DNS-rebind TOCTOU pinning, PENDING-file sweep. Repo `typecheck` clean + lint + `check:tiers`/`check:modules`/`check:i18n` + affected suites (common 64, data-models unit 31, automation 25, files 12) green.

- 2026-07-29: Secrets — **router / access-control integration tests** for the `/admin/secrets` surface, closing the gap where only the data layer + crypto were specced. New [secrets-router.test.ts](monark/app/packages/secrets/tests/integration/secrets-router.test.ts) drives the real `secretsRouter` via `createCaller` as scoped (non-admin) users : **deny** `adminList` without `secrets.read` (a `manage`-only user is still denied) and `adminSet`/`adminDelete` without `secrets.manage` ; reject managing another org's secrets (non-member) ; the **write-only** guarantee — a reader's `adminList` returns key + metadata but never the value/ciphertext ; the emitted `secrets.created/updated/deleted` events carry the key + org but **never** the plaintext (asserted on the captured events) ; and a set → list → delete round-trip incl. a no-op delete that emits nothing. 6 new specs (15/15 secrets integration) ; `typecheck` + `lint` green.

- 2026-07-29: Security — **audit remediation across the automation, files, and outbound-HTTP surfaces** (findings from a full-branch security review). **(H1) Cross-tenant record tamper/delete**: the automation Update/Delete Record nodes called the data-layer mutators with a caller-supplied `recordId` and no org scoping — an author with `record-write`/`-delete` in their org could mutate or hard-delete any record by id (another org's, or one they're row-level-restricted from). Both nodes now resolve the record and enforce `record.organizationId === ctx.organizationId` **and** `isDataRecordRoleAccessible` (mirroring the tRPC router) via a shared `requireOwnerAccessibleRecord` ([nodes/data.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/nodes/data.ts)); `isDataRecordRoleAccessible` is now exported from [@monark/data-models/server](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/server/index.ts). **(H2) Cross-tenant user actions**: the automation user nodes (`user-get`, `user-set-active`, `user-update-profile`, `user-set-metadata`) acted on an arbitrary `userId` with only the owner's org-level permission checked — they now require the target to be a member of the run's org via a new `requireTargetInOrg` (backed by `isMember`, now exported from [@monark/organizations/server](monark/app/packages/organizations/src/server/index.ts)); `user-get` is scoped so it can't be a cross-org directory oracle. **(H3) SSRF**: [@monark/common/http](monark/app/packages/common/src/http.ts) now rejects `https://` to a literal private/loopback/link-local/reserved IP (incl. `169.254.169.254` metadata + IPv4-mapped IPv6), fails **closed** on an unset `NODE_ENV`, sets `redirect: "manual"` so a benign first hop can't 302 into an internal target, and adds a production DNS-resolution guard in `safeFetch`; the webhooks delivery worker also stops following redirects ([worker.ts](monark/app/packages/webhooks/src/server/worker.ts)). **(M2) File MIME/size spoofing**: `files.finalize` now overwrites the client-declared `contentType` with storage's observed type and re-validates size + MIME against the bucket policy (deleting the stray object on violation), so a "PDF-only" field can't be fed HTML/SVG ([files/index.ts](monark/app/packages/files/src/server/index.ts), [data.ts](monark/app/packages/files/src/server/data.ts) `markFileReady`). **(Low)** `setSecret` is now an atomic `upsert` (no P2002 race). New regression tests: two cross-org automation guards (record delete + user disable both refused) and SSRF unit cases (literal-IP rejection, fail-closed `NODE_ENV`). Deferred (tracked, lower-priority): `FileBucket` org-scoping (needs a schema migration), HTTP-trigger replay/rate-limit, a formula length/depth cap, PENDING-file sweep, DNS-rebind TOCTOU pinning. `pnpm typecheck` (repo-clean) + lint + `check:tiers`/`check:modules`/`check:i18n` + affected suites (common 62, secrets 9, automation 25, files 11, webhooks 68+38) green.

- 2026-07-28: Data Models — **File & Attachment field types**. Two new `DataFieldType`s let a record carry uploaded files : **`FILE`** (a single file, optionally format-constrained) and **`ATTACHMENTS`** (many files, optional MIME allow-list + max count) — migration `20260728210000_add_data_model_file_fields`. A field value is a soft `StoredFile.id` reference (single) or `string[]` (many) in `DataRecord.data`, mirroring RELATION ONE/MANY, so validation, GIN indexing, and cells reuse that path ([field-types.ts](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/contracts/field-types.ts), [indexing.ts](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/server/indexing.ts)). It builds entirely on the existing `@monark/files` service : a new client widget ([inputs/file-field.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/fields/inputs/file-field.tsx)) uploads via the `useFileUpload` signed-URL flow and shows name / download / remove chips ; `files.byIds` (new query) hydrates chips and `files.downloadUrl` serves them ([files/server/index.ts](monark/app/packages/files/src/server/index.ts)). **Server-side reference validation** on record write resolves the referenced files and rejects any that aren't a READY file in the caller's org, of an allowed MIME type (exact or `image/*` wildcard), within the size cap, or over the count cap ([data.ts](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/server/data.ts) `validateFileReferences`) — a client can't stash arbitrary/cross-org ids. A single private bucket `data-model-files` is auto-provisioned at boot (`ensureDataModelFilesBucket`, best-effort in [server.ts](monark/app/services/api/src/server.ts)). Admin field editor gains the two types + a config block (allowed MIME types, max size MB, max count) ([field-editor-dialog.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/data-models/[id]/field-editor-dialog.tsx>)) ; new `fields.{uploadFile,uploading,downloadFile,badFileFormat,fileTooLarge}` + `fieldDialog.types.{FILE,ATTACHMENTS}` + `config.{allowedFormats*,maxSizeMb}` i18n (en + fr). `@monark/data-models` gained a core→core dep on `@monark/files`. Covered by a new integration suite ([file-fields.test.ts](monark/app/packages/data-models/tests/integration/file-fields.test.ts), 10 cases : valid FILE/ATTACHMENTS ; rejects non-existent / PENDING / cross-org / disallowed-MIME / oversize / over-count ; and validates on update). Deferred (per [spec](monark/app/docs/features-planning/phase-3/data-models-file-fields.md)) : record-scoped file access (org-gated in v1), orphan GC, "has attachment" filtering. `pnpm gen` + `typecheck` (db + data-models + files + api + web) + `eslint` + `check:tiers`/`check:modules`/`check:i18n` (1769 × 2) green ; migration + integration suite to run with `db:migrate:dev` + `test:integration` (Docker).
- 2026-07-28: Notifications — **fixed cross-module notification-kind augmentations never merging** (a latent type-safety hole). Extended modules type their `notify()` payloads by augmenting `declare module "@monark/notifications/contracts" { interface NotificationDataRegistry { … } }` — but that interface was _declared_ in `contracts/registry.ts` and only _re-exported_ by the `contracts/index.ts` barrel (which the specifier resolves to). Augmenting a re-exporter didn't merge into the interface `NotificationKind = keyof NotificationDataMap` reads, so **no** external module's kinds were type-registered ; it stayed invisible only because no external kind had a string-literal `notify()` call site until calendar's reminder + kanban's assignment subscriber. Fix : the augmentable `NotificationDataRegistry` (+ `NotificationDataMap` / `NotificationKind`) now lives at the barrel [contracts/index.ts](monark/app/packages/notifications/src/contracts/index.ts) — the declaration site the public specifier resolves to — `extends` a `CoreNotificationKinds` interface kept in [registry.ts](monark/app/packages/notifications/src/contracts/registry.ts) ; existing external augmentations merge with **zero changes** to automation/calendar/kanban/data-models. Verified via type-probe that all four modules' kinds now land in `NotificationKind` ; the email-shell fixture was scoped to `CoreNotificationKinds` (it renders only core kinds ; other modules' kinds enter the type transitively via the DomainEvent union). Notifications typecheck + lint + **130 tests** green ; the api's two `NotificationKind` errors (calendar/kanban) are resolved.

- 2026-07-28: Automation — **Scheduled (cron-like) trigger**. A new `Schedule` trigger node runs a flow on a recurring schedule : **interval** ("every 20 minutes"), **daily** ("every day at 09:00"), **weekly** ("every 2 weeks on Wed"), **monthly by date** (a day-of-month, or the last day), and **monthly by weekday** ("the second Wednesday at 08:00"). **Backend is UTC** — a library-free, DST-proof `nextFireTime(schedule, after)` engine ([contracts/schedule.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/contracts/schedule.ts), `AutomationSchedule` zod union) — with an indexed `Automation.scheduleNextRunAt` column ([base.prisma](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/base.prisma), migration `20260728135512_automation_schedule`) kept in sync on every save/enable (`syncScheduleNextRunAt`, idempotent for an unchanged future schedule). A **scheduler** ([scheduler.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/scheduler.ts) `runDueSchedules`) claims due rows, enqueues a `PENDING` run the existing worker drains, and advances to the next fire via an atomic compare-and-set (`claimScheduledFire`) — so a fire enqueues **exactly one** run across overlapping ticks / worker processes. Driven by the worker loop ([worker.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/worker.ts)) plus a `/cron/run-automation-schedules` fallback endpoint ([services/api server.ts](monark/app/services/api/src/server.ts)). The trigger stores the bus-ignored `automation.schedule` sentinel (like manual/HTTP), and exposes `{{ trigger.scheduledFor }}` / `{{ trigger.firedAt }}`. **Editor**: a new `schedule` config-field type renders a [`ScheduleBuilder`](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/schedule-builder.tsx>) (kind picker + kind-specific controls + a live "next run" preview) that works in the **viewer's local timezone**, converting to/from UTC at the storage boundary (rolling the weekday across midnight so it stays correct) — new `automation.editor.schedule.*` i18n (en + fr). 5 schedule-engine unit tests (all four example patterns + day-of-month edges) + a scheduler integration test (enable sets next-run, scheduler enqueues + advances exactly once, run executes) — **23/23** integration + engine unit green ; `pnpm gen` + automation/web/api `typecheck` + `lint` + `check:i18n` (1758 × 2) green. Follow-up : a per-user timezone preference (the editor uses the browser timezone today) and an anchor picker for "every N weeks/days".

- 2026-07-28: Kanban — the **card editor adopts the house `DirtyFormBar`** save affordance. It hand-rolled a bordered footer (delete far-left, Cancel/Save right) with an always-enabled Save and Cancel-closes-panel ; now it tracks dirty state against the loaded card and shows the shared dirty-gated bar anchored to the panel bottom (`containment="container"`) — Save appears only when something changed, **Cancel reverts** to the loaded values (leave via the header X / Escape), matching the Data Models detail-panel editor. Delete moved into a `DangerCard`/`DangerRow` at the bottom of the form ([card-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/card-editor.tsx>) ; new `card.dangerTitle` + `card.deleteRowDescription` i18n, reuses `common.unsavedChanges`). The compact 2-column field rows were **kept** (not migrated to `FieldRow`) — the editor already composes shared field primitives, and `FieldRow`'s label-left layout would have regressed that density for no real gain. `tsc` + `eslint` + `check:i18n` (1738 × 2) green.

- 2026-07-28: Automation — the node inspector's left-edge resize handle now uses the shared **`DragHandle`** chip (`orientation="vertical"`, `active` while dragging, `highlight`), matching the detail-panel and table-column resize edges instead of a hand-rolled `cursor-col-resize` div ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>) ; added a `resizing` state to drive the grip's reveal, dropped the now-unused `resizeInspector` i18n key). Note: the kanban column drag grip was **left as-is** — it's a header drag _icon_ (a keyboard-operable dnd-kit sortable button), a different affordance from `DragHandle`'s pointer-only resize-edge pill, so reusing the chip there would look wrong and regress keyboard reordering. `tsc` + `eslint` + `check:i18n` green.

- 2026-07-28: Secrets — new **`@monark/secrets` core module** : a per-org **encrypted `key → value` store** ("environment variables") that lets automations + future integration modules reach external systems securely. **Crypto**: generalized the TOTP AES-256-GCM helper into a shared [`@monark/common/crypto`](monark/app/packages/common/src/crypto.ts) (`encrypt`/`decrypt`/`loadEncryptionKey`, key passed in) ; `@monark/auth` now delegates to it (no behavior change). Values are encrypted at rest keyed by a **separate** `SECRETS_ENCRYPTION_KEY` (loaded lazily / fail-closed) so its blast radius is independent of TOTP. **Write-only by design**: the plaintext never crosses tRPC — there is **no read-value procedure at all** ; `secrets.adminList` returns names + metadata, `adminSet` (create, or update value/description) and `adminDelete` are the only writes, all gated by `secrets.{read,manage}` and emitting `secrets.{created,updated,deleted}` (org + key, **never the value**). Model [`Secret`](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/base.prisma) (core, migration `20260728130000_add_secrets`), per-org isolation via the `(organizationId, key)` unique. **Automation integration**: `ctx.getSecret(name)` on [`NodeExecutionContext`](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/registry.ts) resolves the running org's secret server-side (stamps `lastUsedAt`, never logged/persisted), wired in [engine.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/engine.ts) ; a new `secret` config-field type stores the secret's **name** (a reference), and the editor renders a name picker (`automation.secrets.list`, gated on `automation.view`) with an empty-state link to `/admin/secrets` — so plaintext never reaches the editor or a persisted run step. **Shared safe fetch**: extracted the copy-pasted outbound-URL guard into [`@monark/common/http`](monark/app/packages/common/src/http.ts) (`assertOutboundUrlSafe` + `safeFetch` with an abort timeout) ; the automation webhook node and the webhooks module now share it (two private copies deleted). **Web**: a write-only admin screen [`/admin/secrets`](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/secrets>) (list + create/edit panel via `DirtyFormBar`, masked value, delete in a `DangerCard`+`ConfirmDialog`, `secrets.manage`-gated actions) registered in `admin-tabs.ts`, en/fr i18n, plus an `admin-secret-panel` screenshot story. Notifications + feature-flag consciously **N/A** (foundational, permission-gated infra). **Docs**: module README, [technical-documentation/secrets.md](monark/app/docs/technical-documentation/secrets.md), an automation-doc note, and a note on [social-automation.md](monark/app/docs/features-planning/social-automation.md) resolving its old tier-boundary contradiction (token storage now sits on this primitive). **Tests**: 11 crypto + 6 http-guard unit ; secrets data-layer integration (round-trip, per-org isolation, the safe projection never leaks a value, description-only update, `lastUsedAt`, delete) ; automation engine integration (a node reads a secret via `ctx.getSecret`, the run step persists the **name** not the value, missing → null). `pnpm gen` + secrets/automation/webhooks/common `typecheck` + `check:i18n` (1737 × 2) + `check:tiers` + common unit (60) green. Key rotation (versioned keys) + DNS-rebinding SSRF hardening are deferred.

- 2026-07-28: Files — new **`@monark/files` core module** : a generic file-upload service + bucket creation, backed by Supabase Storage. **Signed-upload flow** (bytes never touch the API) : `files.createUpload` validates the bucket policy (size / MIME), writes a `PENDING` [`StoredFile`](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/base.prisma), and mints a one-shot signed upload URL ; the browser uploads directly via [`useFileUpload`](monark/app/services/web/src/hooks/use-file-upload.ts) (`uploadToSignedUrl`) ; `files.finalize` confirms + flips to `READY` and emits `files.file-uploaded`. Buckets (`FileBucket`, project-level infra) are created private via `buckets.create` ; files are **org-scoped by key prefix** (`{organizationId}/{fileId}-{name}`). A swappable [`FileStorage`](monark/app/packages/files/src/server/storage.ts) port (Supabase impl + `setFileStorageForTesting`) keeps the router backend-agnostic + testable. Big-5 wired : RBAC (`files.{view,upload,delete,manage-buckets}`), events (`files.{bucket-created,file-uploaded,file-deleted}`), webhooks (free), feature flag `files.enabled` ; notifications N/A. **Web**: an admin page [`/admin/files`](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/files>) (bucket picker + New-bucket dialog + upload + a `DataTable` with Download/Delete) registered in `admin-tabs.ts`, en/fr i18n. **Tests**: 6 unit (`sanitizeFilename`/`buildObjectKey`) + 11 integration (data layer + a router suite via `t.createCallerFactory` with a fake storage : RBAC, over-size/wrong-MIME rejection, finalize→READY, event emission). Core tier (so extended modules can consume it later — first target is Data-Models file attachments, deferred). Models live in `base.prisma` (core), migration `20260728120000_add_files`. `pnpm gen` + files/api `typecheck` + `lint` + `check:i18n` (1697 × 2) + files unit (6) + integration (11) green.

- 2026-07-28: DB / tooling — **per-package Prisma schema fragments** for extended modules. The extended modules `@monark/calendar` and `@monark/kanban` no longer define their models inside the core `schema.prisma` (which had quietly violated the "extended modules don't own core schema" boundary) ; each now owns its own fragment ([calendar](monark/app/packages/calendar/prisma/calendar.prisma), [kanban](monark/app/packages/kanban/prisma/kanban.prisma)) under its `// ── MODULE ──` banner. `prisma/schema.prisma` became a **generated, committed, drift-checked artifact**, assembled by a new `pnpm gen:schema` ([tools/gen-schema.ts](monark/app/tools/gen-schema.ts)) from [`base.prisma`](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/base.prisma) (datasource + generator + all core models) plus every `packages/<module>/prisma/*.prisma` fragment — same codegen-drift discipline as the `DomainEvent` union and the tRPC router (wired into `pnpm gen`). Because Prisma/`postinstall`/the testcontainer suite all keep reading the committed `schema.prisma` unchanged, install/CI/migrations are untouched (verified: `prisma validate` + `prisma generate` + db/calendar `typecheck` clean, and the assembled schema is byte-identical model-wise, so no migration drift). `check:tiers` gained a third rule — an extended-module banner in `base.prisma` is now a hard failure — and the docs (CLAUDE.md codegen + boundaries, [@monark/db README](monark/app/packages/db/README.md)) were updated. Scope was deliberately limited to the two extended modules (keeping their FK relations to `Organization`/`Role` intact) ; fragmenting the core modules and soft-FK-decoupling core↔extended are tracked in [backlog.md](monark/app/docs/todo/backlog.md). **Kanban a11y/primitives**: the board switcher no longer nests a `<button>` (edit pen) inside a `role="button"` — the trigger is a real `<button>` and the pen is a sibling in one bordered pill ([kanban-shell.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/kanban-shell.tsx>)) ; the `AddColumn` inline editor swapped its raw `<input>`/`<button>`s for the shared `Input`/`Button` and gained an `aria-label` ; the card is now keyboard-openable — `SortableCard` composes dnd-kit's key handler so **Enter opens** while **Space** still starts a keyboard drag ([kanban-board-view.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/kanban-board-view.tsx>)) ; the assignee-picker `MemberAvatar` reuses the shared `Avatar` ([card-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/card-editor.tsx>)) ; and a couple of `vw`/`vh` menu bounds moved to `dvh`/`dvw`. **Automation list**: added `useDataTableLayout` + `TableTools` (Sorting in the toolbar cluster, a Columns editor next to the CTA) with real `sortAccessor`s on every column, matching the Data Models list ([automations-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/automations-list.tsx>) ; new shared `table.close` i18n key). **Automation global search**: added an `AutomationSearchProvider` + a gated `automation.automations.search` procedure (org-scoped, `automation.view`, name match → `{id,name}`) registered in [search-providers.ts](monark/app/services/web/src/components/global-search/search-providers.ts), so automations are jump-to-able in the command palette (new `globalSearch.groups.automation` i18n). `pnpm gen` + automation `typecheck` + `eslint` + `check:i18n` (1656 × 2) green. (Two unrelated pre-existing `tsc` errors remain in `packages/kanban/src/server/notification-subscriber.ts` and `packages/secrets/src/server/index.ts` — in-progress work outside this audit.)

- 2026-07-28: Modularity audit fixes (three items). **(1) `check:tiers` now scans real imports**, not just `package.json` — it walks each extended module's `src/` and flags any import of another extended module, closing the blind spot where an undeclared/transitive coupling would pass ([tools/check-tiers.ts](monark/app/tools/check-tiers.ts)). **(2) Reconciled the `schema.prisma` rule with reality** — the docs said extended modules "MUST NOT modify" the schema, but `@monark/calendar` and `@monark/kanban` legitimately own banner-scoped tables there ; the rule now reads "must not reshape core/other modules' tables ; may add your own under a `// ── MODULE ──` banner, sidecar for lightweight data" ([extensibility-contract.md](monark/app/docs/technical-documentation/extensibility-contract.md), [CLAUDE.md](monark/app/CLAUDE.md)). **(3) Closed the calendar domain-event gap** — added [contracts/events.ts](monark/app/packages/calendar/src/contracts/events.ts) (`calendar.created/updated/deleted` + `calendar.event-created/updated/deleted`), registered them with payload-field metadata ([server/event-types.ts](monark/app/packages/calendar/src/server/event-types.ts)), emit them best-effort from every calendar/event mutation ([server/index.ts](monark/app/packages/calendar/src/server/index.ts)), wired `registerCalendarEventTypes()` into [api boot](monark/app/services/api/src/server.ts), and removed the `@monark/calendar:events` `ACKNOWLEDGED_GAPS` entry ([tools/check-modules.ts](monark/app/tools/check-modules.ts)). Calendar events are now webhook-subscribable and appear as automation Event Trigger outputs. `pnpm gen` + calendar/common `typecheck` + `lint` + `check:tiers` + `check:modules` (0 gaps) green.

- 2026-07-27: Automation + Kanban — UI-audit quick fixes. **Automation**: the run-history step status rendered the raw enum ("SUCCEEDED"…) — now goes through the existing `stepStatusLabel(t, …)` so it's translated ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>)). **Kanban**: the board-dialog role-access list swapped its hand-rolled `<input type="checkbox">` for the shared `Checkbox` ([board-dialog.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/board-dialog.tsx>)) ; the board-switcher and column `DropdownMenu`s now pass `modal={false}` (they open over the scrollable board / non-modal card sheet — avoids the stuck `body{pointer-events:none}` freeze + mobile viewport blowout) ([kanban-shell.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/kanban-shell.tsx>), [kanban-board-view.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/kanban-board-view.tsx>)) ; and the card due-date label now formats via next-intl's `useFormatter` (locale-aware month) instead of the browser-locale `toLocaleDateString`. `tsc` + `eslint` + `check:i18n` (1654 × 2) green.

- 2026-07-27: Automation — **fast, DB-free unit coverage for the tricky pure logic**, so the behavior we've been eyeballing is now pinned. Extracted the editor's run-visualization mapping into [run-viz.ts](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/run-viz.ts>) (`edgeRunState` + `flowOutDotClass`) and the manual-run placeholder builder into [sample-payload.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/sample-payload.ts) (`sampleTriggerPayload` + `sampleFieldValue`), both previously inlined and untestable. New suites: run-viz coloring incl. the delay-ordering + branch-gating rules ([tests/lib/automation-run-viz.test.ts](monark/app/services/web/tests/lib/automation-run-viz.test.ts), 12), engine `executionOrder` (topo order, cycle, reachability, value-source pull-in) + `interpolateConfig` ([tests/engine-unit.test.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/tests/engine-unit.test.ts), 10), the sample-payload fix ([tests/sample-payload.test.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/tests/sample-payload.test.ts), 5), and `eventFieldsFor` / `COMMON_EVENT_FIELDS` ([packages/common/tests/event-fields.test.ts](monark/app/packages/common/tests/event-fields.test.ts), 4). 31 new tests, all DB-free; complements the existing DB-backed `engine.test.ts` integration suite. `typecheck` + `lint` + all suites green.

- 2026-07-27: Kanban — **test coverage** for the recently-shipped card fields. New **unit** suite [`tests/parse-subtasks.test.ts`](monark/app/packages/kanban/tests/parse-subtasks.test.ts) (6 tests, no Docker, runs under `pnpm test`) pins `parseSubtasks` — the trusted-shape boundary for the subtask `Json` column : non-array → `[]`, malformed items dropped, strict `done` coercion, id/title length caps, `KANBAN_SUBTASK_MAX` cap. Extended the **integration** suite [`kanban-data.test.ts`](monark/app/packages/kanban/tests/integration/kanban-data.test.ts) (5 → 8 testcontainer tests) with multi-**reviewer** round-trips, an inline **subtask** checklist round-trip through the JSON column, the **status-change move** (`updateCard({ columnId })` appends to the target column), and a seeded-column-**colour** assertion. Removed the placeholder unit test. Also added a **tRPC router** integration suite [`kanban-router.test.ts`](monark/app/packages/kanban/tests/integration/kanban-router.test.ts) (11 tests) driving the real procedures via `t.createCallerFactory` : RBAC guards (`kanban.view`/`edit` deny/allow), per-board **role access** (`requireAccessibleBoard` — restricted board hidden from a viewer, visible to the gated role, bypassed by `kanban.manage`), and **event emission + change-detection** captured off the bus (`card-created` + per-assignee `card-assigned` ; an update's `changed` array + `card-assigned` only for newly-added assignees ; status change → `card-moved` ; single-field change → no false events). It mirrors data-models' `authorization.test.ts` and is **fully self-contained in the module** (public `createRole`/`assignRole` + `on(WILDCARD_EVENT_TYPE)` — no shared harness, no core-module change). `pnpm test` (6 unit) + `test:integration` (19 = 8 data + 11 router) + `lint` green.

- 2026-07-27: Automation — the node inspector is **full-screen on mobile**. It was a bottom sheet capped at `max-h-[85vh]`, leaving a dead gap above it ; switched to the sheet's `full` side variant (`100dvh`, fade-in, built-in top-right close X) so the config panel gets the whole height its stacked sections need ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>)). `tsc` + `eslint` green.

- 2026-07-27: Automation — a **Variables** section in the node inspector surfaces every `{{ ... }}` you can reference from the node you're editing, so you don't have to reopen the trigger or upstream nodes to recall them. For a non-trigger node it lists, grouped by source: the **trigger's** event fields (`{{ trigger.<field> }}`, plus `{{ trigger.body }}` for an HTTP trigger) and each **upstream** node's output — the whole output (`{{ <nodeId> }}`) always, and concrete keys (`{{ <nodeId>.<key> }}`) once a test run has produced them. Availability follows the graph: only ancestors (nodes with a directed path into the one you're editing, via `ancestorNodeIds`) contribute, so shown references are ones that actually resolve at run time. Each reference is click-to-copy (same pattern as the trigger's own Outputs section) ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>) `NodeVariables`, `variableGroups` memo). New `automation.editor` i18n (`variables`, `variablesHint`, `varWholeOutput`, `varTrigger`, `varBodyHint`, en + fr). `tsc` + `eslint` + `check:i18n` (1654 × 2) green.

- 2026-07-27: Kanban — subtask progress on the card face is now a **progress bar flush with the card's bottom edge** instead of a `done/total` chip ([kanban-card.tsx](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/client/ui/kanban-card.tsx) `SubtaskBar`). The bar is a normal-flow element (so the z-indexed avatar stack can't paint over it) and the card is `overflow-hidden` so it clips to the rounded corners. It fills proportionally in the **brand primary** (`var(--primary)`) while in progress and **green** at 100%, with the exact `done/total` on its `title`/`aria-label`. Card **avatars are left-aligned** (they lead the meta row instead of floating right). **Everything is inline `style`** — the negative-margin breakout, the top gap, the track/height, the fill height — because `packages/kanban/src` is not in the app's Tailwind `@source` scan, so any utility used only in the package (e.g. `-mx-3`, `mt-5`, `h-full`) is silently not generated in the real Next app ; that made the bar collapse/vanish on device even though the (separately-built) screenshot harness rendered it. `kanban-board` screenshot updated. `typecheck` (kanban + web) + `lint` green.

- 2026-07-27: Kanban — card editor field order: the **description + subtasks** now sit **below** the metadata rows ([card-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/card-editor.tsx>)). The form reads Title → Status/Estimate → Priority/Due → Assignees → Reviewers → Description → Subtasks, so the compact property rows are up top and the taller free-form content (WYSIWYG description, checklist) sits at the bottom. `kanban-card-panel` screenshot updated. `typecheck` (web) + `lint` green.

- 2026-07-27: Kanban — selecting one card after another no longer replays the card panel's open animation. `CardEditor` ([card-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/card-editor.tsx>)) was split into a stable `Sheet` shell + an inner `CardForm` keyed by card id : the shell stays mounted while the panel is open, so a card-to-card switch remounts only the form (re-seeding its fields) instead of the whole `Sheet`, which had been re-running the slide-in/fade and shifting layout each time (the shell used to force the remount with a `key` on the whole editor). First open still animates; close is unchanged. `typecheck` (web) + `lint` green.

- 2026-07-27: Kanban — cards gain an inline **subtasks checklist**, plus a touch of card polish. Each card owns an ordered `subtasks` list (`{ id, title, done }`), stored as a `Json` column on [`KanbanCard`](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/schema.prisma) (migration `20260727170000_kanban_card_subtasks`). The [card editor](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/card-editor.tsx>) grew a **Subtasks** section (checkbox + title + remove per row, "Add subtask", live `done/total` count), and the card face shows a **progress chip** (`done/total`, green once complete) next to the estimate ([kanban-card.tsx](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/client/ui/kanban-card.tsx)). Contract `KanbanSubtask` + a `parseSubtasks` validator ([contracts/types.ts](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/contracts/types.ts)) ; `cards.create` / `update` accept subtasks and the `card-updated.changed` union gained `subtasks`. **Two TS2589 workarounds** (documented in the dev doc): the recursive `Prisma.JsonValue` on the wide card row blew tRPC's _output_ inference, so `KanbanCardRow` overrides `subtasks` to `unknown` (coerced with `parseSubtasks` at every read) ; and a typed array in the _input_ hit the same wall, so subtasks travel as a JSON string parsed server-side. Also: a little more margin under the card **title**. New `card.subtask*` i18n (en + fr) ; `kanban-board` + `kanban-card-panel` screenshots updated. `pnpm gen` + `typecheck` (kanban + web + api) + `lint` + `check:i18n` (1649 × 2) + kanban integration (5) green.

- 2026-07-27: Kanban — clicking a card now opens the editor as a **side panel**, not a centered modal. On desktop `card-editor.tsx` ([card-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/card-editor.tsx>)) is a **non-modal right-side `Sheet`** (no dimming overlay, board stays visible/interactive behind it) with a shared `PanelHeader` (title + close X) over a scrollable form body and a pinned footer (delete-left, cancel/save-right) ; on mobile it becomes a **full-screen** sheet via the transform-free `full` variant. Outside clicks are ignored on desktop (close is X / Cancel / Escape) so a stray click can't drop the form. Extracted the iOS-safe **visual-viewport** mobile check into a shared [`use-panel-is-mobile`](monark/app/services/web/src/hooks/use-panel-is-mobile.ts) hook (used by both the card panel and `TableDetailLayout`, which dropped its private copy) — a media-query check would read a blown-out layout viewport as "desktop" and lock the off-screen slide variant in. New `kanban-card-panel` screenshot story (desktop panel + mobile full-screen). `typecheck` (web) + `lint` green.

- 2026-07-27: Automation — **right-click a node** to Rename / Replace / Delete, and node naming moved out of the inspector. The editor now handles `onNodeContextMenu` with a cursor-pinned popover ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>) `NodeContextMenu`, viewport-clamped, closes on outside-click / Escape / pane-click). **Rename** opens a small dialog (`RenameNodeDialog`) — the only place a node's name is edited now ; the inspector's Name field is gone, replaced by a read-only name + type line. **Replace** opens the node palette filtered to the same **kind** (so a flow keeps exactly one trigger), then a **confirm dialog** before swapping: the node keeps its id + position, resets config/name, and **prunes only the connections the new node can't carry** (`edgeSurvivesReplace` — a `field:` input whose key is gone, a dropped branch/error output) while keeping compatible wires, whose surviving `field:` targets stay exposed. **Delete** removes the node and its edges. Factored the add/replace node-data build into one `descriptorToNodeData`. New `automation.editor` i18n (`menu*`, `rename*`, `replace*`, `cancel`, en + fr). `tsc` + `eslint` + `check:i18n` (1644 × 2) green.

- 2026-07-27: Automation — **Manual** and **HTTP** trigger nodes (loops deferred). Trigger matching happens at enqueue time by comparing `Automation.triggerEventType` to the event type, and the subscriber ignores `automation.*` events — so both new triggers store a **bus-ignored sentinel** event type ([contracts/triggers.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/contracts/triggers.ts): `automation.manual` / `automation.http`) and never fire from a platform event ; no schema migration. The server derives that sentinel from the graph's trigger node on save (`resolveTriggerEventType`, [router.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/router.ts)). **Manual trigger** ([nodes/manual-trigger.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/nodes/manual-trigger.ts)) runs only via Run now. **HTTP trigger** ([nodes/http-trigger.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/nodes/http-trigger.ts)) is fired by an inbound `POST /hooks/automation/:id` — a new Express route on the API service ([services/api/src/server.ts](monark/app/services/api/src/server.ts)) mirroring the cron-token pattern, but auth is a **per-automation secret** verified in constant time ([http-trigger.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/http-trigger.ts) `handleHttpTrigger`); the request body becomes the trigger payload (`{{ trigger.body.* }}`). The secret is generated server-side via a new `automations.newHttpSecret` mutation and shown, with the URL, in a dedicated editor section ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>)); the node face drops the "no event" line for triggers that have no event to pick. New `automation.editor.http*` i18n (en + fr). 3 new integration tests (manual run, HTTP right/wrong secret + body exposure, disabled-automation reject) — **20/20** integration + 9 validator unit ; `typecheck` (web + automation + api) + `lint` + `check:i18n` (1635 keys × 2) green.

- 2026-07-27: Automation — **manual test runs now seed sample trigger data**, so a `{{ trigger.<field> }}` reference resolves during a "Run now" instead of coming back `undefined`. Previously the synthetic manual payload carried only the base envelope (`type` / `organizationId` / `actorId` / `occurredAt`), so wiring e.g. `{{ trigger.userId }}` (from a `totp.disabled` trigger) into Send Email's subject produced an empty value that failed the node's `subject: z.string().min(1)` check — a correctly-wired flow looked broken on test. `runNow` now builds the payload from the event type's declared `fields` via `sampleTriggerPayload` / `sampleFieldValue`, one placeholder per field (email-shaped keys get a valid `sample@example.com` so email-validated inputs still pass), with the base envelope authoritative ([router.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/router.ts)). Real event-driven runs are unchanged; they carry the actual payload. `typecheck` green.

- 2026-07-27: Automation / Events — **event triggers now advertise their payload fields**, so a flow author sees exactly what information a triggering event carries (who, when, which record …) instead of guessing at `{{ trigger.* }}` paths. The domain-event registry gained an optional per-event `fields` metadata list (`{ key, type, description }`) alongside its description, plus `COMMON_EVENT_FIELDS` (`occurredAt` / `type`) and `eventFieldsFor(type)` that merges the two ([event-registry.ts](monark/app/packages/common/src/event-registry.ts)). Every core module's `server/event-types.ts` (auth, users, feature-flags, organizations, rbac, notifications, data-models, automation, kanban) now declares its events' fields — e.g. `totp.disabled` exposes `userId` + `triggeredBy`. The automation `eventTypes.list` procedure returns the merged fields ([router.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/router.ts)), and the editor's Event Trigger inspector renders a new **Outputs** section listing each field with its type, description, and a click-to-copy `{{ trigger.<key> }}` token ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>) `TriggerOutputs`).
