Metadata only ; it doesn't validate emitted payloads. `pnpm gen` + `typecheck` + `lint` + `check:i18n` green ; new en/fr keys under `automation.editor.outputs*`.

- 2026-07-27: Kanban — **long-press to drag on touch**, so mobile users can pan the board without grabbing a card. The board's one `DndContext` swapped its single `PointerSensor` for an input-aware pair ([kanban-board-view.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/kanban-board-view.tsx>)): a `MouseSensor` (6px activation, unchanged desktop feel) plus a `TouchSensor` with a **250ms delay + 8px tolerance** — a quick swipe scrolls, a held press starts the drag. Paired with dropping `touch-action: none` from the card ([kanban-card.tsx](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/client/ui/kanban-card.tsx)) and the column grip so the browser keeps the pan/scroll gesture until the long-press delay elapses. Fixes accidental card moves when trying to scroll the board on narrow screens. `typecheck` (web + kanban) + `lint` green.

- 2026-07-27: Automation — flow outputs (On success / On error, branch outs) stay in the node **header**, not the body. Enabling a node's error output used to push _both_ its outputs down into the body's port list (the header only kept a lone output) ; now the header owns the whole control-flow spine — flow-in on the left, and the flow outputs on the right as a bare continue handle (single output) or stacked labelled rows (On success grey / On error red, or a branch node's named outs). The body is reserved for **data** ports: input variables on the left, with the right column held for future data-output ports ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>) `FlowNode` ; `hasBody` no longer keys off output count). No new i18n (reuses `outDefault` / `errorPort`). `tsc` + `eslint` green.

- 2026-07-27: Kanban — **multiple reviewers**, a **colour dot on the status picker**, and estimate-first card meta. (1) A card can now have **many reviewers**: replaced the single nullable `reviewerId` with an order-preserving `reviewerIds` text array on [`KanbanCard`](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/schema.prisma) (migration `20260727150000_kanban_card_multi_reviewer`, backfilling any existing single reviewer into a one-element array), threaded through the contract types, data layer, and the `cards.create` / `update` inputs ([data.ts](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/server/data.ts), [index.ts](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/server/index.ts) — deduped, capped at 20 ; the `reviewer` changed-flag now diffs the set order-insensitively). The [card editor](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/card-editor.tsx>) reviewer field became a `MultiSelect` of org members (avatar chips + typeahead), mirroring assignees ; new `card.reviewer{sLabel,Placeholder,Add,Remove}` i18n replacing the single-select `reviewerLabel`/`noReviewer`, en + fr. (2) The editor's **Status** select now shows each column's **colour dot** beside its name (the picker took the columns' `color`). (3) On the card, the **estimate chip renders first** in the meta row (before the priority badge) ([kanban-card.tsx](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/client/ui/kanban-card.tsx)). `pnpm gen` + `typecheck` (kanban + web + api) + `lint` + `check:i18n` (1623 × 2) + kanban integration (5, migration applied to a fresh testcontainer) green ; `kanban-board` screenshot updated.

- 2026-07-27: Kanban — priority is now a **faded colour badge** and the card form's meta fields regrouped. The priority indicator dropped its colour dot for a tinted-background + solid-colour-text badge (the priority hex at ~15% alpha behind full-colour text), matching the Data-Models select badges, on both the card ([kanban-card.tsx](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/client/ui/kanban-card.tsx) `PriorityPill`) and the editor's priority picker ([card-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/card-editor.tsx>)) ; colours stay inline hex since the package isn't in the app's Tailwind `@source` scan. The card editor's meta fields regrouped into two two-column rows — **Status + Estimate**, then **Priority + Due date** — sitting above **Assignees** and **Reviewer** (was Status / Assignees / Reviewer full-width, then Priority+Estimate, then Due). `typecheck` (web + kanban) + `lint` green ; `kanban-board` screenshot updated.

- 2026-07-27: Automation — **per-node run logs**, **resolved wired-input values**, and **stateful edges** in the editor. (1) Nodes can now narrate a run: the execution context gained `ctx.log(message, level?)` ([registry.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/registry.ts)), the engine buffers the lines per step (capped, `warn` truncation notice) and persists them on every finish path ([engine.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/engine.ts)) into a new `AutomationRunStep.logs` JSON column ([schema.prisma](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/schema.prisma) + migration `20260727143531_automation_step_logs`). Built-in nodes (Send Email, Data create/update/delete/find, RBAC assign/remove) emit useful lines. The selected node gets its own collapsible **Logs** section in the inspector (timestamp + level color), separate from the whole-run execution-log dock. (2) A **wired** config field used to hide its editor entirely ; once a test run resolves it, the field now shows the actual value read-only/disabled (from the step's resolved `input`), so you can see what flowed into the connection ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>) `ResolvedValueField`). (3) Edges now reflect run state — idle at rest (unchanged), then as a test run reveals: **pending** (grey dashed march) ahead of the frontier, **pulling** (amber dashed march) into the active node, **transmitted** (green solid) once source→target both succeeded, and **error** (red solid) touching any failed node (`edgeRunState` + a CSS dash keyframe, honoring `prefers-reduced-motion`). Shared `AutomationRunStepLog` contract ([contracts/run.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/contracts/run.ts)) ; new `automation.editor` i18n (`logs`, `resolvedFromRun`, en + fr). New engine integration test proves `ctx.log` lines persist in order with levels (17/17 integration) ; `typecheck` (web + api + automation) + `lint` + `check:i18n` (1621 keys × 2) green.

- 2026-07-27: Automation — per-node **error output** (a catch branch), opt-in under a node's Advanced settings. A node with its error output enabled ([contracts/graph.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/contracts/graph.ts) new optional `errorOutput` on the node instance ; no migration, it's the JSON graph) grows a second, red **"On error"** output ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>) `FlowNode` + `PortRow` error variant). When such a node throws, the engine ([engine.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/engine.ts)) now **catches** it: the step is still recorded FAILED (visible in history), but instead of aborting the run it exposes `{ error }` as the node's output, activates the `error` handle, and continues down the error branch — so you can wire failure handling (notify, fallback, etc.). A node without the error output aborts the run as before. Resume-after-delay was updated to treat a caught failure like a success (its error output re-activates on resume). The toggle lives in a new **Advanced** collapsible in the config panel (hidden by default ; triggers don't get one). New `automation.editor` i18n (en + fr: `advanced`, `errorOutputLabel`, `errorPort`, …). New integration test proves a denied node's failure routes to a downstream node and the run SUCCEEDS (16/16 integration + 9 validator unit) ; `typecheck` (web + automation) + `lint` + `check:i18n` (1617 keys × 2) green.

- 2026-07-27: Automation editor — the Inspector's **Configuration** section is now collapsible and **Advanced** is a lighter disclosure. Config fields sit in a primary expandable (the full-width bar + caret, open by default) so a node with many fields can be folded away ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>) `ConfigPanel`). Advanced (the error-output toggle) dropped its full-width bar for a **secondary** style — just underline-on-hover text and a small caret — so it reads as a minor, tucked-away option rather than a peer section. No new i18n. `typecheck` (web) + `lint` green.
- 2026-07-27: Automation editor — nodes now lead with their **icon**. On the canvas, each node's face shows its icon next to the title and drops the redundant type-name / category subtitle ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>) `FlowNode` — icon carried through `FlowNodeData.icon` from the descriptor, resolved via `nodeIcon`) ; the trigger keeps its chosen-event subtitle. The Inspector's node section leads with the same icon in a small tile beside the type label. Reuses the icon resolver from the add-node picker (descriptor Lucide-name → component, category fallback), so the graph reads by shape at a glance. `typecheck` (web) + `lint` green ; no i18n change.

- 2026-07-27: Automation — hid the redundant close **X** on the add-node palette modal ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>) `AddNodeDialog`, `hideClose` on `DialogContent`). The dialog opens straight onto a full-width search row, so the default top-right X overlapped it and read as off-centre inside the field ; Escape and click-outside still dismiss the palette.
- 2026-07-27: Automation — pre-flight (pre-run) validation, shared by the editor and the server. A new pure, descriptor-driven validator ([contracts/validate.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/contracts/validate.ts) `validateGraph`) checks a graph before it runs: a **required input** that's neither filled nor wired, **no trigger** / more than one, a **trigger with no event** (satisfied by the node's `event-type` field in the editor or the automation's stored `triggerEventType` on the server), an **orphan** node not reachable from the trigger (warning), an unknown node type, and a **cycle** (error today; a comment marks where deliberate loops will be exempted once loop nodes with exit conditions land). Because it's serializable it runs both places: the editor imports it (via the `@monark/automation/contracts` path-mapped source) to show a live **"N issues"** toolbar dropdown — each row jumps to its node — mark the offending config field with a red asterisk, and **disable Run while there are errors** ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>)) ; the server runs the same check in `runNow` ([router.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/router.ts) `assertRunnable`) and rejects a misconfigured graph up front with an itemized `ValidationError` (400) instead of letting a node blow up mid-run. Warnings (orphans) inform but never block. New `automation.editor.preflight.*` i18n (en + fr) ; 9 validator unit tests + the existing 15 integration tests green ; `typecheck` (web + automation) + `lint` + `check:i18n` (1611 keys × 2) green.

- 2026-07-27: Kanban — a card's **description is now a rich-text (WYSIWYG) editor** instead of a plain `<textarea>`. The card editor ([card-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/card-editor.tsx>)) reuses the shared TipTap [`RichTextEditor`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/fields/inputs/rich-text-editor.tsx) from the fields toolkit (headings, bold/italic/strike/code, lists, blockquote, links) with its toolbar strings from `useFieldStrings().labels.richText`, so no new i18n. The value is sanitized HTML (same format the Data Models `richText` fields store) ; existing plain-text descriptions load fine (rendered as a paragraph). The card `description` input caps on [`cards.create` / `cards.update`](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/server/index.ts) were raised 2000 → 20000 chars to fit the HTML markup. No schema change (the column stays `String`) ; the card face doesn't render the description, so there's no read view to update. `tsc` (web + kanban) + `eslint` green. A new board's six seeded columns now carry a progression palette (grey Backlog → blue Todo → amber In Progress → purple Review → cyan QA → green Done) so the header dots read as a pipeline out of the box instead of all grey. `DEFAULT_COLUMNS` (name + colour) in [contracts/types.ts](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/contracts/types.ts) is the single source of truth (`DEFAULT_COLUMN_NAMES` derives from it) and `createBoard` ([data.ts](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/server/data.ts)) seeds the colour alongside the name; the `BoardColumn` header dot already rendered `color`. Existing boards keep their columns' current colour (recolour per-column via the header `…` → Edit). Header layout is now **grip → colour dot → name** ([board-column.tsx](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/client/ui/board-column.tsx)): the reorder grip sits at the far left but **collapses its own width when hidden** ([kanban-board-view.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/kanban-board-view.tsx>)), so at rest the colour dot reads as flush-left and the grip reveals to its left on hover / touch without leaving a reserved gap. The board's **loading skeleton** was rebuilt to match the flat column style (full-height, no-gap columns with divider borders + an underlined dot-and-name header, replacing the old raised/rounded/gapped placeholder) so nothing shifts when the board loads; a `kanban-skeleton` screenshot story covers it.

- 2026-07-27: Automation editor — a two-pane add-node picker and a 3-section Inspector. **Add node** is now a rich modal ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>) `AddNodeDialog`): a **category sidebar** (icon + count) on the left, the category's **nodes** (icon + label + short description) on the right, and a **search** on top that filters across all categories (Enter adds the first match). Each node shows an icon resolved from its descriptor's Lucide icon name (aliased / category-fallback via `nodeIcon` / `categoryIcon`), each category its own icon — scales better than the old dropdown now that there are 20+ nodes. Opens from the toolbar button **or the `N` shortcut** (a canvas keydown guarded against typing in inputs). **Inspector reorganized into 3 sections** — Node identity (type + name + delete), Configuration (fields, under a heading), and Last run — separated by full-width dividers ; the **Last run** collapsible now spans the **full panel width** (breaks out of the panel padding with a tinted body) instead of sitting as an embedded card. New `automation.editor` i18n (`configuration`, `addNodeSearch`, `addNodeEmpty`), en + fr. `typecheck` (web) + `lint` + `check:i18n` green.

- 2026-07-27: Automation editor — the Inspector is now resizable, closeable, and opens on selection. **Opens on node selection** and closes with an ✕ (deselects) ; the desktop panel only mounts when a node is selected, so the canvas keeps full width otherwise ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>)). **Resizable**: drag its left edge to set the width (clamped 288–640px, persisted in `localStorage`) ; the body is an `@container`, so — matching the app's `FieldRow` pattern — config-field **labels flip to the left of their control** once the panel is wide enough (`@md`) and stack when narrow. **"Last run" moved below config** into a **collapsible** section (auto-open on failure / skip) with a colored status icon next to the title — red `AlertCircle` on error, amber `AlertTriangle` on skip. New `automation.editor` i18n (`closeInspector`, `resizeInspector`, `runNoDetail`), en + fr. `typecheck` (web) + `lint` + `check:i18n` green.

- 2026-07-27: Automation editor — the config panel is now an **Inspector** that shows a node's run result. Renamed the right-hand panel (`t("config")` → `t("inspector")`) and, after a test run, selecting a node shows a **"Last run"** section — its status badge + duration, any error, and the actual **Input** and **Output** as scrollable JSON ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>): `RunResult` + `JsonBlock`, fed the selected node's step from the visualized run). Clears when the execution-log overlay is closed. New `automation.editor` i18n (`inspector`, `runResult`, `runInput`, `runOutput`, `statusSkipped`), en + fr. `typecheck` (web) + `lint` + `check:i18n` green.

- 2026-07-27: Automation — value-source nodes no longer need a flow-in wire. A **Constant** / **Transform** is a pure value source with no real input dependency, so it shouldn't have to be threaded into the execution sequence just to run. They now declare `inputs: []` and render **no control-flow input handle** ([constant.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/nodes/constant.ts), [transform.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/nodes/transform.ts) ; the editor's `FlowNode` only draws the flow-in for nodes that have control inputs, [automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>)). The engine's `executionOrder` ([engine.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/engine.ts)) now **pulls a data-source into the run automatically**: any node feeding a `field:` port of a reachable node is added to the execution set and ordered before its consumer, even with no control edge into it — so wiring a Constant's output into a downstream field is enough to make it run. Integration spec updated to a source Constant with no control edge feeding two fields (15/15 against a testcontainer). `typecheck` (web + automation) + `lint` green.

- 2026-07-27: Automation — Play tests the current canvas, and a Constant fans out cleanly. Two fixes to "I wired a value into several fields but only one arrived". (1) **Play now saves first**: Run now executes the _saved_ automation, so pending canvas edits (just-added nodes / wires) were invisible to a test — `handleTestRun` ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>)) persists the graph (when dirty) before running, so the test always reflects what's on screen. (2) **Wired values unwrap**: feeding a node whose output is a single `{ value }` (the Constant / Transform convention) into a config field now passes the value, not the wrapper object — `unwrapLinkedValue` in [engine.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/engine.ts) (multi-key outputs like `{ records }` still pass whole ; use `{{ nodeId.key }}` to pull a scalar from those). One source wired into many fields already resolved correctly per-field — added an integration spec proving a Constant into two fields feeds both the unwrapped scalar (15/15 against a testcontainer). `typecheck` (web + automation) + `lint` green.

- 2026-07-27: Automation editor — live test-run visualization + execution log. Pressing **Play** (Run now) now animates the flow: the editor polls the run ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>), `runs.getById` with a status-gated `refetchInterval`) and **reveals its steps one at a time** (~450ms each, up to the steps that have arrived) so even a run that finished in a single poll walks through step-by-step. Nodes light up by status via a `RunVizContext` the custom node reads — amber pulse for the currently-executing node, emerald for succeeded, red for failed, dimmed for skipped — and the edge into the active node glows amber (`AutomationEdge`). A docked **Execution log** panel (`RunLogPanel`) at the bottom of the canvas streams each step (status dot, node name, duration, output / error, auto-scrolled), shows the run status (Running / Succeeded / Failed / Waiting for a delay), and closes to clear the overlay. Polling keeps running while the run is `PENDING`/`RUNNING`, so a Delay's suspend→resume is reflected live. New `automation.editor` i18n (`executionLog`, `statusRunning/Succeeded/Failed`, `runWaiting`, `closeLog`), en + fr. `typecheck` (web) + `lint` + `check:i18n` green.

- 2026-07-27: Automation editor — opt-in variable inputs, node naming, and an unsaved-changes guard. **Controlled vs uncontrolled inputs**: a config field is no longer always a port ; each field in the config panel has a "use as input" toggle ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>) `ConfigField` cable button) that exposes it as a labelled input port on the node face. Off by default (fields flagged `linkable` start on, for continuity), so nodes stay clean ; the typed value is the default and a wired connection overrides it at run time (unwiring falls back to the typed value ; toggling a field off also drops its edge). Exposure persists per-node in the graph JSON (new optional `inputs: string[]` on the node instance, [contracts/graph.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/contracts/graph.ts) — no migration, it's a blob). **Node identity**: each instance takes an optional `name` (shown on the face instead of the type label), and a **trigger node now shows its chosen event** on its face, so two same-type nodes are tellable apart at a glance. **Unsaved-changes guard**: the canvas tracks a baseline of the loaded/saved graph and flags dirty on any change ; Save is disabled when clean (and reads "Save changes" when dirty), a native `beforeunload` blocks tab-close/refresh, and the Back button routes through a `ConfirmDialog` when there are pending edits. **Connection validation**: `isValidConnection` rejects self-loops and a second wire into an already-fed variable port. New `automation.editor` i18n (en + fr : `useAsInput`, `nodeName`, `triggerNoEvent`, `leave*`, …). `typecheck` (web + automation) + `lint` + `check:i18n` (1591 keys × 2) + automation integration suite (14/14) green ; needs a live check of the toggle → port flow and the leave-guard.

- 2026-07-27: Automation editor — detach a connection by clicking it. Edges now render through a custom `automationEdge` ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>) `AutomationEdge`) that, when selected (click or **tap** — no keyboard needed), shows a ✕ at its midpoint to remove it ; the removal flows through the parent's controlled edge state via a small `EdgeActionsContext` (React Flow's own `setEdges` doesn't stick in a controlled flow). Also enabled `deleteKeyCode` (Backspace / Delete) for selected edges + nodes, and gated selection/deletion on `canEdit` (read-only viewers can't detach). Data-link edges keep their animated primary stroke. New `automation.editor.deleteEdge` i18n (en + fr). `typecheck` (web + automation) + `lint` + `check:i18n` green.

- 2026-07-27: Automation editor — a node's config variables are now its input ports. Instead of one anonymous data handle, every config field on an action node renders as its **own labelled input port** on the left ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>) `FlowNode` + `PortRow`) — e.g. a Send-Email node shows `email` / `subject` / `body` ports — so you can wire an upstream node's output into any one variable (or type it in the config panel). Run order moved to a slim grey **execution-flow spine** on the node header (an entry handle on the left, a continue handle on the right ; branch nodes like Condition still list their named outputs as labelled rows), keeping sequencing visually separate from the primary-coloured variable inputs (legend relabelled Run order / Variable). A wired variable now **locks its panel editor** with a "Wired" badge + hint, since its value comes from the connection at run time. The engine ([engine.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/engine.ts)) resolves a `field:<key>` data link for **any** config field now, not only ones flagged `linkable` (the flag is retained as an author hint ; [contracts/nodes.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/contracts/nodes.ts)). New `automation.editor` i18n keys in en + fr (`fieldWired`, `fieldWiredHint`, relabelled `legend*` / `port*Hint` ; dropped the now-unused `portFlowIn/Out`). `typecheck` (web + automation) + `lint` + `check:i18n` (1574 keys × 2) + automation integration suite (14/14 against a testcontainer) green ; needs a live check that the port dots line up with their labels.

- 2026-07-27: Automation — closed the delay-after-branch gap. A run suspended by a Delay now resumes down **only the branch it originally took**. Each step persists the exact output handles the node activated (new `AutomationRunStep.activeHandles TEXT[]`, migration `20260726195100_automation_step_active_handles`), and the resumable `executeGraph` ([engine.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/engine.ts)) re-activates from those on resume instead of defaulting to all of a node's outputs — so a Condition placed immediately before a Delay no longer re-enables its pruned branch when the run wakes up ([finishRunStep](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/data.ts) stores the handles). New integration spec: a `Condition → (true) Delay → after` graph with a false branch resumes with the false branch still `SKIPPED` (14/14 against a testcontainer). `typecheck` + `lint` green ; migration applied via `--create-only` + `migrate deploy` (stripping the recurring `DataRecord_data_gin` drift).

- 2026-07-26: Automation editor — two more mobile fixes. (1) **Can't add a node on a phone** : `addNode` / `onConnect` minted ids with `crypto.randomUUID()`, which is undefined in a non-secure context — so a phone hitting the LAN dev server over http threw and silently dropped the add. Replaced with a `genId()` helper that falls back to a `Math.random` id (client-only graph keys, same pattern as the webhook subscription-picker) in [automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>). (2) **Zoom controls + minimap sat below the fold** : the full-height shells used `100vh`, which on mobile includes the retracted URL-bar and runs taller than the visible area, pushing React Flow's bottom-anchored chrome off-screen. Switched calendar / kanban / automation (list + editor) to `100dvh` (matching the data layout ; `dvh` tracks the visible viewport). `tsc --noEmit` + `eslint` green.

- 2026-07-26: Automation — control-flow nodes (Constant / Transform / Delay), a User deactivate node, and a durable-suspend engine. **Constant** ([nodes/constant.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/nodes/constant.ts)) emits a fixed text / number / boolean / JSON value. **Transform** ([nodes/transform.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/nodes/transform.ts)) computes a value from a formula — the engine substitutes `{{ }}` tokens, then the shared safe evaluator (`tryEvaluateFormula`, the same one behind computed Data Model fields) runs it (arithmetic + built-in functions, no arbitrary code). **Delay** ([nodes/delay.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/nodes/delay.ts)) pauses the flow durably: a new `ctx.suspend(ms)` records the node done, and the worker re-schedules the run (`markRunForResume`, PENDING at `now + ms`) instead of blocking ; `executeGraph` is now **resumable** ([engine.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/engine.ts)) — on the next pass it rebuilds `upstream` / active-handles from the run's existing steps, skips completed nodes, and continues downstream (so a pause survives a restart). `attempts` was refactored to count _failures_ only (bumped on retry, not on claim/resume) so a delayed run keeps its retry budget. **Deactivate / Reactivate User** node ([nodes/user.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/nodes/user.ts)) toggles `disabledAt` via a newly-exported `setDisabledAt` ([users/data.ts](monark/app/packages/users/src/server/data.ts) + [index.ts](monark/app/packages/users/src/server/index.ts)), gated by `users.disable`. **Bug fix**: the Event Trigger node's config schema required `eventType`, which failed every run of a freshly-seeded automation (empty trigger config) — made it optional (it drives enqueue-time matching, not execution). 20 built-in nodes now. New integration specs cover Constant, Transform, and a full Delay suspend→resume cycle (13/13 against a testcontainer). `typecheck` + `lint` + `check:tiers` green. Still deferred: **user account create** — `signUpUser` needs Supabase creds + a password/invite strategy, so it's an invite-flow decision, not just a node.

- 2026-07-26: Automation — Find-by-filter data node, a real user picker, and unblocked user-management writes. **Find Records** node ([nodes/data.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/nodes/data.ts)) queries a model by a single field predicate (contains / equals / number / boolean, mapped onto `listDataRecords`' `fieldFilters`), respecting the owner's row-level access (a data admin bypasses ; otherwise the owner's roles gate visibility) and exposing the matched records to downstream nodes. **User picker**: the `user` config-field type now renders a select of the org's active members ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>) `ConfigField`), backed by a new `automation.members.list` endpoint ([router.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/router.ts) + `listOrgMembers` in [data.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/data.ts)) — no more raw user-id typing. **Unblocked user writes**: `@monark/users/server` now exports `updateProfileData` and registers a `users.manage-profile` permission ([permissions.ts](monark/app/packages/users/src/server/permissions.ts), [index.ts](monark/app/packages/users/src/server/index.ts)), enabling an **Update User Profile** node ([nodes/user.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/nodes/user.ts), gated by `users.manage-profile`, only non-blank fields applied). 15 built-in nodes now. New integration specs prove the filter finder returns matches and the profile node renames a member (10/10 against a testcontainer). `typecheck` (automation + users + web) + `lint` + `check:tiers` green. Note: user account create / deactivate still deferred (creation lives in `@monark/auth`/Supabase ; no disable-setter is exported yet).

- 2026-07-26: Kanban — card status select + compact board toolbar. **Status on the card form** ([card-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/card-editor.tsx>)) : a Status select (the board's columns) moves the card to the chosen column on save. Server ([data.ts](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/server/data.ts) `updateCard`, [index.ts](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/server/index.ts) `cards.update`) takes an optional `columnId`, repositions the card at the end of the target column, and emits the same `kanban.card-moved` event a drag does, so automation / webhooks see one move signal either way. **Toolbar compaction** ([kanban-shell.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/kanban-shell.tsx>)) : the board switcher / Columns / Edit-board buttons collapsed into a single mobile-friendly **combo** — the board name + chevron open the switcher, an inline pen opens board settings. The `ColumnManager` dialog was removed ; add-column now lives on the board as a trailing inline `AddColumn` slot ([kanban-board-view.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/kanban-board-view.tsx>)), with edit / reorder / delete / WIP already on each column header's grip + `…` menu. `typecheck` (kanban + web) + `lint` (kanban web) + `check:i18n` green.

- 2026-07-26: Automation — mobile fixes for the list + node editor. **List** ([automations-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/automations-list.tsx>)) : the search/action toolbar sat flush against the table (`FilterBar` carries no margin) ; wrapped the toolbar + table in `space-y-4`. **Editor** ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>)) : (1) the persistent `w-80` config panel buried the canvas on a phone — it's now `hidden md:block` on desktop and a **bottom `Sheet`** on mobile, opened by the current node selection (extracted a shared `ConfigPanel` so both surfaces render the same editor) ; (2) the toolbar overflowed and pushed the add-node / save buttons off-screen — its actions (back, enable toggle, run history, run now, add node, save) now collapse to **icon-only below `md`** with `aria-label`s for the icon-only state, and the name truncates, so the whole row fits a phone. `tsc --noEmit` + `eslint` green ; needs a live phone check that the config sheet opens on node-tap and the toolbar fits.

- 2026-07-26: Automation — editor UX + linkable node inputs. **Themed canvas chrome**: the React Flow zoom controls, minimap, handles, edges, and background dots now follow the app tokens (auto light/dark) via a scoped [automation-editor.css](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.css>) using `var(--card/border/accent/muted/primary)` — they were the library's default light styling. **Create is trigger-free**: the New-automation dialog is just a name now ([automations-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/automations-list.tsx>)) ; a new automation seeds a single Event Trigger node ([data.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/data.ts) `SEEDED_TRIGGER_GRAPH`) and the trigger's event is chosen in the editor, so `triggerEventType` is optional at create and set from the trigger node on save. **Linkable field inputs**: a config field can be marked `linkable` ([contracts/nodes.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/contracts/nodes.ts)) — the node renders a `field:<key>` target handle and an incoming edge feeds the upstream node's output as that field's value at run time, overriding the typed value ([engine.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/engine.ts) separates data-link edges from control-flow edges). The Data Create/Update nodes' JSON payload is now linkable (wire an upstream node's object into it), and their model picker is a **select of the org's Data Models** (new `data-model` field type backed by `dataModels.models.list`) instead of a free-text key. **Reliability**: `createPendingRun` now stamps `nextAttemptAt` from the app clock rather than the DB `@default(now())`, so a run can't look "not yet due" under DB/app clock skew (a drifting Docker Desktop VM clock made the integration suite flaky). New integration spec proves a `field:data` link overrides the typed payload (8/8, stable across repeat runs). `typecheck` (automation + web) + `lint` (automation) + `check:i18n` green.

- 2026-07-26: Automation — conditional branching (a **Condition** node + engine active-handle gating). The engine ([engine.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/engine.ts)) now gates each node on its incoming edges' **source handles**: a node runs only if it's the trigger or an already-executed upstream node activated the handle feeding it, otherwise its step is recorded `SKIPPED` and its own outputs never fire — so a whole un-taken branch prunes. Nodes activate all outputs by default (linear flows unchanged) or call `ctx.activateOutputs([...])` ([registry.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/registry.ts)). The new [Condition node](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/nodes/condition.ts) compares an interpolated value (`eq` / `neq` / `contains` / `gt` / `lt` / `gte` / `lte` / `truthy` / `empty`) and activates its `true` or `false` output. The React Flow editor ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>)) renders one id'd source handle per output (so edges carry `sourceHandle`), with labels for multi-output nodes. New integration spec proves the taken branch runs and the other is `SKIPPED` (7/7 against a testcontainer). `typecheck` (automation + web) + `lint` (automation) green.

- 2026-07-26: Automation — enabled by default. Now that `@monark/automation` has shipped end-to-end, its `automation.enabled` feature flag defaults **on** (was off for the dark launch) in [feature-flags.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/feature-flags.ts). The `/automation` route, nav entry, trigger engine, and run worker are live for every org out of the box ; the flag stays registered so operators can still turn it off per-org / per-role via `/admin/feature-flags`. Takes effect on the next API boot (flag defaults register at boot).

- 2026-07-26: DataTable — the row-actions `…` column stays pinned flush to the container's right edge. It was a normal trailing column, so when data columns overflowed it scrolled off-screen (you had to scroll right to reach the menu), and the auto-width slack absorber sat to its right so it wasn't flush even without overflow. Pinned the actions header + cells with `sticky right-0` (opaque `bg-inherit` on rows / `bg-background` on the header so scrolled cells don't bleed through), and moved the slack-absorber spacer to just _left_ of the actions column (it trails only when there's no actions column) so `…` is flush-right in both the overflow and narrower-than-container cases ([data-table.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/data-table.tsx)). Verified with `pinned-actions` (8 columns overflowing a 720px box : `…` pinned + visible on every row) and `pinned-actions-narrow` (no overflow : `…` flush, slack to its left) screenshot stories. `tsc` + `eslint` green.

- 2026-07-26: Automation — expanded the built-in node library from 3 to 12, covering the communication / data / RBAC / user surfaces. **Communication** : added **Send Email** ([nodes/send-email.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/nodes/send-email.ts), raw `sendMail` to an address, distinct from the user-keyed Notification node). **Data** : **Create / Update / Delete / Find Record** ([nodes/data.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/nodes/data.ts)) against `@monark/data-models` (resolve model by key, JSON payload). **RBAC** : **Assign / Remove Role** ([nodes/rbac.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/nodes/rbac.ts), by role key, via `assignRole`/`revokeRole`). **User** : **Get User** (by id/email) + **Set User Metadata** ([nodes/user.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/nodes/user.ts)). Every privileged node re-checks that the automation's **owner** holds the underlying capability at run time (`requireOwnerPermission` in [nodes/shared.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/nodes/shared.ts) — data writes need `data-models.record-*`, role changes need `rbac.assign-role`, metadata needs `users.write-metadata-for-module-*`), so a flow can't escalate beyond its author ; a run with no owner is denied. Added `@monark/data-models` + `@monark/users` as (core-tier) deps. New integration specs prove a data-create node writes a record (admin owner passes) and that the owner re-check fails a node when the owner lacks the permission (6/6 against a testcontainer). Note: user profile / deactivate / create nodes are still blocked on `@monark/users/server` exporting no write ops (creation lives in `@monark/auth`). `typecheck` + `lint` + `check:tiers` green.

- 2026-07-26: Navigation — unified the secondary-sidebar rail. The `xl+` docked nav rail (`w-72`, `left-14`, `top-14`) was hand-rolled twice with identical chrome : once in [`PageLayout`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/page-layout.tsx) (admin / account) and once inline in the [Data layout](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/layout.tsx>). Extracted into a shared [`SidebarRail`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/sidebar-rail.tsx) (a `side` prop covers PageLayout's left sidebar + its future right rail), so the rail geometry — offset past the primary NavRail, height, breakpoint — lives in one place. Standardized the height on `dvh` (was `vh` in PageLayout). No behavior change. `tsc --noEmit` + `eslint` green. Note: the secondary rail stays at `xl` (not the primary rail's `md`) on purpose — the `288px` rail needs the wider viewport to leave room for content, whereas the `56px` primary rail fits from `md`.

- 2026-07-26: Navigation — folded the app bar into the shared shell (one mount, was nine). `<AppBar />` was re-instantiated in every section (admin / account / data layouts, home, loading, calendar / kanban / automation×2 pages), so any header change meant touching them all. It now mounts **once** in [`(authed)/layout`](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/layout.tsx>) inside the same `md:pl-14` rail offset, above `{children}` ; all nine local mounts removed. The brand mark is resolved once in that layout (for the NavRail) and passed to [`AppBar`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/app-bar.tsx) as a prop (dropped its own `bootstrapStatus` fetch — one query per navigation instead of two). Full-height sections (calendar / kanban / automation, and data at `xl`) now size to `calc(100vh - 57px)` / `calc(100dvh - 57px)` since the 57px bar (56px row + 1px hairline) sits **above** their shell rather than inside its flex column — preserving each section's existing scroll model (body-scroll for admin/account/home, inner-scroll for the full-height shells) with no 1px overflow. `tsc --noEmit` + `eslint` green ; needs a live click-through to confirm the full-height views have no body scroll.

- 2026-07-26: Kanban — cards support **multiple assignees**, shown as an avatar stack. Replaced the single nullable `assigneeId` with an order-preserving `assigneeIds` text array on [`KanbanCard`](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/schema.prisma) (migration `20260726040000_kanban_card_multi_assignee`, backfilling any existing single assignee into a one-element array). The [card editor](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/card-editor.tsx>) now uses a `MultiSelect` of org members (avatar chips + typeahead) instead of a single-select ; the [card](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/client/ui/kanban-card.tsx) renders an **overlapping avatar stack** (first 3 + a `+N` overflow chip, background-ringed so they read as distinct). Server: `cards.create` / `cards.update` take `assigneeIds` ([index.ts](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/server/index.ts), deduped, capped at 20) ; an update diffs the set (order-insensitive) to set the `assignee` changed-flag and emits one `kanban.card-assigned` event **per newly-added assignee** (so each new assignee is notified once, and existing ones aren't re-notified) — the notification subscriber is unchanged (still per-assignee, self-assignment skipped). The `card-created` / `card-updated` events now carry the full `assigneeIds`. Contract types (`CardItem.assigneeIds`), the board view's resolver (`assigneesOf`), README + tech doc, and en/fr i18n (`card.assignees*`, dropped the single-select `assigneeLabel`/`unassigned`) updated. New data-layer integration spec (multi-assignee create, wholesale replace with order preserved, untouched on `undefined`, cleared on `[]`, empty default). `pnpm gen` + `tsc` (kanban + web + api) + `eslint` + `check:i18n` + `check:tiers`/`check:modules` + kanban integration (5) green.
- 2026-07-26: Data tables — the per-row `…` actions menu is now **pinned to the right edge** of the row instead of trailing right after the last data column. In the shared [`DataTable`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/data-table.tsx) the actions column (header + body cell) is `position: sticky; right: 0`, so it stays flush-right when the columns don't fill the width and stays visible during horizontal scroll (wide tables) rather than sliding off. The pinned cell needs its own opaque background (a cell paints on top of the row's, so reusing the row's translucent tint directly would apply it twice — reading brighter in dark theme / the header black). The body actions cell layers the row's **real** translucent tint (`hover:bg-muted/30`, selected `bg-primary/10`) as an absolute fill over an opaque `bg-background` base, so it renders pixel-identically to a transparent sibling — exact even for the saturated selected tint (an opaque `color-mix` approximation of alpha-compositing visibly diverged there). The header cell, low-chroma, uses the opaque `color-mix` composite. RBAC/events/notifications/webhooks/flags N/A ; no i18n ; `tsc` (web) + `eslint` green.
- 2026-07-26: Navigation — unified the mobile secondary-nav strip. Admin and Data each had a byte-for-byte-identical scroll-hiding sticky tab bar, while Account used a different non-sticky strip _inside_ its content column. Extracted the shared chrome into [`SecondaryTabsBar`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/secondary-tabs-bar.tsx) (sticky `top-14`, hide-on-scroll-down / reveal-on-scroll-up, `xl:hidden`) ; [AdminTabsBar](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/admin-tabs-bar.tsx>) + [DataTabsBar](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/data-tabs-bar.tsx>) are now thin wrappers over it, and [account/layout](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/account/layout.tsx>) moved its horizontal nav out of the centered content into the same shared bar (so all three sections read + behave identically on mobile). No i18n / behavior change beyond Account gaining the sticky scroll-hide. `tsc --noEmit` + `eslint` green.

- 2026-07-26: Automation — new **Core module** `@monark/automation` : event-triggered flows built on a visual node-graph editor, with a durable outbox + worker execution model and a node-type registry other modules extend. **Data model** (`// ── MODULE: automation ──` in [schema.prisma](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/schema.prisma), migration `20260725231737_add_automation`) : `Automation` (org-scoped, `triggerEventType` + `graph` JSON), `AutomationRun` (the outbox — `@@index([status, nextAttemptAt])`), `AutomationRunStep` (per-node observability). **Engine** : a wildcard event-bus subscriber ([subscriber.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/subscriber.ts)) matches every emit against enabled automations (org via `organizationId` or the actor's memberships, skipping `automation.*` to avoid loops) and enqueues one `PENDING` run each — cheap work only, never blocking `emit` ; a background worker ([worker.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/worker.ts)) claims runs atomically (`updateMany` guard, safe across processes), executes the graph in topological order recording a step per node ([engine.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/engine.ts), with `{{ trigger.x }}` / `{{ nodeId.y }}` interpolation), and retries with backoff (3 attempts) before dead-lettering — the same outbox+worker shape as `@monark/webhooks`. **Extension API** : `registerAutomationNodes("<module>", { … })` ([registry.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/registry.ts)) — any module contributes node types (config schema + server `execute`), mirroring `registerPermissions` ; a `nodeTypes.list` query projects only the serializable descriptor to the editor. Ships three built-ins ([nodes/](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/nodes)) : Event Trigger, Send Notification (a generic `automation.custom-message` kind, en + fr), and Webhook (SSRF-guarded `fetch`). **RBAC** : `automation.{view,create,manage,run}` gate the tRPC router ([router.ts](monark/app/packages/automation/src/server/router.ts)) ; runs execute as the automation owner. **Web** : a flag-gated (`automation.enabled`, default off) `/automation` section — a `DataTable` list ([automations-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/automations-list.tsx>)) + a full React Flow editor ([automation-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/automation/[id]/automation-editor.tsx>), new `@xyflow/react` dep) with a node palette, per-node config panel, save, run-now, and a run-history sheet ; nav entry + `automation.*` i18n (en + fr). Boot-wired in [server.ts](monark/app/services/api/src/server.ts). Docs in [technical-documentation/automation.md](monark/app/docs/technical-documentation/automation.md). Gate green : `pnpm gen` + `typecheck` (automation + api + web) + `lint` + `check:tiers` + `check:modules` + `check:i18n` ; automation integration suite 4/4 against a fresh testcontainer (manual run + event-triggered run + non-match + RBAC deny).

- 2026-07-26: DataTable — empty / error states center on the visible container, not the table. They rendered as a `<td colSpan>` inside the table, so when wide columns overflowed and forced horizontal scroll the message centered on the (wider-than-viewport) table width and drifted off-screen. Moved them out of the `<table>` into a sibling in the scroll wrapper ([data-table.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/data-table.tsx)), pinned with `sticky left-0 w-full` so the block sizes to the visible area and stays centered even while the table scrolls sideways (dropped the now-unused `visibleColumnCount`). Verified with a `table-empty-state` screenshot story (8 columns overflowing a 720px container : message stays centred on the visible box). `tsc` + `eslint` green.

- 2026-07-26: Navigation — persistent desktop primary-nav rail + single-source nav + launcher hidden. The top-level destinations (Calendar / Kanban / Data + an admin pin) were only reachable behind the app bar's hamburger drawer, even on desktop. Added a persistent [`NavRail`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/nav-rail.tsx) — a slim `w-14` icon column pinned to the left edge on `md+` (brand at top, `PRIMARY_NAV` icons with tooltips, admin pin at the bottom) — mounted once in the shared [`(authed)/layout`](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/layout.tsx>), which now offsets its content by the rail width (`md:pl-14`). The hamburger + app-bar logo become `md:hidden` (the rail owns the brand + primary nav on desktop; the drawer stays for mobile), and the two `fixed` secondary-sidebar rails ([PageLayout](monark/app/services/web/src/components/page-layout.tsx), [data/layout](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/layout.tsx>)) shift to `left-14` to clear it. Both nav surfaces (rail + drawer) now read a single [`usePrimaryNav`](monark/app/services/web/src/hooks/use-primary-nav.ts) hook — same `PRIMARY_NAV` source, flag/admin gating, active-state — so adding a destination is a one-line entry + i18n key and the two surfaces can't drift. Hid the "Monark apps" launcher from the [app bar](monark/app/services/web/src/components/app-bar.tsx) (single product for now; re-add `<AppLauncher />` when a second ships). `NavRail` split into a pure `NavRailView` + data wrapper so it previews in the screenshot harness (added `next/image` stub + a `process` shim to [tools/screenshots](monark/app/services/web/tools/screenshots)); verified at mobile + desktop. `tsc --noEmit` + `eslint` green.

- 2026-07-26: Nav chrome — the desktop rail's logo square now lines up with the app bar's bottom divider (was 1px high). The [AppBar](monark/app/services/web/src/components/app-bar.tsx) puts its `border-b` on the outer `<header>`, _below_ the `h-14` row (57px incl. hairline), but the [NavRail](monark/app/services/web/src/components/nav-rail.tsx) logo carried the border on the same `h-14` element — so under Tailwind's global `border-box` the 1px border ate into the 56px and the divider landed 1px above the bar's, breaking the horizontal line where the rail meets the bar. Added `box-content` to the logo link so its border sits outside the 56px, exactly like the AppBar. Also made the `nav-rail` screenshot story's mock header structurally faithful to the real AppBar (it had co-located border + `h-14`, so it couldn't have shown the bug). Verified by measuring both bottoms in the harness (Playwright `getBoundingClientRect`): logo and header now both bottom at y=57, delta 0.000px. `eslint` green.
- 2026-07-26: Kanban — richer cards + board polish. **New card fields** `priority` (LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH/CRITICAL, colour-graded), `estimate` (int), and `reviewerId` on [`KanbanCard`](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/schema.prisma) (migration `20260726023411_kanban_card_fields`) ; wired through the contract types, the data layer, and the `cards.create`/`update` inputs ([data.ts](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/server/data.ts), [index.ts](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/server/index.ts)), with `card-updated.changed` extended to cover them. The [card editor](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/card-editor.tsx>) gained a colour-dotted **priority** picker, a numeric **estimate**, and a **reviewer** select (org members). **Card layout** ([kanban-card.tsx](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/client/ui/kanban-card.tsx)): more padding, a priority pill (colour dot + label) + estimate chip in a meta row, a calendar glyph on the due badge (hand-rolled SVG — the client package keeps no lucide dep), and **clicking anywhere on the card** opens the editor (drag still works via the pointer sensor's activation distance). A shared `KANBAN_PRIORITY_COLOR` keeps the card dot and the picker in sync. **Board/column polish** ([board-column.tsx](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/client/ui/board-column.tsx), [kanban-board-view.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/kanban-board-view.tsx>)): columns are **full-height**; the reorder **grip** reveals on **header** hover (was whole-column) and sits left of the name; the **"…" menu** (add card / edit column / delete column) lives in each header; the "No cards" placeholder is gone, replaced by a **card-like "Add card"** that reveals on column hover (desktop) and sits below the last card / always shows on touch. New `kanban.card.{reviewerLabel,noReviewer,priorityLabel,priorityNone,priority.*,estimate*}` + `kanban.column.reorderAria` i18n, en + fr. Full pre-PR gate green (`pnpm gen` + `typecheck` 19/19 + `lint` 19/19 + `test` 16/16 + `check:tiers` + `check:modules` + `check:i18n`) ; kanban integration 4 tests (migration applied to a fresh testcontainer) ; `kanban-board` screenshot updated (priority pill + estimate chip + calendar-icon due badge).

- 2026-07-26: Global search — modules are now discoverable in the command palette by default, and content search is a real extension point. Two fixes to one root cause (the palette hardcoded its own destination list + a calendar-only content branch, so Kanban and Data were absent). (1) **"Go to" now derives from the drawer nav.** [global-search/routes.ts](monark/app/services/web/src/components/global-search/routes.ts) builds its module destinations from `PRIMARY_NAV` (the drawer's single source of truth, [config/primary-nav.ts](monark/app/services/web/src/config/primary-nav.ts)) instead of a second hand-maintained copy, so Kanban + Data appear immediately and the two lists can never drift again ; labels reuse the drawer's own strings. (2) **New content-provider registry.** [global-search/search-providers.ts](monark/app/services/web/src/components/global-search/search-providers.ts) lists `SearchProvider`s — each a client component (`isActive(pathname)` + its own tRPC search) that renders a shared [`SearchResultsGroup`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/global-search/search-results-group.tsx). Migrated calendar's hardcoded branch into a `CalendarSearchProvider`, and added `KanbanSearchProvider` (cards) + `DataSearchProvider` (current model's records). Kanban gained a gated `cards.search` procedure ([index.ts](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/server/index.ts) + `searchCards` in [data.ts](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/server/data.ts), scoped to accessible boards) and a `?card=` deep-link so a searched card opens its editor ([kanban-shell.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/kanban-shell.tsx>)) ; data records reuse the already-gated `records.list` search and deep-link via `?record=`. Documented both as a module-shipping default in [CLAUDE.md](monark/app/CLAUDE.md) (new "Navigation & global search" section + a DoD checklist item). New i18n (`globalSearch.groups.{kanban,data}`, en + fr ; dropped the now-derived `routes.calendar`). New kanban integration spec for `searchCards` (title match, board-name, soft-delete drop, empty-scope). `pnpm gen` + `tsc` (web + kanban) + `eslint` + `check:i18n` green.

- 2026-07-25: DataTable — the selection checkbox column is now a true fixed 64px. With `table-fixed` + `min-w-full`, a table narrower than its container scaled **every** column up proportionally — so the fixed 64px checkbox column ballooned (~1.5×) on narrow tables like the industries model. Fixed by appending a single width-less trailing spacer cell to each row ([data-table.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/data-table.tsx)) : under fixed table layout the lone auto-width column absorbs all the slack, so the `select` / `actions` chrome columns _and_ every data column (including the still-fully-resizable primary) keep exactly their set widths, with the leftover space falling to the right of the last column. Empty/error rows span `+1` for the spacer. `tsc` + `eslint` green (couldn't re-screenshot — the shared Vite harness bundle is currently broken by an unrelated in-progress kanban import).

- 2026-07-25: Kanban — columns are draggable by their header and carry a "…" menu, right on the board. The board view ([kanban-board-view.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/kanban-board-view.tsx>)) now runs **two** sortable types in one `DndContext`: horizontal column sorting (grab the column header — `BoardColumn` gained a `headerDragProps` slot that turns the header into the drag handle) alongside the existing card sorting. Collision detection is type-aware (dragging a column only collides with columns) and column order is optimistic (`columns.reorder` on drop). Each header also gets a `MoreHorizontal` dropdown ([`ColumnMenu`]) with **Add card**, **Edit column** (name / colour / card-limit via a new [column-edit-dialog.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/column-edit-dialog.tsx>) → `columns.update`), and **Delete column** (confirm → `columns.delete`) ; the trigger stops pointer propagation so opening the menu never starts a column drag. New `kanban.column.*` i18n (en + fr). The "Manage columns" dialog stays for adding columns / bulk edits. `tsc` (web + kanban) + `eslint` + `check:i18n` green ; `kanban-board` screenshot updated.

- 2026-07-25: Kanban — the card editor's due date uses the app's date picker instead of a raw `<input type="date">`. Extracted a controlled, date-only [`DatePicker`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/ui/date-picker.tsx) (trigger button with a calendar glyph + the formatted day or a placeholder, a `Calendar` popover, and a clear ✕) — the plain-`useState` sibling of the RHF-bound field-toolkit `DateField`, so controlled forms get the same look without a form context. Wired it into the [card editor](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/card-editor.tsx>) ; the card still holds its `YYYY-MM-DD` string and submits the same payload, converting to/from a local `Date` at the picker boundary (local-day math, so the highlighted day matches the string regardless of timezone). New `kanban.card.duePlaceholder` / `dueClear` i18n (en + fr). Verified with a `date-picker` screenshot story (empty + chosen states). `tsc` + `eslint` + 236 web tests + `check:i18n` green.
- 2026-07-25: Tables — empty states now explain _why_ a list is empty and offer the right next step. Before, every table's empty message keyed off the search box alone, so a too-restrictive filter (with no search) wrongly showed `No results for ""`. New shared [`TableEmptyState`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/table-empty-state.tsx) + `tableEmptyReason({ hasSearch, hasFilters })` classify the emptiness into four reasons — **no-data** (nothing created → offers the Create/Invite CTA), **no-search** (bad search → offers _Clear search_), **no-filters** (over-restrictive filters → _Clear filters_), **no-search-filters** (both → _Clear search & filters_) — each with its own icon, message, and one next-step button. Constrained-empty copy + clear-action labels come from a shared `table.empty.*` i18n namespace (en + fr) via the [`useTableEmptyLabels`](monark/app/services/web/src/lib/use-table-empty-labels.ts) hook, while the genuinely-empty title stays each screen's own "No X yet" copy. Propagated to **every** list — data-model records, admin users / roles / webhooks (+ deliveries) / organizations / data-models — reusing the filter-menu `activeFilterCount` (detect active filters) and `clearAllFilters` (the clear action) so per-screen wiring is a few lines. Pruned the now-dead per-screen `emptySearch` keys (6 × en/fr). Verified with a `table-empty-state` screenshot story (all four reasons) ; `tsc` + `eslint` + 236 web tests + `check:i18n` (en/fr parity) green.
- 2026-07-25: Admin UI — hide organization-scope choices on a single-tenant deploy. Most org-scope UI (role/webhook/invite org pickers, the org list, breadcrumb) already collapsed via `bootstrapStatus.mode`; this closes the remaining leaks so a single-org instance never surfaces an org-vs-platform distinction. Added a shared [`useIsSingleTenant`](monark/app/services/web/src/lib/use-is-single-tenant.ts) hook (the detection was copy-pasted across ~7 callers) and gated: the org name appended to pending-invite rows in the users list ([users-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/users/users-list.tsx>)); the read-only "Scope" row in the webhook editor, previously shown to any sysadmin ([webhook-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/webhooks/webhook-editor.tsx>)); the "Pick the scope and the role" assign-role subtitle ([admin-roles.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/users/[id]/admin-roles.tsx>)); and the system-admins card's "…every permission across every organization" subtitle ([system-admins-card.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/rbac/system-admins-card.tsx>)) — the last two swap to scope-free copy via new `dialogSubtitleSingle` / `subtitleSingle` i18n keys (en + fr). Multi-tenant deploys are unchanged. `tsc` (web) + `lint` + `check:i18n` (en/fr parity) green.
- 2026-07-25: Forms — dropped the always-on character counter under `text` / `longText` fields. `TextField` / `LongTextField` ([text-fields.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/fields/inputs/text-fields.tsx)) no longer render the persistent `{count}/{max}` line ; a `maxLength` limit now surfaces **only as an error** (`FormMessage`, via the schema's existing `tooLong` message) if a value ever exceeds it, so a regular correct form stays uncluttered and saves the vertical space. Removed the now-dead scaffolding : the `counter` prop + its message-row rendering from [`FieldShell`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/fields/field-shell.tsx), the `charCount` entry from `FieldLabels` ([types.ts](monark/app/services/web/src/components/fields/types.ts)) + [`useFieldStrings`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/fields/strings.tsx), and the `fields.charCount` i18n key (en + fr). The input keeps its hard `maxLength` cap, so typing still can't exceed the limit. README field-type table updated. `eslint` + `check:i18n` (1485 keys, en/fr parity) green ; `fields-form` screenshot confirms the Title counter is gone.

- 2026-07-25: Kanban — fixed unreliable card drops + flattened the board look ([kanban-board-view.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/kanban-board-view.tsx>), [board-column.tsx](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/client/ui/board-column.tsx), [board-area.tsx](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/client/ui/board-area.tsx)). Root cause of "can't drop into every other column": `closestCorners` collision detection measured distance to the nearest droppable _corner_, so hovering a sparse/empty column snapped onto a card in the neighbouring column — the drop (and the flickery highlight) landed in the wrong place. Switched to a `pointerWithin` strategy (with a `rectIntersection` fallback for gaps + the keyboard sensor) so the column under the pointer is the exact target. Also reworked the move to resolve entirely **on drop** from stable committed state (no mid-drag card-state mutation, which had its own stale-closure races), and added a highlight-only `onDragOver` that lights up the whole resolved column (over a card or empty space alike) instead of only the raw `isOver` body. Visual: columns are now **flat on the background** — no raised muted card, separated by divider borders with underlined headers and no gaps between them (cards keep their card styling). `tsc` (web + kanban) + `eslint` green ; `kanban-board` screenshot updated.

- 2026-07-25: Kanban — board role access + WIP-limit editing (finishing two half-built surfaces). **Role access**: the board dialog ([board-dialog.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/board-dialog.tsx>)) now shows a role checklist to managers (`kanban.manage`), sourced from `rbac.myRoles` like the calendar's manage dialog; it initializes from the board's current `roleAccess` (threaded from the shell) and passes `roleIds` to `boards.create` / `update` (the server already gated + persisted them). Leaving all roles unchecked keeps the board visible to everyone. **WIP limits**: the column manager ([column-manager.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/column-manager.tsx>)) gained a per-column card-limit number input wired to `columns.update` — the board header already rendered `N / limit` (turning red over the cap), so this makes the surfaced limit settable (it stays advisory, not enforced). New i18n (`kanban.boardDialog.rolesLabel` / `rolesHint`, `kanban.columns.wipAria`), en + fr. `tsc` (web) + `eslint` + `check:i18n` green.

- 2026-07-25: Kanban — Phase 2 completes the Big-5 wiring + card assignees. **Notifications**: a new `kanban.card-assigned` domain event (emitted on create-with-assignee and reassignment) drives a `kanban.card.assigned` notification kind ([notification-kinds.ts](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/server/notification-kinds.ts), category ACTIVITY, in-app default-on + email opt-in, en + fr) via a subscriber ([notification-subscriber.ts](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/server/notification-subscriber.ts)) that notifies the assignee (self-assignment skipped); payload declaration-merged in [contracts/notifications.ts](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/contracts/notifications.ts). **Feature flag**: `kanban.board` (default-on, [flags.ts](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/server/flags.ts)) now gates the feature — the [route](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban/page.tsx>) 404s when off and the primary-nav entry hides (added an optional `flag` to `PrimaryNavEntry`; the [nav menu](monark/app/services/web/src/components/primary-nav-menu.tsx) filters via `featureFlags.getAllForSession`). **Assignees**: a `kanban.members` query ([data.ts](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/server/data.ts) `listOrgMembers`) backs an assignee picker in the card editor and avatar resolution on cards. All three `register*` helpers wired into [server.ts](monark/app/services/api/src/server.ts) (flags / notification-kinds / subscriber phases). New i18n (`kanban.card.assigneeLabel` / `unassigned`), en + fr; developer doc [technical-documentation/kanban.md](monark/app/docs/technical-documentation/kanban.md). Deferred: board role-access UI, WIP enforcement, card labels/filtering. `pnpm gen` + `tsc` (kanban + web + api) + `eslint` + `check:i18n` + `check:tiers` + `check:modules` green; kanban integration suite (3 tests) green on a Postgres testcontainer.

- 2026-07-25: Admin UI — stopped displaying machine identifiers as read-only chrome. Removed the organization `slug` (both the read-only field in the org profile editor [organization-detail.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/organizations/[id]/organization-detail.tsx>) and the mono subtext in the org list [organizations-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/organizations/organizations-list.tsx>)); the Data Model `key` (the page-header subtitle + the field-row key line in [model-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/data-models/[id]/model-editor.tsx>), and the list subtext in [data-models-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/data-models/data-models-list.tsx>) — the deleted-model row keeps its "Deleted" badge); and the record `slug` subtext in the records list ([records-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/models/[modelKey]/records-list.tsx>)). These are auto-derived, immutable, non-user-facing identifiers; showing them read-only was clutter with no action attached. Meaningful operational values (webhook endpoint URLs / event types, delivery ids, formula-insert field chips) and the dev overlay are untouched. Dropped the now-dead org i18n (`detail.labels.slug`, `detail.slugHint`), en + fr. `tsc` (web) + `lint` + `check:i18n` green.
- 2026-07-25: Data models — the record title is a reserved convention, not an admin choice. Every model now auto-creates a required TEXT field with key `title` on creation ([createDataModel](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/server/data.ts) does it in a transaction); `DataRecord.title` is always that field's value, so the "which field backs the title" picker + "Use as title field" row action are gone from the schema builder ([model-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/data-models/[id]/model-editor.tsx>)), the `title` field shows a read-only "Title" badge, and it can't be archived / deleted / retyped or made optional ([router.ts](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/server/router.ts) guards). Backend simplified: **dropped `DataModel.titleFieldId`** (column + relation + the `models.update` input field + its validation + the archive-nulling branch); `computeTitle(data)` now just reads `data.title` with no DB lookup or title-field pointer; `recomputeModelRecords` no longer special-cases a formula-backed title (a formula can't back the title anymore). Migration [20260725210000](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/migrations/20260725210000_data_models_reserved_title_field/migration.sql) backfills existing data (creates a `title` field per model lacking one; seeds `data.title` from the denormalized `DataRecord.title`) before dropping the column. Records list identifies the pinned primary column by `key === "title"` ([records-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/models/[modelKey]/records-list.tsx>)); new shared `TITLE_FIELD_KEY` constant ([field-types.ts](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/contracts/field-types.ts)). Dropped the now-dead i18n (`editor.settings.titleField*`, `editor.fields.actions.makeTitleField`) and added `editor.fields.titleBadge`, en + fr. Integration + unit suites updated for the auto-created title field (data-models 61 + 28, calendar subscriber 5, all green); `tsc` (data-models / calendar / web) + `lint` + `check:i18n` + `check:tiers` green.
- 2026-07-25: Data tables — the Data section now uses a deterministic full-height layout, so a list's table fills the viewport and scrolls internally with a single scrollbar (no page scroll, no blank strip, no flicker). Earlier attempts had the shared [`DataTable`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/data-table.tsx) self-measure a height cap against the viewport ; that was fragile (a stale one-shot `docTop`, a reserved `pb-20` reappearing under the table as a second scrollbar, and a `scrollHeight`-based reservation that fed back into a runaway flicker). Replaced measurement with a CSS flex height chain in the Data shell, gated to the app's desktop breakpoint (`xl`, where the vertical rail replaces the horizontal tab bar) : [`data/layout.tsx`](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/layout.tsx>) becomes a `xl:h-[100dvh]` flex column whose `<main>` is the single bounded scroll region (`xl:flex-1 xl:min-h-0 xl:overflow-y-auto`), the records page ([page.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/models/[modelKey]/page.tsx>)) is `flex flex-col xl:h-full` whose table wrapper is `xl:flex-1 xl:min-h-0` ([records-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/models/[modelKey]/records-list.tsx>) passes `tableClassName`), and `DataTable` gained a **`fillParent`** prop ([types.ts](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/types.ts)) that at `xl+` takes `h-full` of its (already-bounded) parent and scrolls internally, skipping all measurement. A taller detail/form page under the same `<main>` scrolls `<main>` itself — still one scrollbar. **Below `xl` (phones / tablets)** everything falls back to plain block flow so the page scrolls naturally : the secondary tab bar's hide-on-scroll and the mobile URL-bar collapse keep working, and the `flex-1`/`h-full` classes are `xl:`-gated so they can't collapse to zero against the unbounded mobile parent. `DataTable` keeps its self-measuring mode as the default for window-scrolling sections (admin, etc.) that haven't adopted the shell yet, so this change is scoped to the Data section ; those can opt in to `fillParent` under the same pattern later. RBAC/events/notifications/webhooks/flags N/A ; `tsc` (web ; unrelated in-progress kanban error aside) + `eslint` on touched files green.
- 2026-07-25: Data models — records list filters by **any field**, not just archived status. Previously the polymorphic records list ([records-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/models/[modelKey]/records-list.tsx>)) only had the active/deleted toggle. The filter menu now lists **every** field of the model, each opening a value sub-menu whose control matches the field type — text/URL/email/long/rich-text → contains, number → exact match, boolean → an "Any / Yes / No" picker, single-select (status-like) → a checklist that matches **any of one-or-more** chosen values, date/datetime → a day picker, multi-select → a checklist (matches any chosen value). Server side, `records.list` gained a `fieldFilters` input and [`listDataRecords`](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/server/data.ts) translates each into a Prisma predicate against the JSONB `data` column (`string_contains` for text/date, `equals` for number/boolean, `equals` OR-ed for single-select "any of", `array_contains` OR-ed for multi-select), ANDed with search + row-level access ([router.ts](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/server/router.ts)). FORMULA fields are filterable by their inferred result type ; RELATION is deferred (it needs an async id→label picker the `FilterConfig` union doesn't model yet). Neutral / empty values compose out (no-op), and changing a filter resets pagination. Select / multi-select filter options render with the field's **configured badge colors** (the same colored chips the value shows in the table + form), so a status' color is a visual reminder in the menu — `FilterOption.label` is a `ReactNode`, so the option carries a `<Badge>` while `searchText` keeps it typeahead-matchable. New `data.records.filter*` i18n (en + fr). Covered by 3 new records integration specs (text/number/boolean, select/multi-select-any, AND + neutral-pruning) — the full records suite (12) passes against a real Postgres testcontainer ; `pnpm gen` + `tsc` (web + data-models) + `eslint` + `check:i18n` green.
- 2026-07-25: Kanban — a new board view (sibling to the calendar), Phase 1. Added the `@monark/kanban` extended module: an org owns multiple named **boards**, each with configurable **columns** (Backlog / Todo / In Progress / Review / QA / Done by default) and drag-and-drop **cards**. Like the calendar, it owns its own Prisma models directly in [schema.prisma](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/schema.prisma) under a `// ── MODULE: kanban ──` banner (`KanbanBoard` / `KanbanBoardRoleAccess` / `KanbanColumn` / `KanbanCard`, migration `20260725200201_add_kanban`) ; column + card ordering use an integer `position` in gaps of 10 with a full-list reorder transaction (the `data-models` house pattern), and `moveCard` re-columns + reindexes the target. The module ships contracts (types + a real `KanbanEvents` union), a server layer ([data.ts](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/server/data.ts) + the [`kanbanRouter`](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/server/index.ts) whose every mutation guards with `requirePermission(ctx, "kanban.<key>", org.id)` and emits a domain event), pure-React client primitives ([client/ui](monark/app/packages/kanban/src/client/ui)), and the wired drag-and-drop web view ([services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/kanban>)) built on `@dnd-kit`. **Big-4 wiring**: RBAC (`kanban.{view,create,edit,delete,manage}`), the event bus (`kanban.board-created` / `column-created` / `card-created` / `card-updated` / `card-moved` / `card-deleted`), and webhooks (free via `registerKanbanEventTypes()`) are all live ; `register*` helpers wired into [server.ts](monark/app/services/api/src/server.ts), nav entry + `kanban` i18n namespace (en + fr) added. Notifications (card-assigned) and a `kanban.board` feature-flag gate are the deferred Phase 2. `pnpm gen` + `tsc` (web + api + kanban) + `eslint` + `check:i18n` + `check:tiers` + `check:modules` green.

- 2026-07-25: Admin forms — stopped asking users to hand-author machine identifiers. Removed the optional **`key`** inputs from the Data Model create form ([data-models-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/data-models/data-models-list.tsx>)) and the field create dialog ([field-editor-dialog.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/data-models/[id]/field-editor-dialog.tsx>)), and made the organization **`slug`** read-only in the org profile editor ([organization-detail.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/organizations/[id]/organization-detail.tsx>)). These identifiers are load-bearing (RELATION targets, `/data/models/[modelKey]` routing, per-model permission + event keys, org URLs) but were already server-derived and collision-safe (`findFreeDataModelKey` / `findFreeDataFieldKey` from the name/label; org slug at bootstrap), so letting a user type them was friction with a permanent-bad-choice downside for an immutable value. Keys/slugs stay **visible read-only** where they were (model editor subtitle, field row subtext, org slug field) so they remain discoverable. No server change: the tRPC create inputs keep `key`/`slug` optional-with-override, so migration/tooling can still set them explicitly. Dropped the now-dead i18n (`admin.dataModels.create.key*`, `…fieldDialog.key*`, `admin.organizations.detail.invalidSlug` + `placeholders.slug`; reworded `slugHint`), en + fr. `tsc` + `eslint` + `check:i18n` green.

- 2026-07-25: Data tables — bulk edit. The shared [`DataTable`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/data-table.tsx) gained opt-in multi-row selection (a leading checkbox column + header select-all, via a `selection` prop wired to TanStack row selection ; transient, never persisted with the layout — the select cell + checkbox stop click propagation so selecting never opens a row). A new [`BulkEditBar`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/bulk-edit-bar.tsx) shows a count + edit/clear bar when rows are selected and opens a **field-driven** edit dialog : pick one of the data model's editable fields (computed `formula` fields excluded), set a single value (reusing `AutoForm` for the per-type input + validation), and — when the selected rows currently hold different values for that field — confirm past an overwrite warning before applying to all. Wired into the generic records list ([records-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/models/[modelKey]/records-list.tsx>)), so it lights up for every Data Model.
