Backend: new [`records.bulkUpdate`](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/server/router.ts) (`{ ids, data }`, ≤500 ids, same overlay applied per row through `updateDataRecord`'s merge+revalidate+recompute, one `record-updated` event per row) gated by a **new, separate** `data-models.record-bulk-write` permission ([permissions.ts](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/server/permissions.ts)) — required _in addition to_ per-model `record-write`, so bulk edit is grantable/revocable apart from single-record editing ; the UI hides the whole affordance when the caller lacks it. New `table.selectRow`/`selectAll` + `data.records.bulkEdit.*` i18n (en + fr) ; integration specs prove the deny-without-bulk, apply-to-all, and reject-cross-model paths. `gen` + `tsc` + `eslint` + `check:tiers`/`check:modules` + data-models integration tests green.

- 2026-07-25: Data models — **adding or editing a `FORMULA` field now backfills existing records**, fixing formula columns rendering empty in the record table. Compute-on-write only ran on record writes, so any record that predated a formula field (or predated an expression change) had no stored value — the form preview computed it live and looked fine, but the data table (which shows the _stored_ value) rendered a dash. New `recomputeModelRecords(dataModelId)` in [data.ts](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/server/data.ts) re-evaluates every record's formulas in place (skipping records whose output is unchanged, so a no-op re-save writes nothing) and refreshes a formula-backed title ; `createDataField` calls it after adding a `FORMULA` field and `updateDataField` after a formula's expression changes. Two integration specs (backfill on add, re-backfill on expression change) ; full formula suite (9) green against a real Postgres testcontainer. RBAC/events/notifications/webhooks/flags unchanged (rides the existing schema-edit permission ; a bounded admin-time recompute).
- 2026-07-25: Data models — a `FORMULA` field's result type is now **inferred**, not configured. Dropped the `resultType` picker from the schema builder's formula editor ([formula-config.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/data-models/[id]/formula-config.tsx>)) and the `resultType` key from the field config ([field-types.ts](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/contracts/field-types.ts), config is now just `{ expression }`). A new `inferResultType` ([formula.ts](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/contracts/formula.ts)) derives the type from the expression's outermost operation (comparisons/logic → BOOLEAN, arithmetic → NUMBER, `&`/text functions → TEXT, date functions → DATE; a bare reference or a mixed-branch `if`/`coalesce` falls back to TEXT). The override is an explicit cast — four new functions `toNumber` / `toString` / `toBool` / `toDate` both coerce the value and pin the type from the outermost position. Compute-on-write ([data.ts](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/server/data.ts)) and the client `FieldDef` adapter ([data-field-adapter.ts](monark/app/services/web/src/components/fields/data-field-adapter.ts)) now compute the type via `inferResultType` instead of reading it from config ; the editor shows the inferred type read-only as a badge. Feels more natural (like Notion) and removes a drift-prone redundant field. Updated i18n (`…formula.resultLabel` replaces `resultType`, help text documents the casts, en+fr) and the Prisma enum comment. Tests: engine unit specs gained cast-function + `inferResultType` coverage ; integration configs dropped `resultType` (inference yields the same types). RBAC/events/notifications/webhooks/flags unchanged (pure refactor of an existing additive field).

- 2026-07-25: Forms — extended the label-left layout to the `AutoForm` / fields toolkit. [`FieldShell`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/fields/field-shell.tsx) (the wrapper behind every schema-driven field) now flips its label from above the control to a left 10rem column at the same `@md` container-query threshold as the hand-rolled [`FieldRow`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/field-row.tsx), so an `AutoForm` in a detail panel reads identically to the hand-rolled forms beside it. The control + help + error now stack together in a right-column wrapper, so a wrapping error / char-counter aligns under the control rather than the label. `richText` (WYSIWYG) and `longText` (textarea) opt out and always stack full-width — they need the row width for a usable editing area — and `boolean` is unchanged (already its own label-left / toggle-right settings row, never via `FieldShell`). [`AutoForm`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/fields/auto-form.tsx) wraps its fields in `@container` so the flip scopes to the form's own width (flips in a wide panel / full page, stacks on mobile). Verified with the `fields-form` screenshot story: desktop flips every field except Notes (richText) + Published (boolean); mobile stacks all. `eslint` green on the touched files.

- 2026-07-25: Forms — restored the desktop side-panel label-left layout. The shared [`FieldRow`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/field-row.tsx) flips a field's label from above the control to a left 10rem column via a container query, but the breakpoint was `@lg` (512px container content-box) — above the content-box of the app's real detail panels (`sm:max-w-lg`/`xl`/`2xl` = 512/576/672px outer, ~464/528/624px after the panel body's `px-6`), so the flip almost never engaged in a side panel (a `sm:max-w-lg` panel never did; `xl` only barely). Lowered the switch to `@md` (~448px), which fires in every desktop detail panel while a mobile full-screen panel (well under 448px) still stacks. One shared-component change, so it fixes label-left across every `@container` form app-wide (webhook / organization / RBAC role / admin + self-service profile editors, calendar manage dialog, and the data forms). Verified with a temporary `field-row-flip` screenshot story modelling the real panel widths + `px-6` padding (mobile stacks; lg/xl/2xl all flip left); story removed after confirming. `eslint` green on the touched files.

- 2026-07-09: List surfaces — sticky table headers, relocated tools, and uniform toolbar button sizing. (1) **Sticky headers, no double scrollbar**: the [`DataTable`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/data-table.tsx) now caps its table+footer region to the space between its top and the viewport bottom (measured `docTop`, stable across page scroll) and scrolls the body internally, so the `thead` stays pinned via `sticky top-0` as you scroll — the header is opaque (`bg-background` behind the header row's `bg-muted/30` tint) so rows can't bleed through, with a `border-b` divider. The cap also subtracts the scrolling `<main>`'s own `padding-bottom` so the bounded table can't push the document past the viewport — the list page no longer grows a _second_ page scrollbar on top of the table's internal one (it fit the viewport before only by scrolling). Falls back to normal page scroll on short viewports. (2) **Follow button is icon-only**: `WatchButton` ([watch-buttons.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/models/[modelKey]/watch-buttons.tsx>)) dropped its "Follow / Following" face label (moved to `aria-label` + `title`) and is now `size="icon"`, matching the filter/sort icon buttons. (3) **Filter & sort sit with the search ; columns stays with the actions**: [`FilterBar`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/filter-bar.tsx) now renders the `tools` slot immediately right of the search field (grouped with it) instead of over on the right with the actions ; the primary CTA(s) keep the far-right `ml-auto` spot. `TableTools` gained an `include?: ToolId[]` prop so the cluster can be split — each list renders filters + sorting next to the search and a separate `include={["columns"]}` instance over with the right-hand actions (on the records list, right of the Follow bell), so the column selector lives with the actions rather than the query controls ([table-tools.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/table-tools.tsx)). Applied to **every** list screen — records, admin users / roles / webhooks / organizations / data-models (the read-only webhook-deliveries log, which has no search or actions, keeps its cluster together). A single-control `TableTools` renders inline on mobile too (no full-screen dialog), and the columns trigger now uses the `Columns3` glyph (matching the collapsed menu's columns row, which already did — fixing a small icon inconsistency). (4) **Uniform sizing**: with Follow now `size="icon"` (h-9 w-9), the toolbar's filter/sort/columns icon buttons (h-9 w-9) and the Create button (`size="default"`, h-9) all share one height. Verified the toolbar reposition + sizing via the `table-toolbar` screenshot story (sticky scroll behavior needs a running-app check — it's viewport-measured and doesn't reproduce in the isolated harness). `tsc` + `eslint` green.
- 2026-07-09: Data models — computed `FORMULA` field type + popover formula editor. A new safe (no `eval`) expression engine ([formula.ts](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/contracts/formula.ts): tokenizer → recursive-descent parser → tree-walking evaluator) supports column references (by field key), arithmetic (`+ - * / %`), string concat (`&`), comparisons, `&&`/`||`/`!`, and a Notion-like function library (`if`, `round`, `concat`, `dateAdd`, `datediff`, `upper`, `min`/`max`/`sum`, …). The engine is pure + isomorphic, so the **same** code evaluates server-side on write (`applyComputedFields` in [data.ts](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/server/data.ts) — the authoritative value persisted into `DataRecord.data`, so formula columns are filterable/sortable/indexable) and client-side for the live preview a formula field shows in the record form ([formula-field.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/fields/inputs/formula-field.tsx)). v1 is same-record only (no cross-record rollups). Formula keys are stripped from client input then recomputed; formulas that reference other formulas evaluate in dependency order (topological sort — a formula can even back the title field). At field-save time the expression is validated against the model: references must resolve to real sibling fields (never itself), and the formula dependency graph must stay acyclic (`A → B → A` rejected). `DataFieldType` gained `FORMULA` (migration [20260708020000](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/migrations/20260708020000_add_formula_field_type/migration.sql)); config is `{ expression, resultType }`. The schema builder gets a popover formula editor ([formula-config.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/data-models/[id]/formula-config.tsx>)) — a result-type picker, an expression textarea with click-to-insert column chips + a function reference, and a live syntax/reference validity check driven by the shared engine. New i18n (`…fieldDialog.config.formula.*`, en+fr). RBAC/events/webhooks unchanged (a formula rides existing record-write paths); Notifications + Feature-flags consciously N/A (a computed field surfaces no new user signal and is a low-risk additive opt-in type). 17 engine unit tests + 7 integration specs (compute-on-write, formula-of-formula ordering across reordered positions, recompute-on-update, text formula as title, unknown-ref/self-ref/cycle rejection). Full gate green (gen no-drift, tsc 17/17, eslint 17/17, unit 14/14 + data-models(54) integration, tiers/modules/i18n).
- 2026-07-08: Data models — record + model watchers with notifications (the engine's Notifications integration). A user can follow a single record (`DataRecordWatcher`) or a whole model (`DataModelWatcher`) — migration [20260708010000](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/migrations/20260708010000_add_data_watchers/migration.sql). On any `data-models.record-{created,updated,deleted}` event, a new subscriber ([record-watch-subscriber.ts](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/server/record-watch-subscriber.ts)) fans out an in-app `data-models.record-changed` notification (registered kind, IN_APP, opt-out-able — [notification-kinds.ts](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/server/notification-kinds.ts)) to the union of the record's + the model's watchers, minus the actor and minus anyone the record's row-level access hides it from. The record-deleted event now carries `recordTitle` (the row is gone by the time subscribers run on a hard delete). New tRPC `records.setWatch` / `isWatching` + `models.setWatch` / `isWatching` (gated on read access), and Follow/Following toggles in the record detail panel + full-page editor ([watch-buttons.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/models/[modelKey]/watch-buttons.tsx>)) and the model list header. `@monark/data-models` now depends on `@monark/notifications` (both core). New i18n (`data.records.watch.*`, en+fr). Integration coverage: watcher notified on change, actor excluded, model-watcher notified on create, no-access watcher excluded. Verified in the real UI (model + record follow persist across reload). Gate green (gen, tsc 17/17, eslint 17/17, unit + data-models(47) integration, tiers/modules/i18n).
- 2026-07-08: Data models — per-record access UI. The record detail panel + full-page editor now show an admin-only "Access" section ([record-access-section.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/models/[modelKey]/record-access-section.tsx>)) — a checkbox list of the org's roles (via `rbac.adminListRoles`), wired to `records.getAccess` / `records.setAccess`, mirroring the calendar role picker. Empty selection = visible to everyone who can access the model; picking roles restricts it (admins always bypass). Gated on `rbac.isAdmin`, so non-admins never see it. The full-page editor's model prop now carries `organizationId` for the roles query. New `data.records.access.*` i18n (en + fr). Verified by driving the real UI (grant a role → save → reload → persisted → restore); web tsc + eslint + 233 tests + check:i18n green.
- 2026-07-08: Data models — row-level (per-record) authorization. A new `DataRecordRoleAccess` join table ([schema.prisma](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/schema.prisma), migration [20260708000000](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/migrations/20260708000000_add_data_record_role_access/migration.sql)) mirrors `CalendarRoleAccess`: a `DataRecord` with no rows is visible to everyone who can access its model (the default — fully backward-compatible), with rows only the listed roles. Enforcement layers on top of the existing model-level permission check — `listDataRecords` filters by the caller's role ids (`recordRoleAccessWhere`), and `records.getById` / `update` / `delete` / `restore` 404 a restricted record for callers whose roles aren't listed (so existence doesn't leak) ([data.ts](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/server/data.ts), [router.ts](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/server/router.ts)). Data admins (`data-models.manage-schema`, which ADMIN/SYSADMIN short-circuit) bypass the row filter; a blanket `data-models.record-read` does not — row-level is real privacy. `requireModelAccess` now returns the resolved org id so the record procedures build the caller's role-access context (`getUserRoles` + the manage-schema bypass, mirroring calendar). New `records.getAccess` / `records.setAccess` procedures (gated on `manage-schema`) read/replace a record's role list. The authorization integration suite gained 6 row-level specs (restricted-record hidden/404'd for a non-gating role, visible to a gating role, generic-read does NOT bypass, admin bypass, setAccess/getAccess round-trip). No admin UI yet to pick per-record roles (tracked in the README's Deferred). Full gate green (gen, tsc 17/17, eslint 17/17, unit + rbac(40)/webhooks(38)/data-models(43) integration, tiers/modules/i18n).
- 2026-07-07: Data models — per-model RBAC + webhook entries are now multi-tenant-safe. The per-model permissions / event types stay globally registered (so `isKnownPermission` + grant-time validation pass, and a same-named model key across orgs correctly maps to one shared, org-enforced permission), but are marked `orgScoped` and filtered at display time: the `/admin/rbac` catalog ([index.ts](monark/app/packages/rbac/src/server/index.ts)) and the webhook picker ([router.ts](monark/app/packages/webhooks/src/server/router.ts)) only show an org the org-scoped entries a registered visibility resolver reports for it. New generic hooks — `registerOrgScopedPermissionVisibility` / `orgVisiblePermissionKeys` ([permissions.ts](monark/app/packages/rbac/src/contracts/permissions.ts)) and `registerOrgScopedEventTypeVisibility` / `orgVisibleEventTypes` ([event-registry.ts](monark/app/packages/common/src/event-registry.ts)) — keep `@monark/rbac` + `@monark/common` ignorant of `data-models`; `registerDataModelVisibilityResolvers()` ([registrations.ts](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/server/registrations.ts), wired at boot in [server.ts](monark/app/services/api/src/server.ts)) returns exactly the caller-org's live models' keys, so one org never sees another org's model keys. Closes the multi-tenant caveat noted when per-model integration first landed. New integration coverage asserts the cross-org isolation + soft-delete-hides. Full gate green (gen, tsc 17/17, eslint 17/17, unit + rbac/webhooks/data-models/common integration, tiers/modules/i18n).
- 2026-07-07: Data models — each model auto-integrates into RBAC + webhooks as it's created. On create (refreshed on rename) a model now registers three per-model record permissions (`data-models.<key>-record-{read,write,delete}`) and three per-model event types (`data-models.<key>-record-{created,updated,deleted}`), grouped under `Data Model: <name>` in the `/admin/rbac` catalog and the webhook subscription picker ([registrations.ts](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/server/registrations.ts)). `requireModelAccess` now accepts the per-model permission OR the generic `data-models.record-*` (admins short-circuit both), so a role can be scoped to one model's records without the blanket grant; existing grants are unaffected. The engine keeps emitting the generic `data-models.record-*` event but tags it with the per-model type via a new generic `DomainEventBase.subscriptionAliases` field ([events.ts](monark/app/packages/common/src/contracts/events.ts)); the webhooks matcher ([data.ts](monark/app/packages/webhooks/src/server/data.ts), [subscribers.ts](monark/app/packages/webhooks/src/server/subscribers.ts)) now matches on `[type, ...aliases]`, routing the generic emit to a per-model subscription (per-model event literals can't join the codegen'd `DomainEvent` union). The in-memory registries are re-hydrated from the DB at api boot via `hydrateDataModelRegistrations` on the async boot path ([server.ts](monark/app/services/api/src/server.ts)), next to `syncFlagsToDatabase`. Notifications + feature-flags are a conscious N/A (the engine emits events; notification-worthy reactions belong to subscribing modules, and the change is additive/non-breaking so no gate). Also fixed a soft-delete regression from the org-required change: [20260707060000_data_models_key_unique_partial](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/migrations/20260707060000_data_models_key_unique_partial/migration.sql) swaps the plain `@@unique([organizationId, key])` back to a hand-written partial unique (`WHERE deletedAt IS NULL`), so a soft-deleted model frees its key for reuse as the data layer already assumes. New unit + integration coverage (per-model registration, alias routing); full gate green (gen, tsc 17/17, eslint 17/17, 233 unit + data-models/webhooks integration, tiers/modules/i18n).
- 2026-07-07: Data models — every Data Model is now org-scoped; platform-wide (null-org) models are disallowed. `DataModel.organizationId` and `DataRecord.organizationId` became non-null and the two NULL-aware partial-unique indexes on `(organizationId, key)` collapsed into a single `@@unique([organizationId, key])` ([schema.prisma](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/schema.prisma)); the [20260707050000_data_models_org_required](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/migrations/20260707050000_data_models_org_required/migration.sql) migration re-parents any existing null-org models + records to the singleton organization before enforcing NOT NULL (single-tenant deploy; a multi-tenant one would need an explicit target org per model). The data layer ([data.ts](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/server/data.ts)) drops `organizationId: string | null` throughout — `findFreeDataModelKey`, `findDataModelByKey`, `CreateDataModelInput`, and `listDataModels`'s `where` (no more `OR: [{organizationId}, {organizationId: null}]`) — and the router ([router.ts](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/server/router.ts)) drops the platform branch from `requireModelAccess`, the `platform` input on `getByKey`, and the `scope` enum on `create`, which now always resolves the caller's active org. Web callers simplify to a single org-scoped `getByKey` ([resolve-model.ts](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/models/[modelKey]/resolve-model.ts>), records-list, record-full-page), and the admin create form drops its scope picker + the now-dead scope column and its six i18n keys (en + fr stay at parity). `pnpm gen` + `tsc` (17/17) + `eslint` (17/17) + 233 web tests + `check:tiers` / `check:modules` / `check:i18n` green.
- 2026-07-07: Projects / Industries — dropped the entire legacy module in favor of the polymorphic Data Models engine. The bespoke `@monark/projects` package (its Prisma models `Project` / `Industry` / `ProjectContributor` / the `_ProjectIndustries` join + `ProjectPublicStatus` enum, tRPC router, permissions, and event types) is gone, along with its two web surfaces (`/data/projects`, `/data/industries`), their global-search sections + navigation entries ([global-search-dialog.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/global-search/global-search-dialog.tsx), [routes.ts](monark/app/services/web/src/components/global-search/routes.ts)), and every projects/industries i18n key (en + fr stay at parity). The `@monark/projects` manifest tier and its `register*` calls in [server.ts](monark/app/services/api/src/server.ts) were removed, and the module's `check-modules` acknowledged-gap entry deleted. The DB drop ships as a hand-written migration ([20260707043022_drop_legacy_projects_industries](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/migrations/20260707043022_drop_legacy_projects_industries/migration.sql)) — authored by hand rather than via `migrate dev` so it drops the (non-empty) tables non-interactively and deliberately leaves the hand-maintained `DataRecord_data_gin` index alone. `/data` now server-redirects to the first registered Data Model (empty state if the org has none) since `DATA_TABS` is empty and every tab is a dynamically-registered model. Industries data was migrated into a platform "Industries" Data Model beforehand ; the legacy `Industry` rows are dropped by this change. `tsc` (web + api) + `eslint` + `check:tiers` + `check:modules` + `check:i18n` + 233 web tests green.

- 2026-07-06: Data models — reorder fields by dragging instead of up/down arrows. The model editor's field list ([model-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/data-models/[id]/model-editor.tsx>)) swapped its per-row `ArrowUp` / `ArrowDown` buttons for a `GripVertical` drag handle, wired with `@dnd-kit` (`DndContext` + `SortableContext` + `verticalListSortingStrategy` + pointer / keyboard sensors) exactly like the DataTable's columns / sorting panels. The field list is now held in local state so a drop reorders optimistically (re-synced from the query on every change), persisting via the existing `dataModels.fields.reorder` mutation ; a new `onError` re-invalidates so a failed reorder doesn't stick. New `fields.dragToReorder` i18n + a `fields.reorderError` toast string (en + fr) ; dropped the now-unused `fields.moveUp` / `fields.moveDown`. `tsc` + `eslint` + `check:i18n` + 233 tests green.

- 2026-07-06: Calendar — narrower hour-label gutter on mobile. The day view's left timeline column was a fixed `w-16` (64px), which eats scarce horizontal room on a phone where the single merged event column is already tight. It's now `w-12` (48px) below Tailwind's `md` breakpoint and back to `w-16` at `md`+ ([day-timeline.tsx](monark/app/packages/calendar/src/client/ui/day-timeline.tsx)), with the day view's sticky all-day-row spacer given the same `w-12 md:w-16` so the gutter and the event column stay aligned at every width ([day-view.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/calendar/day-view.tsx>)). The `md` breakpoint (768px) matches `useIsMobile` exactly — and since the calendar forces every view to the day view below it, the desktop-only week view (which assumes a 64px gutter for its column math) is unaffected. The 10px hour labels ("23:00") still fit comfortably in 48px. `tsc` + `eslint` green (calendar package + touched web file).
- 2026-07-06: DataTable — row-actions "…" menu no longer breaks the page layout on mobile. [Reported](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/data-table.tsx): opening a row's action menu on a phone-width viewport visually shifted the whole page (header, nav, everything) instead of just the menu appearing. Root cause : `RowActionsMenu`'s `<DropdownMenu>` was Radix's default _modal_, which drives a body scroll-lock (`overflow: hidden`) on open ; on mobile a table with several columns is already a little wider than the viewport, silently masked by the page's own `overflow-x: clip`, and the scroll-lock's style swap disturbed that masking, blowing the layout viewport out to the table's full width. Fixed with `modal={false}` on the row-actions menu — same class of fix as the `CalendarChip` menu ([[radix-pointer-events-freeze]]) — since this menu never opens a follow-up modal itself, there's no menu→dialog handoff to worry about, and non-modal also means it can no longer contribute to that separate pointer-events-freeze failure mode either. `tsc` + `eslint` + 233 web tests green ; verified with Playwright at an iPhone-13 viewport (the layout viewport measurement returned to matching the true screen width across open/close, where before it inflated on open).

- 2026-07-06: Data / projects list — the "Updated" relative-time column now respects the app's locale instead of the browser/OS default. Both [data-models-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/data-models/data-models-list.tsx>) and [projects-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/projects/projects-list.tsx>) hand-rolled their own `formatRelative` via `new Intl.RelativeTimeFormat(undefined, …)`, which resolves against the environment's default locale rather than the session's chosen one — on a machine with a non-English OS locale this rendered French ("il y a 4 minutes") inside an otherwise-English UI. Replaced both with the existing [`formatRelativeTime(value, locale)`](monark/app/services/web/src/lib/format-time.ts) helper (already used by the notifications bell, account pages, etc.) fed `useLocale()` from `next-intl`, removing ~34 lines of duplicated bucket logic. `tsc` + `eslint` + 233 web tests green.

- 2026-07-06: Data Models — admin schema-builder + generic record browsing UI, plus two bugs caught in manual verification. Fifth slice of [`@monark/data-models`](monark/app/packages/data-models), landing the frontend on top of the four backend phases : a [`DataField` → `FieldDef` adapter](monark/app/services/web/src/components/fields/data-field-adapter.ts) (`dataFieldToFieldDef`, `recordDataToDefaultValues`) maps a polymorphic field 1:1 onto the existing fields toolkit, so the admin schema editor and the generic record UI reuse `AutoForm` / `fieldColumn` rather than hand-rolling per-type forms. `/admin/data-models` ([data-models-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/data-models/data-models-list.tsx>)) is the model list + create panel on the standard `DataTable`/`FilterBar`/`TableDetailLayout` shell ; `/admin/data-models/[id]` ([model-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/data-models/[id]/model-editor.tsx>)) is a full-page editor for settings (name, description, title field), fields (add/edit/reorder/archive/index via a [`FieldEditorDialog`](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/data-models/[id]/field-editor-dialog.tsx>) covering all 12 field types' config sub-forms), and a registry-driven Integrations section that renders whatever `dataModels.integrations.listAvailable` returns (Calendar's `time`/`calendarRef` slots today) without hardcoding any module name. `/data/models/[modelKey]` ([records-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/models/[modelKey]/records-list.tsx>)) is the generic record list + detail panel, gated on `data-models.record-read` / `read-schema` ; every dynamically-registered Data Model gets a sidebar tab alongside the static Projects/Industries entries ([data/layout.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/layout.tsx>)).

  Manual end-to-end verification (fresh signup → TOTP enrollment → create a model → add text/number/select fields → set a title field → browse and create/edit a record, driven with Playwright against the real dev stack) caught two real bugs the type checker couldn't : (1) `/data/layout.tsx`, a Server Component, was passing Lucide icon _components_ as a prop into the client `DataSidebar`/`DataTabsBar` for the per-model dynamic tabs — Next.js forbids functions crossing the Server→Client serialization boundary, which hard-crashed the entire `/data` section (including the pre-existing Projects/Industries screens) the moment any Data Model existed. Fixed by carrying a serializable icon key instead (`DataTabIcon = "folderKanban" | "tags" | "database"` in [data-tabs.ts](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/data-tabs.ts>)) and resolving it back to a component in a client-only `TAB_ICONS` map in [data-sidebar.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/data-sidebar.tsx>). (2) The `/admin/data-models/[id]` breadcrumb showed the raw cuid instead of the model's name, because [app-bar-breadcrumb.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/app-bar-breadcrumb.tsx)'s per-pattern dispatch (`pickCrumbForSegment`) never got a case for this new route — added a `DataModelNameCrumb`, matching the existing `UserNameCrumb`/`OrgNameCrumb`/`RoleNameCrumb`/`WebhookNameCrumb` pattern (resolve via `dataModels.models.getById`, fall back to the id while loading/on error). New `tests/lib/data-field-adapter.test.ts` (11 tests) ; full pre-PR gate (`gen` + `typecheck` + `lint` + `test` + `check:tiers` + `check:modules` + `check:i18n`) green across all 18 workspace packages.

- 2026-07-05: Data Models — per-field expression indexing. Fourth slice of [`@monark/data-models`](monark/app/packages/data-models) : `fields.requestIndex` / `fields.indexStatus` on `dataModelsRouter`, backed by a new `server/indexing.ts`. Provisions a type-appropriate `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` on `DataRecord` — a B-tree over the extracted-and-cast value for scalar types (`::numeric` / `::boolean` / `::timestamptz` / plain text), a GIN over the raw jsonb array for `MULTI_SELECT`/`RELATION` — scoped to the field's own Data Model via a partial `WHERE "dataModelId" = '…' AND "deletedAt" IS NULL`. `CONCURRENTLY` can't run inside a transaction and can take a while on a large table, so `requestFieldIndex` returns `"pending"` immediately and the build runs fire-and-forget in the background, tracked through the existing `DataFieldIndex` table's `status` column (`pending → building → ready`/`failed`) ; `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY IF NOT EXISTS` plus a status short-circuit make repeated requests idempotent. This is v1's complete answer to "fast filtering on any admin-defined field" per the original design : the baseline GIN index (already shipped in the first migration) makes every field filterable _correctly_ from day one, speed at scale is opt-in per hot field. New integration suite (`tests/integration/indexing.test.ts`, 4 tests) verifies the actual Postgres DDL — index method via `pg_indexes.indexdef`, and a live query using the cast expression returns correct results — since none of this is meaningfully testable with a mocked client. Full pre-PR gate green.
- 2026-07-05: Data Models — module integration registry + Calendar materialization. Third slice of [`@monark/data-models`](monark/app/packages/data-models) : `registerModelIntegration(module, { slots, description })` in `contracts/integrations.ts`, mirroring the `registerPermissions`/`registerFlags` idiom — a module declares named "slots" (a type constraint, required-ness, and for `RELATION` a `relationTarget`) and an admin maps a Data Model's own fields onto them per-model via new `trpc.dataModels.integrations.{listAvailable,get,save}` procedures. `upsertModelIntegration` validates the mapping (unknown module, wrong field type, mismatched `relationTarget`, or a required slot unmapped while `enabled: true` all reject) before persisting to the existing `DataModelIntegration` table ; `DataModelSchemaChangedEvent.kind` gained an `"integration"` variant. This is the "mapping, not reserved field keys" mechanism the whole engine is designed around ([polymorphic-db.md](monark/app/docs/features-planning/phase-2/polymorphic-db.md)) — nothing in the generic engine hardcodes any consuming module's name or field-key vocabulary.

  First real consumer : [`@monark/calendar`](monark/app/packages/calendar) now declares two slots (`time`: DATE/DATETIME ; `calendarRef`: RELATION targeting `Calendar`) via `registerCalendarModelIntegration()`, and `registerCalendarDataModelSubscriber()` listens for `data-models.record-{created,updated,deleted}` to upsert / soft- or hard-delete a real `CalendarEvent`, keyed on two new additive columns — `CalendarEvent.sourceModule` / `sourceRecordId` (migration `20260706010000_add_calendar_event_source`, `@@unique` on the pair ; Postgres treats NULL as distinct so ordinary hand-created events, both columns null, are never constrained). Materialized events are `eventType: "PUNCTUAL"` with `startAt === endAt` (the integration maps one "time" slot, not a start/end pair) ; a record that stops satisfying an enabled mapping gets its event soft-deleted, not orphaned. Calendar's own read paths (day view, reminder sweep) need zero changes. `@monark/calendar` gained `@monark/data-models` and (a pre-existing, previously-accidental) `@monark/notifications` as declared dependencies — the latter was imported by `notification-kinds.ts` without ever being listed in `package.json`, working only by hoisting-order luck until this phase's first real vitest module-graph resolution caught it.

  Also fixed along the way : `valueSchemaFor`'s `DATE`/`DATETIME` branch only accepted real `Date` instances, but `DataRecord.data` is JSON — a `Date` written on create survives only as an ISO string on the next read, so `updateDataRecord`'s re-validation of the merged (DB-read) object failed on every date field. Now preprocesses either a `Date` or a valid date string into a `Date` before validating, so both the client (real `Date`s from a picker) and the server (strings from storage) validate correctly. New integration suites : `packages/data-models/tests/integration/integrations.test.ts` (7 tests, slot-mapping validation) and `packages/calendar/tests/integration/data-model-subscriber.test.ts` (5 tests, first integration suite for `@monark/calendar` — it previously had none), plus new `vitest.config.ts`/`vitest.integration.config.ts` for both packages (data-models was missing its unit/integration split entirely until this phase, which briefly caused the integration suite to run — and collide — inside the plain unit `test` script). Full pre-PR gate green across all 18 workspace packages.

- 2026-07-05: Data Models — `DataRecord` CRUD. Second slice of [`@monark/data-models`](monark/app/packages/data-models) (see the prior schema-management entry below) : `records.{list,getById,create,update,delete,restore}` on `dataModelsRouter`, gated by three new permissions — `data-models.record-read` / `record-write` / `record-delete` (hyphenated, not dotted — the rbac registry's key regex forbids `.` inside a key). A record's `data` payload is validated against its model's active fields via the shared `valueSchemaForField` (unknown keys stripped, matching zod's default `z.object()` behavior) ; `update` re-validates the full merged object (existing `data` overlaid with the patch), not just the patched keys, so a partial edit can't silently violate a required field. `DataRecord.title` is denormalized and recomputed on every write from the model's `titleFieldId` (falls back to `"Untitled"` when unset or empty ; `RICH_TEXT` values are stripped to plain text via the newly-exported `stripHtmlTags`, with whitespace collapsed so tag-stripping doesn't leave doubled spaces). `restore` is gated by `record-write`, matching the existing `projects.restore` / `industries.restore` convention (undoing a delete reads as an edit, not a deletion) — the schema-management phase's `models.restore` didn't need this distinction since it only has one permission tier. New `data-models.record-{created,updated,deleted}` events, carrying `dataModelKey` alongside `dataModelId` so a future subscriber (e.g. Calendar's planned materialization subscriber) can filter without a round-trip lookup. New integration suite (`tests/integration/records.test.ts`, 9 tests) covering validation, title derivation, partial-merge re-validation, slug dedup, and soft-delete/restore/hard-delete ; full pre-PR gate (`gen` + `typecheck` + `lint` + `test` + `check:tiers` + `check:modules` + `check:i18n`) green across all 18 workspace packages.
- 2026-07-05: Data Models — new core module, schema management phase. Added [`@monark/data-models`](monark/app/packages/data-models) (core tier, [modules.manifest.ts](monark/app/modules.manifest.ts)), the first slice of the polymorphic "Data Model" engine described in [docs/features-planning/phase-2/polymorphic-db.md](monark/app/docs/features-planning/phase-2/polymorphic-db.md). Prisma models `DataModel` / `DataField` / `DataRecord` / `DataModelIntegration` / `DataFieldIndex` under a new `// ── MODULE: data-models ──` banner ([schema.prisma](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/schema.prisma), migration `20260706000312_add_data_models`, hand-augmented with two NULL-aware partial-unique indexes — `DataModel` keys are unique per-org or globally when platform-wide — plus a baseline GIN index on `DataRecord.data`). This phase ships `DataModel` + `DataField` CRUD only (`dataModelsRouter` at `trpc.dataModels.{models,fields}.*`), gated by new `data-models.manage-schema` / `read-schema` permissions ; `DataRecord` CRUD and the module-integration registry land in later phases. The field-type validation logic that used to live only in [`services/web/src/components/fields/schema.ts`](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)>) is now shared : [`valueSchemaFor`](monark/app/packages/data-models/src/contracts/field-types.ts) in `@monark/data-models/contracts` builds the structural zod fragment for a field's value from its type + config, and the client's `schemaFor` is now a thin adapter onto it — one implementation instead of two hand-synced ones, verified by the existing 23-test `fields.test.ts` suite passing unchanged. New integration suite (`packages/data-models/tests/integration/data.test.ts`, 9 tests) locks in the partial-unique-index behavior against a real Postgres testcontainer. `registerDataModelsPermissions()` / `registerDataModelsEventTypes()` wired into [server.ts](monark/app/services/api/src/server.ts). `tsc` + `eslint` + `check:tiers` + `check:modules` green.
- 2026-07-05: Data-list config — "Reset columns", and reset actions only show when there's something to reset. Renamed the columns-panel reset from "Reset layout" to **"Reset columns"** (`table.reset`, en + fr) and narrowed [`useDataTableLayout`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/use-data-table-layout.ts)'s `reset()` to the _column_ dimensions only (order / visibility / widths) — sorting keeps its own "Reset sorting" — plus a new `columnsModified` flag. Every reset action is now gated on having something to reset : the columns reset shows only when `columnsModified` ; the all-filters "Reset filters" (top-level dropdown, mobile modal footer, collapsed drill-in, mobile dialog) renders only when `activeFilterCount > 0` instead of showing perpetually disabled ; the per-field filter reset and the sorting reset were already gated (`isFilterActive` / active-sorts). `FilterClearButton` dropped its now-dead `disabled` branch ([filter-menu.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/filter-menu.tsx), [table-tools.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/table-tools.tsx)). `tsc` + `eslint` + `check:i18n` + 222 tests green.

- 2026-07-05: DataTable — the whole row is now clickable, not just the primary label. A click anywhere in a row activates its primary column ([data-table.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/data-table.tsx)) — `router.push` on the primary `href`, else `primaryColumn.onSelect` — so opening a record no longer requires hitting the (often short) label. Rows only get the affordance (`cursor-pointer`) when the primary is actually actionable for that row (a link `href` or an `onSelect` ; e.g. archived rows with a `undefined` href stay inert). The handler yields to any real control inside the row (the primary link, the row-actions `…` button, inline links / inputs — matched via `closest(...)`) and bails on a text-selection drag, and the `…` trigger button now `stopPropagation`s its click so opening the menu never also activates the row. Inline links like the projects URL cell keep opening in their new tab without selecting the row. `tsc` + `eslint` green.

- 2026-07-05: Data / projects — the projects table's URL column is now a clickable link instead of muted plain text. The cell rendered `p.url` as a `text-muted-foreground` span ([projects-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/projects/projects-list.tsx>)) ; it now wraps it in an `<a target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">` styled as a primary link (keeping the mono truncation). Safe because the field is stored through `z.string().url()`, so the value always carries a scheme (no relative-link pitfall), and the row's primary label still owns row selection so the link doesn't clash with opening the detail panel. Industries has no URL column, so this was the only data-section URL field. `tsc` + `eslint` + 222 tests green.

- 2026-07-05: Feature flags — fixed the dev-overlay panel showing no flags, and made it session-scoped. The panel computed its key list from a client-side `listFlagKeys()` ([feature-flags.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/dev-overlay/panels/feature-flags.tsx)), but the flag registry is populated only by `registerFlags(...)` at api boot (server-side), so in the browser it was always empty — the panel queried `getMany({ keys: [] })` and `getFlags` short-circuits an empty list to `{}`, rendering nothing. Added an auth-gated [`featureFlags.getAllForSession`](monark/app/packages/feature-flags/src/server/index.ts) procedure that resolves _every_ registered flag at the caller's own session scope (`userId` + `activeOrganizationId`, derived from `ctx`, never trusted from input) and returns the resolved map ; the panel now derives its keys from that response and shows flags as they actually resolve for the logged-in user, rather than the previous empty-scope defaults (which the `/admin/feature-flags` page already covers). Updated the panel's scope note + added an `empty` string (en + fr) ; `pnpm gen:routers` re-run. `tsc` + `eslint` + feature-flags (21) + web (222) tests green.

- 2026-07-05: Filters — sticky search header inset to match, and standardized the data-list "New" CTAs. The filter field's sticky option-search now uses the same inset separator as the reset row (a padded `bg-border` rule) instead of a full-bleed `border-b` : both `DesktopOptionSearch` / `MobileOptionSearch` moved into an opaque `px-1.5` band with the shared `INSET_SEPARATOR_CLASS` (renamed from `RESET_SEPARATOR_CLASS` since it's shared by both the search and reset now) in [filter-menu.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/filter-menu.tsx). Separately, the "New industry" button was `size="sm"` (smaller `text-xs`) while "New project" used the default size ; dropped the `size="sm"` so they match, and removed the redundant `mr-1 h-4 w-4` icon styling on both (the `Button` already sets `gap-2` + `[&_svg]:size-4`), matching the plain `<Plus className="h-4 w-4" />` convention the admin CTAs already use ([industries-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/industries/industries-list.tsx>), [projects-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/projects/projects-list.tsx>)). `tsc` + `eslint` + 222 tests green.

- 2026-07-05: Filters — every reset row now reads identically, matching the data-table Sort / Columns config. Previously the three surfaces diverged : the top-level "Reset filters" used a full-bleed `DropdownMenuSeparator`, the per-field sub-menu reset was a flush `rounded-none` `border-t` item with no padding, and only the Sort / Columns panels had the preferred look (an _inset_ separator over a padded button). Standardized on the Sort / Columns style everywhere via shared `RESET_ROW_CLASS` / `RESET_SEPARATOR_CLASS` in [filter-menu.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/filter-menu.tsx) : the top-level reset swaps its separator for an inset `bg-border` rule ; the sticky per-field resets (desktop sub-menu `DropdownMenuItem` and mobile/collapsed `FilterClearButton`/plain button) now sit in an opaque sticky _band_ whose `px-1.5` insets the separator + button to line up with the Sort / Columns `p-1.5` popovers ; and `FilterClearButton` became a plain padded button (was a ghost `Button`). Dropped the now-unused `DropdownMenuSeparator` import. `tsc` + `eslint` + 222 tests green ; verified against the `filter-menu-clear` story.

- 2026-07-05: Filters — the per-field "Reset" is now pinned to the bottom of the option list. Mirroring the sticky option-search at the top, a filter field's "Reset" action now sticks to the bottom of its scrollable control with an opaque surface background, so on a long option list (e.g. dozens of industries) you can clear that one field without scrolling to the end to reach it. Applied in both the desktop field sub-menu (`DesktopFilterControl` — the `DropdownMenuItem` gains `sticky bottom-0` + `bg-popover` + a top border, replacing the plain separator) and the mobile / collapsed control (`FilterFieldControl` — the reset `Button` gains `sticky bottom-0` + its host surface bg) in [filter-menu.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/filter-menu.tsx). The `filter-menu-clear` story now uses an 18-option field to exercise it. `tsc` + `eslint` + 222 tests green.

- 2026-07-05: Data table — the three toolbar config menus (Filter / Sort / Columns) now carry a small title so an opened menu names itself. Added a shared [`PanelTitle`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/panel-title.tsx) (one place, so the three read identically) rendered at the top of each standalone desktop menu : the filter dropdown ([filter-menu.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/filter-menu.tsx), titled from `labels.title ?? labels.trigger`) and the sort + columns popovers ([table-tools.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/table-tools.tsx), titled from `labels.sort.label` / `labels.columns`). It lives in its own neutral module rather than `data-table/panels` so the lower-level filter menu doesn't have to import up into the data-table layer. The mobile modals + collapsed drill-in views already named their sections (`h3` / `SubmenuHeader`), so they're unchanged. Also dropped the "No sorting" empty-state line that sat above "Add sort field" in the sorting panel ([panels.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/panels.tsx)) — it read oddly under the new title ; when nothing is sorted the panel now goes straight to the add-field list (its leading divider only renders when there are active sorts above it). The `none` label is still used as the collapsed sorting row's summary. `tsc` + `eslint` + 222 tests green.

Every filter/sort reset (the all-filters "Reset filters", the per-field "Reset" in each field's sub-menu, and the "Reset sorting" action) now uses the `RotateCcw` reload icon and full-opacity text, matching the columns panel's "Reset layout" row instead of the earlier greyed-out `FilterX` treatment ([filter-menu.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/filter-menu.tsx), [panels.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/panels.tsx)). Renamed the filter menu's all-clear from "Clear filters" to **"Reset filters"** to match the data-table config's "Reset layout" verb (`filters.clearAll` text, en + fr). Added a **per-field reset** so a single filter can be cleared in one click instead of deselecting each value : [`DesktopFilterControl`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/filter-menu.tsx) appends a "Reset" `DropdownMenuItem` (kept-open) and `FilterFieldControl` a "Reset" button, both shown only while that field is active, driven by the existing `clearFilter` + a new optional `FilterMenuLabels.resetField` (`filters.resetField` = "Reset" / "Réinitialiser"). Added a **"Reset sorting"** action at the bottom of the [`SortingPanel`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/panels.tsx) that clears every sort field at once (`setSorting([])`), gated on there being active sorts, via a new optional `DataTableSortLabels.reset` (`table.sortReset`, en + fr). Threaded the two new labels through all filter callers (projects, industries, users, webhooks) and every sort-label builder (those four + organizations, roles, deliveries, dev gallery). `filter-menu-clear` screenshot story updated (mobile shows the per-field "Reset" on the active field + the "Reset filters" footer). `tsc` + `eslint` + `check:i18n` + 222 tests green.

- 2026-07-05: Data table — reorderable column headers now show a grip on hover so it's clear they can be dragged, not just that the cursor changes. Added a `GripVertical` affordance to [`SortableHeader`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/data-table.tsx) that fades in on header hover / focus (reusing the same `group/th` reveal the idle sort-arrow hint already uses). It sits in the header's left padding gutter and is `pointer-events-none` + `aria-hidden` — purely indicative, since the whole `HeaderLabel` already owns the dnd-kit drag listeners — so it can't shift the header text out of alignment with the body cells or interfere with the drag / sort click. The pinned primary + actions columns (`StaticHeader`, non-reorderable) don't get it. `tsc` + `eslint` + 222 tests green.

- 2026-07-05: Filters — a primary-colored dot now marks active filters so they stand out at a glance. Added [`FilterActiveDot`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/filter-menu.tsx) (a small `bg-primary` dot, rendering an empty fixed-width slot when the filter is inactive so labels stay aligned), placed before the field label in all three filter surfaces : the desktop `FilterMenu` dropdown rows, the mobile filter modal's section headers, and the collapsed tools popover's [`FilterFieldNavRow`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/table-tools.tsx). Active-ness reuses the existing `isFilterActive` predicate, so every filter type (select / multi-select / text / date) lights up consistently. Verified via the `filter-menu-clear` screenshot story (the active "Status" field shows the dot ; the untouched "Tags" field doesn't). `tsc` + `eslint` + 222 tests green.
- 2026-07-05: Fields — a field-type glyph now sits before each field name in the data section's tables and forms, so a reader can tell short text from a select from a URL at a glance. Added [`FIELD_TYPE_ICON`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/fields/field-icons.tsx), a `FieldType → lucide glyph` map (text→Type, longText→AlignLeft, richText→Pilcrow, number→Hash, boolean→ToggleLeft, date→Calendar, datetime→CalendarClock, singleSelect→CircleDot, multiSelect→Tags, relation→Waypoints, url→Link, email→AtSign). The toolkit renders it automatically: [`FieldShell`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/fields/field-shell.tsx) + [`BooleanField`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/fields/inputs/boolean-field.tsx) show it before the form label, and [`fieldColumn`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/fields/field-column.ts) before the table header (opt out with `headerIcon: null`). For hand-rolled surfaces the shared primitives gained an icon slot: [`DataColumnDef`/`PrimaryColumnDef.headerIcon`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/types.ts) (rendered in the table's `HeaderLabel`, carried through TanStack `columnDef.meta`) and [`FieldRow.icon`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/field-row.tsx). Wired the projects + industries list columns and panel forms ([projects-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/projects/projects-list.tsx>), [project-form.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/projects/project-form.tsx>), [industries-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/industries/industries-list.tsx>)). Verified with the `fields-table` + `fields-gallery` screenshot stories. `tsc` + `eslint` green.

Resets every active filter to its neutral value (select → default, multi-select → empty, text / date → empty) in one click. Added [`clearAllFilters`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/filter-menu.tsx) + a shared `FilterClearButton`, and an optional `FilterMenuLabels.clearAll` label (omit to hide). Rendered in all four filter surfaces : the desktop `FilterMenu` dropdown (a `DropdownMenuItem` below a separator, disabled while nothing's active, keeps the menu open so the cleared state is visible), the mobile filter modal footer, and the `TableTools` collapsed-tools drill-in popover + mobile dialog. Wired the four list screens (projects, industries, users, webhooks) off a new `filters.clearAll` i18n key ("Clear filters" / "Effacer les filtres", en + fr). New `filter-menu-clear` screenshot story (mobile modal verified). `tsc` + `eslint` + `check:i18n` + 222 tests green.

- 2026-07-05: Filters — a multi-select filter's summary count now reads "N Active" instead of a bare "N". On a filter field row (the desktop filter dropdown and the collapsed tools popover), a multi-select with selected values showed just the number of picks, which didn't explain what the number meant. [`filterSummary`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/filter-menu.tsx) now takes an optional `labels.valueCount` formatter and renders it (e.g. "3 Active") ; `FilterMenuLabels` gained the optional `valueCount?: (count: number) => string`, threaded through the desktop `FilterMenu` rows and the collapsed [`FilterFieldNavRow`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/table-tools.tsx). Wired the two screens with multi-select filters — projects (status + industries) and admin users (role + status) — off a new shared `filters.activeValues` i18n key ("{count} Active" / "{count} actifs", en + fr) ; screens that omit the formatter fall back to the bare count. `tsc` + `eslint` + 222 tests + `check:i18n` green.
- 2026-07-05: Notifications — broke notification preferences down to per-event email opt-in, mirroring the RBAC / webhook selectors. The `/account/notifications` UI ([notification-prefs.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/notification-prefs.tsx)) dropped the two-switch-per-row (in-app + email) layout for the shared [`GroupedMultiSelect`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/grouped-multi-select.tsx) in checkbox mode : one email checkbox left of each event's label + description, collapsible category groups, search, and tri-state select-all. In-app delivery is no longer user-configurable (platform-controlled), so only email-capable kinds render ; `requiredEmail` security kinds show as checked + disabled with a "required" hint (a new minimal `isItemDisabled` predicate + `monoItems` group flag on the shared component ; the two callers now pass `onToggle(kind, enabled)` with the channel hardcoded to `EMAIL`). Added four new security notification kinds, each wired to a real trigger : `auth.signed-in` (opt-in, default-off, on every `user.signed-in`), `auth.device-revoked` (single-device sign-out receipt on `trusted-device.revoked`, skipping the per-row events of a bulk sweep), `auth.recovery-code-used` (forced, on `totp.recovery-code-used`), and `auth.recovery-codes-regenerated` (forced ; a **new** `totp.recovery-codes-regenerated` domain event now emitted from [`regenerateRecoveryCodes`](monark/app/packages/auth/src/server/totp.ts) + registered for webhooks). Registry augmentation, en/fr templates, defs, and `enrich` cases land in [packages/notifications](monark/app/packages/notifications) ; the bulk-suppression flag (`bulk?: boolean` on `TrustedDeviceRevokedEvent`, set by [`revokeAllTrustedDevices`](monark/app/packages/auth/src/server/trusted-devices.ts)) keeps a "revoke all" from firing N device-revoked emails alongside the one `auth.all-devices-revoked`. i18n (kind labels + per-event `kindDescriptions`, en + fr), the notifications + auth READMEs, and `pnpm gen:events` all updated. As part of this the shared [`GroupedMultiSelect`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/grouped-multi-select.tsx) — the one selector behind all three of the RBAC role editor, the webhook subscription picker, and notification prefs — absorbed the group count-chip format as a built-in default (`"{selected} / {total}"`), so the three sections dropped their duplicated, drifting count strings (`categoryCount` / `moduleSelectionCount` / `count`) and now read identically ; a new opt-in `secondaryPrefix` slot lets a row lead its description with a marker (used here for the `Required` badge on forced rows, so the reason the checkbox is locked sits right next to it).

- 2026-07-05: Layout — page content now docks next to the sidebar instead of floating at the viewport centre. [`PageLayout`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/page-layout.tsx) previously kept its `mx-auto max-w-2xl` content centred on the whole _viewport_ while a `fixed` `w-72` rail sat in the gutter, so the content read as off-centre (a lopsided empty gap on the far side) and any full-bleed hero spilled under the rail. It now reserves each present rail's width (`xl:pl-72` / `xl:pr-72`) so the content centres in the region _between_ the rails ; pages with no rails stay viewport-centred. This fixes the reported `/account/profile` bug where the [`UserBanner`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/user-banner.tsx) hero extended under the account sidebar and its hover "edit" pen (centred on the full-viewport banner) landed left-of-centre of the visible area. The banner's viewport bleed was updated to match : below `xl` it still bleeds to the viewport, at `xl+` it spans the sidebar's right edge → viewport right edge (`w-[calc(100vw-18rem)]`, offset to start at 18rem), so the hero + pen align with the docked content. Applies to every `PageLayout` sidebar surface (account tabs + admin) ; the Data section already docked via `main`'s `xl:pl-78`. Verified with a `profile-banner-layout` screenshot fixture (content + banner centre on the region-right-of-sidebar axis, avatar aligned with the fields below). `tsc` + `eslint` green.

- 2026-07-05: Mobile detail panel — fixed the form still overflowing the screen ("pinch to see the whole width"). Root cause was a feedback lock in [`TableDetailLayout`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/table-detail-layout.tsx) : the mobile check used a media query, which reads the **layout viewport** (ICB) ; when the ICB blew out past 767px for even a transient on iOS, the check flipped to "desktop", switched the panel to the transform-based `slide-in-from-right` variant (`translateX(100%)`, off-screen), and that locked the layout viewport open — so the form's `@lg` container query fired and it went two-column and overflowed. Fixed by driving the mobile decision off `visualViewport.width` (the true screen width, immune to ICB blow-out) instead of a media query / `innerWidth`, and by pinning the mobile panel's `maxWidth` to that visual width so the panel and its form `@container` can never exceed the screen even if the layout viewport momentarily blows out. Verified under headless WebKit (iPhone emulation) : with the layout viewport force-blown to 1000px, the panel stays pinned at 390px and the form stays single-column. `tsc` + `eslint` + 222 tests green.

- 2026-07-05: Shared UI — extracted a standard `DragHandle` chip for resizable / draggable edges. The little pill grip was hand-rolled in three places with drifting styles ; it now lives once in [`@monark/components/ui/drag-handle`](monark/app/packages/components/src/ui/drag-handle.tsx) and is reused by the calendar event's bottom resize handle ([day-event-block.tsx](monark/app/packages/calendar/src/client/ui/day-event-block.tsx), which also dropped a duplicated inline copy and its `resizeHover` state), the detail-panel resize edge ([table-detail-layout.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/table-detail-layout.tsx), whose previously-invisible edge now shows the chip on hover / drag), and the data-table column resize edge ([data-table.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/data-table.tsx)). `DragHandle` renders the hit area, reveals its grip on hover (always on touch) unless `active`, and takes an `orientation` (grip shape + resize cursor), an optional `color` for the calendar's per-event tint, and an optional `highlight` that tints the whole strip a neutral `muted-foreground/20` on hover / drag (a revival of the panel's old hover rail, recoloured neutral, enabled on the detail-panel edge; off elsewhere). The neutral grip fill is a fully opaque `color-mix` tone rather than a translucent `muted-foreground/40`, so it holds its contrast over the tinted rail instead of washing out. The upstream `@monark/ui` drawer grabber ([ui/components/ui/drawer.tsx](monark/ui/components/ui/drawer.tsx)) was aligned to the same grip visual as plain markup (it sits outside the app's module graph). Registered the shared package with an `@source` directive in [globals.css](monark/app/services/web/src/app/globals.css) so Tailwind v4 generates classes authored there. The calendar package takes a new `@monark/components` dependency (a non-tiered UI package, so no boundary crossed).

- 2026-07-05: Calendar — a freshly created event now appears without a page refresh. The day / week / month views refetched events after a create / update / delete by calling `utils.calendar.events.listForDay.fetch(range)`, but `fetch` = `queryClient.fetchQuery`, which honours the app's 30s default `staleTime` ([trpc-provider.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/lib/trpc-provider.tsx)) and so returned the _cached_ (pre-mutation) events without hitting the server — the new event only showed after the query went stale or the page reloaded (most visible right after creating a new calendar, since that whole flow happens well inside 30s). Swapped the three post-mutation `fetch(range)` calls in [day-view.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/calendar/day-view.tsx>), [week-view.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/calendar/week-view.tsx>), and [month-view.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/calendar/month-view.tsx>) for `invalidate(range)`, which marks the query stale and force-refetches the active observer regardless of `staleTime`. `tsc` + `eslint` + 222 tests green.

- 2026-07-05: Panel header — open-full-page button moved to the right, next to close. The Maximize2 "open full page" control used to sit on the _left_ of the [`PanelHeader`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/panel-header.tsx) (via the `left={{ mode: "window", … }}` window-controls variant) ; it now renders on the right as a fixed control immediately left of the close × so the two window affordances read together. Replaced the `window` left-mode with dedicated top-level `fullPageHref` / `fullPageLabel` props (the `left` prop keeps only the `back` arrow variant) ; the overflow width budget now reserves for both right-side fixed buttons. Migrated all six detail panels (projects, industries, organizations, users, roles, webhooks) to the new props. `panel-header` screenshot story updated ; `tsc` + `eslint` + 222 tests green.

- 2026-07-05: Detail panel — resized width now persists per screen. The desktop detail-panel width was a single global preference (`detail-panel-width`), so widening the projects panel also widened the industries panel. [`TableDetailLayout`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/table-detail-layout.tsx) gained a `storageKey` prop that namespaces the persisted width (`detail-panel-width:<storageKey>`) ; the load effect keys on it (and resets when it changes) and the resize-end write targets it. Wired each list screen to its own key — `projects`, `industries`, `admin-users`, `admin-webhooks`, `admin-roles`, `admin-organizations` — so every panel remembers an independent width ; a caller that omits `storageKey` still falls back to the shared key. Updated the pattern's doc in [CLAUDE.md](monark/app/CLAUDE.md). `tsc` + `eslint` + 222 tests green.
- 2026-07-05: Data tables — cursor Prev/Next pagination with a total count, now the default for every list backed by a "GET ALL". Established the shared backend convention in [pagination.ts](monark/app/packages/common/src/pagination.ts) (new `@monark/common/pagination` dependency-free subpath) : a list method takes `PaginationArgs` (`{ limit?, cursor? }`) and returns `Paginated<T>` (`{ items, nextCursor, total }`), built with `cursorFindArgs` / `toPage` and a `count()` on the same filter ; ordering is a stable `id`-terminated keyset. Migrated the two flagged data models — `listProjects` / `listIndustries` in [data.ts](monark/app/packages/projects/src/server/data.ts) and their tRPC procedures in [index.ts](monark/app/packages/projects/src/server/index.ts) (industries gained a server-side `search`). On the frontend, added a `usePaginatedList` hook + a `DataTablePagination` footer baked into [`DataTable`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/data-table.tsx) via a new `pagination` prop (footer shows "Showing 1–25 of {total}", a page-size selector, and Prev / Next ; the hook holds a cursor stack for Prev and resets to page 1 when the filters change) ; footer strings come from [`usePaginationLabels`](monark/app/services/web/src/lib/use-pagination-labels.ts) so the pattern stays text-free. Wired [projects-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/projects/projects-list.tsx>) and [industries-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/industries/industries-list.tsx>) (the latter now fetches the panel record via `getById` since a selected row may page away), and updated the dropdown / global-search consumers to request an explicit high `limit` and read `.items`. Documented the convention in [CLAUDE.md](monark/app/CLAUDE.md) + package READMEs, added `table.pagination` i18n keys (en + fr). Full pre-PR gate green : `gen` + `tsc` + `eslint` + 222 tests + `check:tiers` + `check:i18n`.
- 2026-07-05: Data forms — dropped most field hints, kept only the critical ones. The per-field help lines under the project and industry form inputs were mostly noise (too technical or restating the obvious), so they're gone : `titleHelp`, `slugHelp`, `urlHelp`, `industriesHelp`, `descriptionHelp` on the project form ([project-form.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/projects/project-form.tsx>)) and `displayNameHelp`, `slugHelp`, `descriptionHelp` on both the industry panel form and its full-page editor ([industries-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/industries/industries-list.tsx>), [industry-edit-form.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/industries/[id]/industry-edit-form.tsx>)). Kept the genuinely useful chrome : the live "Will be saved as {slug}" preview and the keywords hint (trimmed to just the non-obvious interaction, "Press Enter to add each keyword"). Removed the now-orphaned help keys from both locale catalogs (en + fr). `tsc` + `eslint` + 222 tests + `check:i18n` all green.
- 2026-07-05: Toasts — dismissable, bottom-centered, and panel-safe. Enabled Sonner's `closeButton` on the app-wide [`Toaster`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/ui/sonner.tsx) so every toast shows a × to close it by hand instead of only timing out (swipe-to-dismiss stays too), themed to match the toast surface and pinned to the top-right corner (via the `--toast-close-button-*` vars set inline on the toaster, overriding Sonner's default top-left in LTR), and moved the toaster from `bottom-right` to `bottom-center` ([layout.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/app/layout.tsx)). Fixed the interaction where clicking a toast's × dismissed an open detail panel : the non-modal desktop `Sheet` in [`TableDetailLayout`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/table-detail-layout.tsx) treated the toast (a body-level portal) as an outside pointer-down and closed ; added `[data-sonner-toaster]` to its `onInteractOutside` keep-open allowlist alongside the existing menu / dialog / table exclusions. Single shared change ; applies to every `toast.*` call across the app. `tsc` + `eslint` green, 222 tests pass.

- 2026-07-05: Mobile — detail panels opened via a deep link no longer blow out the viewport. `TableDetailLayout` gated its mobile-vs-desktop `Sheet` variant on the shared `useIsMobile`, which is `false` on the first render (SSR-safe) and only settles in an effect. When a panel was already open on that first render — a deep link like `/data/projects?project=<id>` (industry chip links, global search, primary-column hrefs all produce these) — it briefly rendered the desktop `side="right"` variant, whose `slide-in-from-right` transform paints off-screen on a phone and permanently expands the layout viewport (the "everything's zoomed out, pinch to see the full width" bug), and it opened as a right-side sheet instead of the full-screen modal. The admin panels dodged it only because they're opened by tapping a row _after_ mount. Fixed by resolving mobile up front with a local `usePanelIsMobile` that reads `matchMedia` in its lazy initializer ([table-detail-layout.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/table-detail-layout.tsx)) — safe from hydration mismatch because the panel is a client-only portal never present in the SSR HTML, so it can't disagree with the server. Panels now render the transform-free full-screen `full` variant from the first paint ; verified headlessly that a deep-linked panel is full-width (`inset-0 w-full`, doc width == viewport) at 150ms with no hydration warning. The shared `useIsMobile` is untouched (calendar SSRs against it). `tsc` + `eslint` green.

- 2026-07-05: Detail panels — correct, create/edit-aware titles in the panel header (not the content). The list→detail panels were showing either a static generic title in the `PanelHeader` (Role / Webhook endpoint / User) or the record's own name (projects), while the editor content _also_ rendered a "Create a role" / "New webhook endpoint" / create-edit heading — a confusing double title. Now each panel header shows a single mode-aware title and the content drops its copy : the RBAC role editor and webhook editor no longer render their in-panel `<h2>` (full-page keeps its `PageHeader`), and the five list screens set `PanelHeader title` to `panel.isCreate ? panelCreateTitle : panelEditTitle` — "Create a role"/"Edit role", "New webhook endpoint"/"Edit webhook endpoint", "Create new user"/"Edit user", "Create new project"/"Edit project", "Create new industry"/"Edit industry" ([role-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/rbac/role-editor.tsx>), [webhook-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/webhooks/webhook-editor.tsx>), roles/webhooks/users/projects/industries list screens). The project + industry form bodies already gated their titles to full-page, so no content change was needed there. New `panelCreateTitle` / `panelEditTitle` keys in the five namespaces (en + fr) ; the `SheetTitle` accessible name matches. `tsc` + `eslint` + the 222-test suite green.

- 2026-07-05: Notifications — per-event preferences (was per-category). A user can now silence one specific notification (e.g. "New device sign-in") instead of a whole category, and every registered kind is surfaced — including `calendar.event.reminder`, which the old category UI never exposed. **Schema**: `NotificationPreference.category` → `kind` (unique `userId, kind, channel`), migration `20260705040000_notification_prefs_per_kind` which resets existing rows (approved) so users revert to registry defaults. **Backend**: `prefs.ts` resolves/writes per kind ; the router's `get`/`adminGet` return every kind grouped-ready by category with per-channel cells (`enabled` / `forced` (requiredEmail) / `available` (kind supports the channel)), `set`/`adminSet` validate the kind + channel are writable, and the `notification.preference-changed` event carries `kind` instead of `category` ([router.ts](monark/app/packages/notifications/src/server/router.ts), [prefs.ts](monark/app/packages/notifications/src/server/prefs.ts), [events.ts](monark/app/packages/notifications/src/contracts/events.ts)). **UI**: a shared [`NotificationPrefs`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/notification-prefs.tsx) used by both self-service ([notifications-section.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/account/notifications-section.tsx>)) and admin ([admin-notifications.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/users/[id]/admin-notifications.tsx>)), built on `GroupedMultiSelect` — which gained a **trailing-controls mode** (`renderItemTrailing`, optional selection callbacks) so the same collapsible/search/section shell now backs all three of RBAC, webhooks, and notifications. Each kind row shows a human label + mono kind id + a toggle per channel ; unsupported channels are hidden, `requiredEmail` renders locked-on. New `account.notifications.prefs.{kinds.*,categories.ACTIVITY/DIGEST,search*}` i18n in en + fr. Updated the prefs + dispatch test suites (category→kind). Verified the matrix headlessly. `tsc` (web/api/notifications/projects) + `eslint` green ; notifications 106 + web 222 tests pass ; `pnpm gen` re-run.

- 2026-07-05: Data-model forms — three-section layout (Attributes / Description / Discussion). The project and industry forms are now split into three `PageSection`s instead of one. **Attributes** holds the reorderable structured fields (for projects, still ordered by the table's configured column order via `fieldOrder`, description excluded since it was never a column) ([project-form.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/projects/project-form.tsx>), [industries-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/industries/industries-list.tsx>)). **Description** is now the main-content section backed by the shared TipTap [`RichTextEditor`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/fields/inputs/rich-text-editor.tsx) WYSIWYG (the project description moves from a plain `Textarea` to rich text ; the industry form already used it) — the editor's programmatic `setContent` doesn't emit, so loading an existing record doesn't false-trigger the dirty bar. **Discussion** is a new reusable, presentational [`DiscussionSection`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/discussion-section.tsx) : a comment composer over a flat, thread-less comment list, text-free (takes translated `labels` + `comments`), wired for now to a static two-comment preview via `useDiscussionPreview` with a disabled composer — swap `comments` for real data when the feature lands. The project form gained a full-page `<h1>` (shown only when `containment === "viewport"`, since the panel's `PanelHeader` already carries the record title). New shared `dataForm` i18n namespace (section titles + the discussion preview strings, en + fr) ; the project `descriptionHelp` copy updated from "Markdown" to rich text. Verified via a new `discussion-section` screenshot story ; `tsc` + `eslint` + 222 tests + `check:i18n` (1339 keys × en/fr) all green.
- 2026-07-04: Event popover polish — aligned toggle, capped box, avatar chips. The Standard / Punctual / Full-day segmented control is now `h-9` (matches an input) so it lines up with neighbouring fields in the multi-column layout ([new-event-popover.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/calendar/new-event-popover.tsx>)). The desktop popover no longer grows past the viewport : it's capped at `max-h-[calc(100dvh-2rem)]` as a flex column with a scrollable body + pinned footer (mirroring the mobile dialog), so a long description or a stack of reminders scrolls inside the box instead of overflowing the screen. The shared [`MultiSelect`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/multi-select.tsx) gained a per-option `leading` slot (passed through to the `Chip`'s existing `leading`), rendered in both the selected chip and the dropdown row, so removable chips can carry a small avatar / colour dot (e.g. participants). Verified via the `toggle-avatar-chips` screenshot story ; `tsc` + `eslint` green, 222 tests pass.

- 2026-07-04: Industries — WYSIWYG description. Added an optional `Industry.description` column ([schema.prisma](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/schema.prisma), migration `20260705031603_add_industry_description`) storing sanitized Tiptap HTML. Threaded it through the create / update Zod inputs + data layer + tRPC procedures ([index.ts](monark/app/packages/projects/src/server/index.ts), [data.ts](monark/app/packages/projects/src/server/data.ts)) — `undefined` leaves it unchanged, `null` clears it — and added `"description"` to the `industry.updated` event's `changed` union ([events.ts](monark/app/packages/projects/src/contracts/events.ts)). The industry detail-panel form now edits it with the shared controlled [`RichTextEditor`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/fields/inputs/rich-text-editor.tsx) (toolbar labels from `useFieldStrings`), wired into its `useState` + `DirtyFormBar` model ([industries-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/industries/industries-list.tsx>)) ; new `admin.industries.form.description*` i18n in en + fr. `tsc` (projects + web + api) + `eslint` + the 222-test suite green ; `pnpm gen` re-run (no drift). Note : `prisma migrate dev` also folded a pre-existing un-migrated `TrustedDevice.expiresAt` default into this migration — benign (DB now matches schema) but unrelated to industries.

- 2026-07-04: Calendar event popover + WYSIWYG toolbar polish. Reordered the event popover's right column to Type → Date → Start/End times → Participants → Location → Calendar → Reminders (top-down) and widened the popover (`w-165` → `w-190`) so it all fits ([new-event-popover.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/calendar/new-event-popover.tsx>)) ; each reminder row now reads "N `[unit]` before" (new `reminderBefore` i18n, en + fr) so it's clear the offset is _before_ the event.
